```js
  // src/content/hud-overlay.ts
  function countryToFlagEmoji(code) {
    if (!code || code.length !== 2) return "";
    const upper = code.toUpperCase();
    const A = 127462;
    const first = upper.charCodeAt(0) - 65;
    const second = upper.charCodeAt(1) - 65;
    if (first < 0 || first > 25 || second < 0 || second > 25) return "";
    return String.fromCodePoint(A + first, A + second);
  }
  function barColor(score) {
    if (score < 25) return "#10b981";
    if (score < 45) return "#38bdf8";
    if (score < 68) return "#f59e0b";
    if (score < 82) return "#f97316";
    return "#ef4444";
  }
  function escapeHtml(raw) {
    return raw.replace(/&/g, "&amp;").replace(/</g, "&lt;").replace(/>/g, "&gt;").replace(/"/g, "&quot;").replace(/'/g, "&#39;");
  }
  var SentinelHudOverlay = class {
    host = null;
    shadow = null;
    cardEl = null;
    minPillEl = null;
    settings;
    callbacks;
    currentResult = null;
    selectedFormat = "blitz";
    isMinimized = false;
    isHidden = true;
    detailsExpanded = true;
    isAbortWindowOpen = false;
    isWatched = false;
    loadingUsername = null;
    // Drag state
    dragging = false;
    dragStartX = 0;
    dragStartY = 0;
    startRight = 20;
    startTop = 72;
    constructor(settings, callbacks) {
      this.settings = settings;
      this.callbacks = callbacks;
      this.detailsExpanded = !settings.compactMode;
      if (settings.hudPosition) {
        this.startRight = settings.hudPosition.right;
        this.startTop = settings.hudPosition.top;
      }
    }
    mount() {
      if (this.host && document.body.contains(this.host)) return;
      this.host = document.createElement("div");
      this.host.id = "chess-sentinel-hud-root";
      this.shadow = this.host.attachShadow({ mode: "open" });
      const styleEl = document.createElement("style");
      styleEl.textContent = HUD_SHADOW_CSS;
      this.cardEl = document.createElement("div");
      this.cardEl.className = "sentinel-card hidden";
      this.minPillEl = document.createElement("div");
      this.minPillEl.className = "minimized-pill hidden";
      this.minPillEl.addEventListener("click", () => {
        this.isMinimized = false;
        this.syncVisibility();
      });
      this.shadow.append(styleEl, this.cardEl, this.minPillEl);
      document.body.appendChild(this.host);
      this.applyPosition();
      this.setupGlobalDragListeners();
    }
    unmount() {
      if (this.host) {
        this.host.remove();
        this.host = null;
        this.shadow = null;
        this.cardEl = null;
        this.minPillEl = null;
      }
    }
    updateSettings(next) {
      const prevLang = this.settings.language;
      this.settings = next;
      if (prevLang !== next.language) {
        this.render();
      }
    }
    setWatchedState(watched) {
      this.isWatched = watched;
      this.render();
    }
    setAbortWindow(open) {
      if (this.isAbortWindowOpen !== open) {
        this.isAbortWindowOpen = open;
        if (this.currentResult) this.render();
      }
    }
    showLoading(username) {
      this.mount();
      this.loadingUsername = username;
      this.currentResult = null;
      this.isHidden = false;
      this.render();
      this.syncVisibility();
    }
    showResult(result, watched, isAbortWindowOpen) {
      this.mount();
      this.loadingUsername = null;
      this.currentResult = result;
      this.selectedFormat = result.primaryFormat;
      this.isWatched = watched;
      this.isAbortWindowOpen = isAbortWindowOpen;
      this.isHidden = false;
      this.render();
      this.syncVisibility();
    }
    showError(username, message) {
      this.mount();
      this.loadingUsername = null;
      this.isHidden = false;
      if (!this.cardEl) return;
      const lang = this.settings.language;
      this.cardEl.innerHTML = `
      <div class="tier-accent-line"></div>
      ${this.renderHeaderHtml(lang)}
      <div class="loading-box">
        <div>
          <div class="loading-text">@${escapeHtml(username)}</div>
          <div class="loading-sub">${escapeHtml(message)}</div>
        </div>
      </div>
    `;
      this.bindHeaderEvents();
      this.syncVisibility();
    }
    hide() {
      this.isHidden = true;
      this.syncVisibility();
    }
    toggleVisibility() {
      this.mount();
      if (this.isHidden) {
        this.isHidden = false;
        this.isMinimized = false;
      } else if (this.isMinimized) {
        this.isMinimized = false;
      } else {
        this.isMinimized = true;
      }
      this.syncVisibility();
    }
    expandFromBadge() {
      this.mount();
      this.isHidden = false;
      this.isMinimized = false;
      this.syncVisibility();
    }
    syncVisibility() {
      if (!this.cardEl || !this.minPillEl) return;
      if (this.isHidden) {
        this.cardEl.classList.add("hidden");
        this.minPillEl.classList.add("hidden");
        return;
      }
      if (this.isMinimized) {
        this.cardEl.classList.add("hidden");
        this.minPillEl.classList.remove("hidden");
      } else {
        this.cardEl.classList.remove("hidden");
        this.minPillEl.classList.add("hidden");
      }
    }
    applyPosition() {
      const clampedRight = Math.max(8, Math.min(window.innerWidth - 180, this.startRight));
      const clampedTop = Math.max(8, Math.min(window.innerHeight - 80, this.startTop));
      if (this.cardEl) {
        this.cardEl.style.right = `${clampedRight}px`;
        this.cardEl.style.top = `${clampedTop}px`;
      }
      if (this.minPillEl) {
        this.minPillEl.style.right = `${clampedRight}px`;
        this.minPillEl.style.top = `${clampedTop}px`;
      }
    }
    setupGlobalDragListeners() {
      window.addEventListener("pointermove", (e) => {
        if (!this.dragging) return;
        const dx = e.clientX - this.dragStartX;
        const dy = e.clientY - this.dragStartY;
        this.startRight = Math.max(8, Math.min(window.innerWidth - 220, this.startRight - dx));
        this.startTop = Math.max(8, Math.min(window.innerHeight - 60, this.startTop + dy));
        this.dragStartX = e.clientX;
        this.dragStartY = e.clientY;
        this.applyPosition();
      });
      window.addEventListener("pointerup", () => {
        if (this.dragging) {
          this.dragging = false;
          this.callbacks.onSavePosition({ right: this.startRight, top: this.startTop });
        }
      });
    }
    renderHeaderHtml(lang) {
      const t = TRANSLATIONS[lang];
      const nextLang = lang === "tr" ? "EN" : "TR";
      return `
      <div class="hud-header" data-drag-handle="1">
        <div class="brand">
          <svg class="brand-shield" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.2">
            <path d="M12 2L4 5.5v6.2c0 5.3 3.4 9.9 8 11.3 4.6-1.4 8-6 8-11.3V5.5L12 2z"/>
            <path d="M9 12l2 2 4-4" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
          </svg>
          <span class="brand-title">Chess Sentinel</span>
        </div>
        <div class="header-actions">
          <button class="icon-btn lang-btn" data-action="lang" title="Switch Language / Dil De\u011Fi\u015Ftir">${nextLang}</button>
          <button class="icon-btn" data-action="swap" title="${escapeHtml(t.actions.swapTarget)}">\u21C5</button>
          ${this.currentResult ? `<button class="icon-btn ${this.isWatched ? "active" : ""}" data-action="watch" title="${escapeHtml(
        this.isWatched ? t.actions.watchingSuspect : t.actions.watchSuspect
      )}">\u{1F441}</button>` : ""}
          <button class="icon-btn" data-action="refresh" title="${escapeHtml(t.actions.refresh)}">\u21BB</button>
          <button class="icon-btn" data-action="minimize" title="${escapeHtml(t.actions.minimize)}">\u2014</button>
          <button class="icon-btn" data-action="close" title="Close">\u2715</button>
        </div>
      </div>
    `;
    }
    bindHeaderEvents() {
      if (!this.cardEl) return;
      const header = this.cardEl.querySelector('[data-drag-handle="1"]');
      if (header) {
        header.addEventListener("pointerdown", (e) => {
          const target = e.target;
          if (target.closest("button")) return;
          this.dragging = true;
          this.dragStartX = e.clientX;
          this.dragStartY = e.clientY;
        });
      }
      this.cardEl.querySelectorAll("button[data-action]").forEach((btn) => {
        btn.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
          e.stopPropagation();
          const act = btn.dataset.action;
          if (act === "lang") {
            const next = this.settings.language === "tr" ? "en" : "tr";
            this.settings = { ...this.settings, language: next };
            this.render();
            this.callbacks.onToggleLanguage(next);
          } else if (act === "swap") {
            this.callbacks.onSwapTarget();
          } else if (act === "watch" && this.currentResult) {
            this.callbacks.onToggleWatchlist(this.currentResult);
          } else if (act === "refresh") {
            this.callbacks.onRefresh();
          } else if (act === "minimize") {
            this.isMinimized = true;
            this.syncVisibility();
          } else if (act === "close") {
            this.hide();
          }
        });
      });
    }
    render() {
      if (!this.cardEl || !this.minPillEl) return;
      const lang = this.settings.language;
      const t = TRANSLATIONS[lang];
      if (this.loadingUsername && !this.currentResult) {
        this.cardEl.style.setProperty("--tier-color", "#38bdf8");
        this.cardEl.style.setProperty("--tier-border", "rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.3)");
        this.cardEl.style.setProperty("--tier-glow", "rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.25)");
        this.cardEl.innerHTML = `
        <div class="tier-accent-line"></div>
        ${this.renderHeaderHtml(lang)}
        <div class="loading-box">
          <div class="spinner"></div>
          <div>
            <div class="loading-text">@${escapeHtml(this.loadingUsername)}</div>
            <div class="loading-sub">${escapeHtml(t.scanningOpponent)}</div>
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
        this.minPillEl.innerHTML = `<span class="min-pct">\u2026</span><span class="min-user">@${escapeHtml(
          this.loadingUsername
        )}</span>`;
        this.bindHeaderEvents();
        return;
      }
      const res = this.currentResult;
      if (!res) return;
      const theme = TIER_THEMES[res.tier];
      this.cardEl.style.setProperty("--tier-color", theme.color);
      this.cardEl.style.setProperty("--tier-bg", theme.accentBg);
      this.cardEl.style.setProperty("--tier-border", theme.border);
      this.cardEl.style.setProperty("--tier-glow", theme.glow);
      this.minPillEl.style.setProperty("--tier-color", theme.color);
      this.minPillEl.style.setProperty("--tier-border", theme.border);
      this.minPillEl.style.setProperty("--tier-glow", theme.glow);
      const fmtData = res.formats[this.selectedFormat] || res.formats[res.primaryFormat];
      const displayProb = res.isBannedFairPlay ? 100 : this.selectedFormat === res.primaryFormat ? res.probability : fmtData.formatProbability;
      const radius = 35;
      const circ = 2 * Math.PI * radius;
      const offset = circ - Math.min(100, Math.max(0, displayProb)) / 100 * circ;
      let alertHtml = "";
      if (res.isBannedFairPlay) {
        alertHtml = `<div class="alert-banner banned">\u{1F6A8} ${escapeHtml(t.bannedFairPlayBanner)}</div>`;
      } else if (this.isAbortWindowOpen && displayProb >= 55) {
        alertHtml = `<div class="alert-banner abort-warn">\u26A0\uFE0F ${escapeHtml(t.abortWindowActive)} \u2014 ${escapeHtml(
          t.considerAborting
        )}</div>`;
      } else if (this.isAbortWindowOpen && displayProb < 30) {
        alertHtml = `<div class="alert-banner abort-safe">\u{1F6E1}\uFE0F ${escapeHtml(t.safeToPlay)}</div>`;
      }
      const flagEmoji = countryToFlagEmoji(res.countryCode);
      const avatarHtml = res.avatarUrl ? `<img class="avatar" src="${escapeHtml(res.avatarUrl)}" alt="" />` : `<div class="avatar">${escapeHtml(res.displayName.slice(0, 1).toUpperCase())}</div>`;
      const titleBadgeHtml = res.title ? `<span class="title-pill">${escapeHtml(res.title.toUpperCase())}</span>` : "";
      const verdictText = res.isBannedFairPlay ? t.tiers.banned.summary : displayProb >= 60 ? t.considerAborting : displayProb >= 35 ? t.cautionAdvised : t.safeToPlay;
      const ageStr = formatAgeShort(res.accountAgeDays, lang);
      const accStr = fmtData.avgAccuracy !== null ? `${fmtData.avgAccuracy}%` : "\u2014";
      const accClass = fmtData.avgAccuracy !== null && fmtData.avgAccuracy - fmtData.expectedAccuracy >= 4 ? "neg" : fmtData.avgAccuracy !== null ? "pos" : "";
      const cvVal = res.pacing.medianCV;
      const cvStr = cvVal !== null ? String(cvVal) : "\u2014";
      const cvClass = cvVal !== null && cvVal < 0.48 ? "neg" : cvVal !== null && cvVal < 0.62 ? "warn" : "pos";
      const d7 = fmtData.eloDelta7d;
      const d7Str = d7 === null ? "0" : d7 > 0 ? `+${d7}` : `${d7}`;
      const d7Class = d7 !== null && d7 >= 90 ? "warn" : d7 !== null && d7 > 0 ? "pos" : "";
      const formDotsHtml = res.recentForm.map((r) => {
        const lbl = lang === "tr" ? r === "W" ? "G" : r === "L" ? "M" : "B" : r;
        return `<span class="form-dot ${r}">${lbl}</span>`;
      }).join("");
      const wldLabel = fmtData.recentTotal > 0 ? lang === "tr" ? `Son: ${fmtData.recentWins}G ${fmtData.recentLosses}M ${fmtData.recentDraws}B (%${fmtData.recentWinPct})` : `Recent: ${fmtData.recentWins}W ${fmtData.recentLosses}L ${fmtData.recentDraws}D (${fmtData.recentWinPct}%)` : lang === "tr" ? `Genel: %${fmtData.overallWinPct} (${fmtData.overallTotal})` : `All: ${fmtData.overallWinPct}% (${fmtData.overallTotal})`;
      const flagsHtml = res.flags.map(
        (f) => `
        <div class="flag-chip ${f.severity}">
          <span>${f.icon}</span>
          <span>${escapeHtml(formatFlag(lang, f.id, f.params))}</span>
        </div>
      `
      ).join("");
      const signalRows = [
        { label: t.signals.recentWinrate, score: res.signals.recentWinrate },
        { label: t.signals.overallWinrate, score: res.signals.overallWinrate },
        { label: t.signals.accuracyAnomaly, score: res.signals.accuracyAnomaly },
        { label: t.signals.movePacing, score: res.signals.movePacing },
        { label: t.signals.eloVelocity, score: res.signals.eloVelocity },
        { label: t.signals.accountTrust, score: res.signals.accountTrust }
      ];
      const signalsHtml = signalRows.map(
        (s) => `
        <div class="sig-row">
          <div class="sig-head">
            <span>${escapeHtml(s.label)}</span>
            <span class="sig-score">${s.score}/100</span>
          </div>
          <div class="sig-bar-bg">
            <div class="sig-bar-fill" style="width:${s.score}%;background:${barColor(s.score)}"></div>
          </div>
        </div>
      `
      ).join("");
      const formatTabsHtml = LIVE_FORMATS.map((fmt) => {
        const f = res.formats[fmt];
        const active = fmt === this.selectedFormat ? "active" : "";
        const elo = f.currentRating ? String(f.currentRating) : "\u2014";
        return `
        <button class="fmt-tab ${active}" data-fmt="${fmt}">
          <span>${fmt}</span>
          <span class="fmt-elo">${elo}</span>
        </button>
      `;
      }).join("");
      const pctDisplay = lang === "tr" ? `<span>%</span>${displayProb}` : `${displayProb}<span>%</span>`;
      this.cardEl.innerHTML = `
      <div class="tier-accent-line"></div>
      ${this.renderHeaderHtml(lang)}
      ${alertHtml}
      <div class="hero">
        <div class="gauge-wrap">
          <svg class="gauge-svg" viewBox="0 0 86 86">
            <circle class="gauge-track" cx="43" cy="43" r="${radius}"></circle>
            <circle
              class="gauge-progress"
              cx="43"
              cy="43"
              r="${radius}"
              stroke-dasharray="${circ.toFixed(2)}"
              stroke-dashoffset="${offset.toFixed(2)}"
            ></circle>
          </svg>
          <div class="gauge-center">
            <div class="gauge-pct">${pctDisplay}</div>
            <div class="gauge-caption">${escapeHtml(t.cheatProbability)}</div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="identity">
          <div class="player-row">
            ${avatarHtml}
            ${titleBadgeHtml}
            <a class="username-link" href="https://www.chess.com/member/${encodeURIComponent(
        res.displayName
      )}" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">
              ${escapeHtml(res.displayName)}
            </a>
            ${flagEmoji ? `<span>${flagEmoji}</span>` : ""}
          </div>
          <div class="tier-badge-row">
            <span class="tier-pill">${escapeHtml(t.tiers[res.tier].label)}</span>
            <span class="conf-tag">\u2022 ${escapeHtml(t.confidenceLabel[res.confidence])}</span>
          </div>
          <div class="verdict-sub">${escapeHtml(verdictText)}</div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="format-tabs">${formatTabsHtml}</div>

      <div class="stat-grid">
        <div class="stat-tile">
          <div class="stat-val">${escapeHtml(ageStr)}</div>
          <div class="stat-lbl">${escapeHtml(t.stats.accountAge)}</div>
        </div>
        <div class="stat-tile">
          <div class="stat-val ${accClass}">${escapeHtml(accStr)}</div>
          <div class="stat-lbl">${escapeHtml(t.stats.avgAccuracy)}</div>
        </div>
        <div class="stat-tile">
          <div class="stat-val ${cvClass}">${escapeHtml(cvStr)}</div>
          <div class="stat-lbl">${escapeHtml(t.stats.moveCV)}</div>
        </div>
        <div class="stat-tile">
          <div class="stat-val ${d7Class}">${escapeHtml(d7Str)}</div>
          <div class="stat-lbl">${escapeHtml(t.stats.elo7d)}</div>
        </div>
      </div>

      ${res.recentForm.length > 0 ? `<div class="form-strip">
              <div class="form-dots">${formDotsHtml}</div>
              <div class="wld-summary">${escapeHtml(wldLabel)}</div>
            </div>` : ""}

      ${flagsHtml ? `<div class="flags-list">${flagsHtml}</div>` : ""}

      <button class="details-toggle" data-toggle-details="1">
        <span>${escapeHtml(this.detailsExpanded ? t.actions.hideDetails : t.actions.toggleDetails)}</span>
        <span>${this.detailsExpanded ? "\u25B4" : "\u25BE"}</span>
      </button>

      <div class="signals-panel ${this.detailsExpanded ? "" : "collapsed"}">
        ${signalsHtml}
      </div>
    `;
      const minPctText = lang === "tr" ? `%${displayProb}` : `${displayProb}%`;
      this.minPillEl.innerHTML = `
      <span>\u{1F6E1}\uFE0F</span>
      <span class="min-pct">${minPctText}</span>
      <span class="min-user">@${escapeHtml(res.displayName)}</span>
    `;
      this.bindHeaderEvents();
      this.cardEl.querySelectorAll("button[data-fmt]").forEach((btn) => {
        btn.addEventListener("click", () => {
          const fmt = btn.dataset.fmt;
          if (fmt) {
            this.selectedFormat = fmt;
            this.render();
          }
        });
      });
      const toggleBtn = this.cardEl.querySelector('[data-toggle-details="1"]');
      if (toggleBtn) {
        toggleBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
          this.detailsExpanded = !this.detailsExpanded;
          this.render();
        });
      }
    }
  };

  // src/content/inline-badge.ts
  var BADGE_CLASS = "chess-sentinel-inline-pill";
  var STYLE_ID = "chess-sentinel-inline-style";
  function ensureInlineStyles() {
    if (document.getElementById(STYLE_ID)) return;
    const style = document.createElement("style");
    style.id = STYLE_ID;
    style.textContent = `
    .${BADGE_CLASS} {
      display: inline-flex;
      align-items: center;
      gap: 5px;
      margin-left: 7px;
      padding: 2px 8px;
      border-radius: 999px;
      font-family: 'Inter', -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, 'Segoe UI', Roboto, sans-serif;
      font-size: 11.5px;
      font-weight: 700;
      line-height: 1.35;
      letter-spacing: 0.01em;
      cursor: pointer;
      user-select: none;
      vertical-align: middle;
      transition: transform 0.16s ease, filter 0.16s ease, box-shadow 0.16s ease;
      backdrop-filter: blur(8px);
      white-space: nowrap;
      z-index: 20;
    }
    .${BADGE_CLASS}:hover {
      transform: translateY(-1px) scale(1.03);
      filter: brightness(1.12);
    }
    .${BADGE_CLASS} .cs-pill-dot {
      width: 6px;
      height: 6px;
      border-radius: 50%;
      flex-shrink: 0;
    }
    .${BADGE_CLASS} .cs-pill-wld {
      opacity: 0.85;
      font-weight: 600;
      font-size: 10.5px;
      padding-left: 4px;
      border-left: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.16);
    }
  `;
    document.head.appendChild(style);
  }
  function findPlayerAnchorContainer(targetSide) {
    const selectors = targetSide === "top" ? [
      "#board-layout-player-top .user-tagline-component",
      "#board-layout-top .user-tagline-component",
      ".board-layout-top .user-tagline-component",
      ".player-component.player-top .user-tagline-component",
      ".player-top .user-tagline-component",
      "#board-layout-player-top .cc-user-block",
      ".player-top .cc-user-block",
      "#board-layout-player-top .user-tagline-compact-row",
      ".board-layout-top cc-user-username-component",
      "#board-layout-player-top cc-user-username-component",
      ".player-top cc-user-username-component"
    ] : [
      "#board-layout-player-bottom .user-tagline-component",
      "#board-layout-bottom .user-tagline-component",
      ".board-layout-bottom .user-tagline-component",
      ".player-component.player-bottom .user-tagline-component",
      ".player-bottom .user-tagline-component",
      "#board-layout-player-bottom .cc-user-block",
      ".player-bottom .cc-user-block",
      "#board-layout-player-bottom .user-tagline-compact-row",
      ".board-layout-bottom cc-user-username-component",
      "#board-layout-player-bottom cc-user-username-component",
      ".player-bottom cc-user-username-component"
    ];
    for (const sel of selectors) {
      const el = document.querySelector(sel);
      if (el) {
        if (el.tagName.toLowerCase() === "cc-user-username-component" || el.classList.contains("cc-user-username-component") || el.classList.contains("user-tagline-username")) {
          return el.parentElement || el;
        }
        return el;
      }
    }
    return null;
  }
  function renderInlineUsernameBadge(result, targetSide, lang, onClick) {
    ensureInlineStyles();
    removeInlineUsernameBadges();
    const container = findPlayerAnchorContainer(targetSide);
    if (!container) return;
    const theme = TIER_THEMES[result.tier];
    const pf = result.formats[result.primaryFormat];
    const probText = lang === "tr" ? `%${result.probability}` : `${result.probability}%`;
    const wldText = pf.recentTotal > 0 ? lang === "tr" ? `${pf.recentWins}G/${pf.recentLosses}M` : `${pf.recentWins}W/${pf.recentLosses}L` : lang === "tr" ? `${pf.overallWins}G/${pf.overallLosses}M` : `${pf.overallWins}W/${pf.overallLosses}L`;
    const pill = document.createElement("span");
    pill.className = BADGE_CLASS;
    pill.style.background = theme.accentBg;
    pill.style.border = `1px solid ${theme.border}`;
    pill.style.color = theme.color;
    pill.style.boxShadow = `0 2px 10px ${theme.glow}`;
    pill.title = lang === "tr" ? `Chess Sentinel \u2014 Hile Olas\u0131l\u0131\u011F\u0131: %${result.probability} (Detaylar i\xE7in t\u0131kla)` : `Chess Sentinel \u2014 Cheat Probability: ${result.probability}% (Click for HUD)`;
    const dot = document.createElement("span");
    dot.className = "cs-pill-dot";
    dot.style.background = theme.color;
    dot.style.boxShadow = `0 0 6px ${theme.color}`;
    const probSpan = document.createElement("span");
    probSpan.textContent = probText;
    const wldSpan = document.createElement("span");
    wldSpan.className = "cs-pill-wld";
    wldSpan.style.color = "#e2e8f0";
    wldSpan.textContent = wldText;
    pill.append(dot, probSpan, wldSpan);
    pill.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      e.stopPropagation();
      onClick();
    });
    container.appendChild(pill);
  }
  function removeInlineUsernameBadges() {
    document.querySelectorAll(`.${BADGE_CLASS}`).forEach((el) => el.remove());
  }

  // src/content/index.ts
  var SentinelContentController = class {
    settings = { ...DEFAULT_SETTINGS };
    hud = null;
    detector = null;
    lastCtx = null;
    targetSide = "top";
    activeUsername = null;
    latestResult = null;
    async init() {
      this.settings = await getSettings();
      this.hud = new SentinelHudOverlay(this.settings, {
        onSwapTarget: () => this.swapTargetSide(),
        onRefresh: () => {
          if (this.activeUsername) {
            void this.analyzeTarget(this.activeUsername, this.lastCtx?.detectedFormat, true);
          } else {
            this.detector?.forceRecheck();
          }
        },
        onToggleLanguage: (nextLang) => {
          void this.persistSettings({ language: nextLang });
        },
        onToggleWatchlist: (result) => {
          void this.handleWatchlistToggle(result);
        },
        onSavePosition: (pos) => {
          void this.persistSettings({ hudPosition: pos });
        }
      });
      this.detector = new ChessComOpponentDetector(
        (ctx) => this.handleMatchContext(ctx),
        () => this.handleMatchLeft()
      );
      this.detector.start();
      this.setupKeyboardShortcut();
      this.setupStorageSync();
      this.setupRuntimeMessages();
    }
    async persistSettings(patch) {
      this.settings = await updateSettings(patch);
      this.hud?.updateSettings(this.settings);
      if (this.latestResult && this.settings.showInlineBadge) {
        renderInlineUsernameBadge(
          this.latestResult,
          this.targetSide,
          this.settings.language,
          () => this.hud?.expandFromBadge()
        );
      }
    }
    swapTargetSide() {
      if (!this.lastCtx) return;
      const nextSide = this.targetSide === "top" ? "bottom" : "top";
      const candidate = nextSide === "top" ? this.lastCtx.topPlayer : this.lastCtx.bottomPlayer;
      if (!candidate) return;
      this.targetSide = nextSide;
      void this.analyzeTarget(candidate, this.lastCtx.detectedFormat, false);
    }
    handleMatchContext(ctx) {
      const prevOpponent = this.lastCtx?.opponent?.toLowerCase();
      this.lastCtx = ctx;
      if (ctx.opponent.toLowerCase() !== prevOpponent) {
        this.targetSide = ctx.opponentSide;
      }
      const target = this.targetSide === "bottom" ? ctx.bottomPlayer || ctx.opponent : ctx.topPlayer || ctx.opponent;
      this.hud?.setAbortWindow(ctx.isAbortWindowOpen);
      if (target.toLowerCase() !== this.activeUsername?.toLowerCase()) {
        void this.analyzeTarget(target, ctx.detectedFormat, false);
      }
    }
    handleMatchLeft() {
      this.activeUsername = null;
      this.latestResult = null;
      this.lastCtx = null;
      removeInlineUsernameBadges();
      this.hud?.hide();
    }
    async analyzeTarget(username, preferredFormat, forceRefresh) {
      this.activeUsername = username;
      if (this.settings.autoShowHud || forceRefresh) {
        this.hud?.showLoading(username);
      }
      try {
        const msg = {
          type: "ANALYZE_PLAYER",
          username,
          preferredFormat,
          forceRefresh
        };
        const response = await chrome.runtime.sendMessage(msg);
        if (this.activeUsername?.toLowerCase() !== username.toLowerCase()) return;
        if (!response || !response.ok || !response.result) {
          this.hud?.showError(username, response?.error || "Could not retrieve Chess.com telemetry");
          return;
        }
        const result = response.result;
        this.latestResult = result;
        const watchlist = await getWatchlist();
        const isWatched = watchlist.some(
          (w) => w.username.toLowerCase() === result.username.toLowerCase()
        );
        if (this.settings.autoShowHud || forceRefresh) {
          this.hud?.showResult(result, isWatched, this.lastCtx?.isAbortWindowOpen ?? false);
        }
        if (this.settings.showInlineBadge) {
          renderInlineUsernameBadge(result, this.targetSide, this.settings.language, () => {
            this.hud?.showResult(result, isWatched, this.lastCtx?.isAbortWindowOpen ?? false);
            this.hud?.expandFromBadge();
          });
        } else {
          removeInlineUsernameBadges();
        }
      } catch {
      }
    }
    async handleWatchlistToggle(result) {
      try {
        const pf = result.formats[result.primaryFormat];
        const msg = {
          type: "TOGGLE_WATCHLIST",
          entry: {
            username: result.username,
            initialProbability: result.probability,
            ratingAtSave: pf.currentRating,
            format: result.primaryFormat,
            avatarUrl: result.avatarUrl,
            isBannedFairPlay: result.isBannedFairPlay,
            accountStatus: result.accountStatus
          }
        };
        const resp = await chrome.runtime.sendMessage(msg);
        if (resp && resp.ok) {
          this.hud?.setWatchedState(Boolean(resp.added));
        }
      } catch {
      }
    }
    setupKeyboardShortcut() {
      window.addEventListener("keydown", (e) => {
        if (e.altKey && (e.key === "s" || e.key === "S")) {
          e.preventDefault();
          if (!this.latestResult && this.lastCtx?.opponent) {
            void this.analyzeTarget(this.lastCtx.opponent, this.lastCtx.detectedFormat, true);
          } else {
            this.hud?.toggleVisibility();
          }
        }
      });
    }
    setupStorageSync() {
      if (typeof chrome === "undefined" || !chrome.storage?.onChanged) return;
      chrome.storage.onChanged.addListener(() => {
        void (async () => {
          this.settings = await getSettings();
          this.hud?.updateSettings(this.settings);
          if (!this.settings.showInlineBadge) {
            removeInlineUsernameBadges();
          } else if (this.latestResult) {
            renderInlineUsernameBadge(
              this.latestResult,
              this.targetSide,
              this.settings.language,
              () => this.hud?.expandFromBadge()
            );
          }
        })();
      });
    }
    setupRuntimeMessages() {
      if (typeof chrome === "undefined" || !chrome.runtime?.onMessage) return;
      chrome.runtime.onMessage.addListener((msg, _sender, sendResponse) => {
        if (msg.type === "TOGGLE_HUD_FROM_POPUP") {
          if (!this.latestResult && this.lastCtx?.opponent) {
            void this.analyzeTarget(this.lastCtx.opponent, this.lastCtx.detectedFormat, true);
          } else {
            this.hud?.toggleVisibility();
          }
          sendResponse({ ok: true });
        }
        return false;
      });
    }
  };
  var controller = new SentinelContentController();
  if (document.readyState === "loading") {
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => void controller.init());
  } else {
    void controller.init();
  }
})();

```

```js
function formatFlag(lang, flagId, params) {
  const template = TRANSLATIONS[lang].flags[flagId] || TRANSLATIONS.en.flags[flagId] || flagId;
  if (!params) return template;
  return template.replace(
    /\{(\w+)\}/g,
    (_, key) => params[key] !== void 0 ? String(params[key]) : `{${key}}`
  );
}
function formatAgeShort(days, lang) {
  if (days === null || days < 0) return "\u2014";
  const s = TRANSLATIONS[lang].stats;
  if (days < 60) return `${Math.max(1, days)} ${s.daysShort}`;
  if (days < 365) return `${Math.round(days / 30)} ${s.monthsShort}`;
  const yrs = (days / 365).toFixed(days >= 1095 ? 0 : 1);
  return `${yrs} ${s.yearsShort}`;
}

// src/popup/index.ts
function escapeHtml(raw) {
  return raw.replace(/&/g, "&amp;").replace(/</g, "&lt;").replace(/>/g, "&gt;").replace(/"/g, "&quot;").replace(/'/g, "&#39;");
}
function barColor(score) {
  if (score < 25) return "#10b981";
  if (score < 45) return "#38bdf8";
  if (score < 68) return "#f59e0b";
  if (score < 82) return "#f97316";
  return "#ef4444";
}
var PopupController = class {
  root;
  tab = "scanner";
  settings = { ...DEFAULT_SETTINGS };
  recentScans = [];
  watchlist = [];
  activeResult = null;
  scanning = false;
  checkingBans = false;
  errorMsg = null;
  searchQuery = "";
  constructor(root) {
    this.root = root;
  }
  async init() {
    await this.loadAll();
    if (this.recentScans.length > 0) {
      this.activeResult = this.recentScans[0];
    }
    this.render();
  }
  async send(msg) {
    return await chrome.runtime.sendMessage(msg);
  }
  async loadAll() {
    try {
      const [sResp, rResp, wResp] = await Promise.all([
        this.send({ type: "GET_SETTINGS" }),
        this.send({ type: "GET_RECENT_SCANS" }),
        this.send({ type: "GET_WATCHLIST" })
      ]);
      if (sResp?.ok) this.settings = sResp.settings;
      if (rResp?.ok) this.recentScans = rResp.scans;
      if (wResp?.ok) this.watchlist = wResp.list;
    } catch {
    }
  }
  async runPlayerScan(username) {
    const clean = username.trim();
    if (!clean) return;
    this.scanning = true;
    this.errorMsg = null;
    this.render();
    try {
      const resp = await this.send({
        type: "ANALYZE_PLAYER",
        username: clean,
        forceRefresh: true
      });
      if (!resp.ok || !resp.result) {
        this.errorMsg = resp.error === "PLAYER_NOT_FOUND" ? this.settings.language === "tr" ? "Oyuncu bulunamad\u0131." : "Player not found." : resp.error || "API Error";
      } else {
        this.activeResult = resp.result;
        const scansResp = await this.send({
          type: "GET_RECENT_SCANS"
        });
        if (scansResp?.ok) this.recentScans = scansResp.scans;
      }
    } catch (e) {
      this.errorMsg = e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Scan failed";
    } finally {
      this.scanning = false;
      this.render();
    }
  }
  renderActiveResultCard(lang) {
    if (!this.activeResult) return "";
    const r = this.activeResult;
    const t = TRANSLATIONS[lang];
    const theme = TIER_THEMES[r.tier];
    const pf = r.formats[r.primaryFormat];
    const pctText = lang === "tr" ? `%${r.probability}` : `${r.probability}%`;
    const bars = [
      { lbl: t.signals.recentWinrate, val: r.signals.recentWinrate },
      { lbl: t.signals.overallWinrate, val: r.signals.overallWinrate },
      { lbl: t.signals.accuracyAnomaly, val: r.signals.accuracyAnomaly },
      { lbl: t.signals.movePacing, val: r.signals.movePacing },
      { lbl: t.signals.eloVelocity, val: r.signals.eloVelocity },
      { lbl: t.signals.accountTrust, val: r.signals.accountTrust }
    ];
    const flagsHtml = r.flags.slice(0, 3).map(
      (f) => `<div style="font-size:10.5px;color:#cbd5e1;line-height:1.3">${f.icon} ${escapeHtml(
        formatFlag(lang, f.id, f.params)
      )}</div>`
    ).join("");
    return `
      <div class="scan-result-card" style="border-color:${theme.border}">
        <div class="scan-top">
          <div class="scan-user">
            ${r.avatarUrl ? `<img class="scan-avatar" src="${escapeHtml(r.avatarUrl)}" alt="" />` : `<div class="scan-avatar">${escapeHtml(r.displayName[0]?.toUpperCase() || "?")}</div>`}
            <div>
              <div>
                ${r.title ? `<span style="background:#dc2626;color:#fff;font-size:9px;font-weight:800;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:4px;margin-right:4px">${escapeHtml(r.title)}</span>` : ""}
                <a class="scan-name" href="https://www.chess.com/member/${encodeURIComponent(
      r.displayName
    )}" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">@${escapeHtml(r.displayName)}</a>
              </div>
              <div class="scan-meta">
                ${escapeHtml(r.primaryFormat.toUpperCase())} ${pf.currentRating ?? "\u2014"} \u2022
                ${escapeHtml(formatAgeShort(r.accountAgeDays, lang))} \u2022
                ${escapeHtml(t.tiers[r.tier].label)}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="prob-badge-lg" style="background:${theme.accentBg};color:${theme.color};border:1px solid ${theme.border}">
            ${pctText}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div class="mini-bars">
          ${bars.map(
      (b) => `
            <div class="mini-bar-item">
              <div class="mini-bar-lbl">
                <span>${escapeHtml(b.lbl)}</span>
                <strong>${b.val}</strong>
              </div>
              <div class="mini-bar-track">
                <div class="mini-bar-fill" style="width:${b.val}%;background:${barColor(b.val)}"></div>
              </div>
            </div>
          `
    ).join("")}
        </div>

        ${flagsHtml ? `<div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:4px;padding-top:4px;border-top:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.06)">${flagsHtml}</div>` : ""}
      </div>
    `;
  }
  renderScannerTab(lang) {
    const t = TRANSLATIONS[lang];
    const recentHtml = this.recentScans.length === 0 ? `<div class="empty-state">${escapeHtml(t.popup.emptyScans)}</div>` : `<div class="item-list">
            ${this.recentScans.slice(0, 8).map((item) => {
      const th = TIER_THEMES[item.tier];
      const pStr = lang === "tr" ? `%${item.probability}` : `${item.probability}%`;
      return `
                  <div class="list-row" data-select-scan="${escapeHtml(item.username)}">
                    <div class="row-left">
                      <div>
                        <div class="row-name">@${escapeHtml(item.displayName)}</div>
                        <div class="row-sub">${escapeHtml(item.primaryFormat.toUpperCase())} \u2022 ${escapeHtml(
        t.tiers[item.tier].label
      )}</div>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <span class="row-prob" style="background:${th.accentBg};color:${th.color};border:1px solid ${th.border}">${pStr}</span>
                  </div>
                `;
    }).join("")}
          </div>`;
    return `
      <form class="search-bar" id="scan-form">
        <input
          class="search-input"
          id="scan-input"
          type="text"
          value="${escapeHtml(this.searchQuery)}"
          placeholder="${escapeHtml(t.popup.searchPlaceholder)}"
          autocomplete="off"
        />
        <button class="primary-btn" type="submit" ${this.scanning ? "disabled" : ""}>
          ${this.scanning ? "\u2026" : escapeHtml(t.popup.scanBtn)}
        </button>
      </form>
      ${this.errorMsg ? `<div style="color:#f87171;font-size:11.5px;font-weight:600">${escapeHtml(this.errorMsg)}</div>` : ""}
      ${this.renderActiveResultCard(lang)}
      <div class="sec-title">${escapeHtml(t.popup.recentScansTitle)}</div>
      ${recentHtml}
    `;
  }
  renderWatchlistTab(lang) {
    const t = TRANSLATIONS[lang];
    const bannedCount = this.watchlist.filter((w) => w.isBannedFairPlay).length;
    const listHtml = this.watchlist.length === 0 ? `<div class="empty-state">${escapeHtml(t.popup.emptyWatchlist)}</div>` : `<div class="item-list">
            ${this.watchlist.map((w) => {
      const initText = t.popup.addedOn.replace("{prob}", String(w.initialProbability));
      return `
                  <div class="list-row">
                    <div class="row-left">
                      <div>
                        <a class="row-name" style="text-decoration:none" href="https://www.chess.com/member/${encodeURIComponent(
        w.username
      )}" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">@${escapeHtml(w.username)}</a>
                        <div class="row-sub">${escapeHtml(initText)} \u2022 ${w.ratingAtSave ?? "\u2014"} Elo</div>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:6px">
                      ${w.isBannedFairPlay ? `<span class="banned-pill">\u{1F6A8} ${escapeHtml(t.popup.bannedTag)}</span>` : `<span class="active-pill">${escapeHtml(t.popup.activeTag)}</span>`}
                      <button class="pill-btn" data-remove-watch="${escapeHtml(w.username)}" title="${escapeHtml(
        t.popup.removeBtn
      )}">\u2715</button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                `;
    }).join("")}
          </div>`;
    return `
      <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between">
        <div class="sec-title">${escapeHtml(t.popup.watchlistTitle)} (${bannedCount}/${this.watchlist.length})</div>
        <button class="primary-btn" id="refresh-bans-btn" style="padding:6px 10px" ${this.checkingBans || this.watchlist.length === 0 ? "disabled" : ""}>
          ${escapeHtml(this.checkingBans ? t.popup.checkingBans : t.popup.checkBansBtn)}
        </button>
      </div>
      ${listHtml}
    `;
  }
  renderSettingsTab(lang) {
    const t = TRANSLATIONS[lang];
    const s = this.settings;
    return `
      <div class="setting-item">
        <div>
          <div class="setting-title">${escapeHtml(t.popup.settingsLanguage)}</div>
        </div>
        <select class="select-input" data-setting-select="language">
          <option value="tr" ${s.language === "tr" ? "selected" : ""}>T\xFCrk\xE7e (TR)</option>
          <option value="en" ${s.language === "en" ? "selected" : ""}>English (EN)</option>
        </select>
      </div>

      <div class="setting-item">
        <div>
          <div class="setting-title">${escapeHtml(t.popup.settingsSensitivity)}</div>
        </div>
        <select class="select-input" data-setting-select="sensitivity">
          <option value="conservative" ${s.sensitivity === "conservative" ? "selected" : ""}>${escapeHtml(
      t.popup.sensitivityConservative
    )}</option>
          <option value="balanced" ${s.sensitivity === "balanced" ? "selected" : ""}>${escapeHtml(
      t.popup.sensitivityBalanced
    )}</option>
          <option value="strict" ${s.sensitivity === "strict" ? "selected" : ""}>${escapeHtml(
      t.popup.sensitivityStrict
    )}</option>
        </select>
      </div>

      <div class="setting-item">
        <div>
          <div class="setting-title">${escapeHtml(t.popup.settingsAutoHud)}</div>
          <div class="setting-desc">${escapeHtml(t.popup.settingsAutoHudDesc)}</div>
        </div>
        <input class="toggle-input" type="checkbox" data-setting-check="autoShowHud" ${s.autoShowHud ? "checked" : ""} />
      </div>

      <div class="setting-item">
        <div>
          <div class="setting-title">${escapeHtml(t.popup.settingsInlineBadge)}</div>
          <div class="setting-desc">${escapeHtml(t.popup.settingsInlineBadgeDesc)}</div>
        </div>
        <input class="toggle-input" type="checkbox" data-setting-check="showInlineBadge" ${s.showInlineBadge ? "checked" : ""} />
      </div>

      <div class="setting-item">
        <div>
          <div class="setting-title">${escapeHtml(t.popup.settingsCompact)}</div>
          <div class="setting-desc">${escapeHtml(t.popup.settingsCompactDesc)}</div>
        </div>
        <input class="toggle-input" type="checkbox" data-setting-check="compactMode" ${s.compactMode ? "checked" : ""} />
      </div>

      <div class="setting-item">
        <div>
          <div class="setting-title">${escapeHtml(t.popup.settingsRatedOnly)}</div>
          <div class="setting-desc">${escapeHtml(t.popup.settingsRatedOnlyDesc)}</div>
        </div>
        <input class="toggle-input" type="checkbox" data-setting-check="ratedOnly" ${s.ratedOnly ? "checked" : ""} />
      </div>
    `;
  }
  render() {
    const lang = this.settings.language;
    const t = TRANSLATIONS[lang];
    const bodyHtml = this.tab === "scanner" ? this.renderScannerTab(lang) : this.tab === "watchlist" ? this.renderWatchlistTab(lang) : this.renderSettingsTab(lang);
    this.root.innerHTML = `
      <div class="popup-shell">
        <div class="top-bar">
          <div class="brand-group">
            <div class="brand-icon">
              <svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.4">
                <path d="M12 2L4 5.5v6.2c0 5.3 3.4 9.9 8 11.3 4.6-1.4 8-6 8-11.3V5.5L12 2z"/>
                <path d="M9 12l2 2 4-4" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
              </svg>
            </div>
            <div>
              <div class="brand-name">${escapeHtml(t.appName)}</div>
              <div class="brand-sub">${escapeHtml(t.appSubtitle)}</div>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="top-controls">
            <button class="pill-btn" id="quick-lang-btn">${lang === "tr" ? "EN" : "TR"}</button>
            <button class="pill-btn" id="toggle-hud-btn" title="${escapeHtml(t.popup.toggleInPageHud)}">HUD</button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div class="nav-tabs">
          <button class="nav-tab ${this.tab === "scanner" ? "active" : ""}" data-tab="scanner">
            ${escapeHtml(t.popup.tabScanner)}
          </button>
          <button class="nav-tab ${this.tab === "watchlist" ? "active" : ""}" data-tab="watchlist">
            ${escapeHtml(t.popup.tabWatchlist)} (${this.watchlist.length})
          </button>
          <button class="nav-tab ${this.tab === "settings" ? "active" : ""}" data-tab="settings">
            ${escapeHtml(t.popup.tabSettings)}
          </button>
        </div>

        <div class="tab-body">${bodyHtml}</div>

        <div class="footer-note">${escapeHtml(t.popup.privacyNote)}</div>
      </div>
    `;
    this.bindEvents();
  }
  bindEvents() {
    this.root.querySelectorAll("button[data-tab]").forEach((btn) => {
      btn.addEventListener("click", () => {
        this.tab = btn.dataset.tab;
        this.render();
      });
    });
    const quickLang = this.root.querySelector("#quick-lang-btn");
    quickLang?.addEventListener("click", () => {
      void (async () => {
        const next = this.settings.language === "tr" ? "en" : "tr";
        const resp = await this.send({
          type: "UPDATE_SETTINGS",
          patch: { language: next }
        });
        if (resp?.ok) this.settings = resp.settings;
        this.render();
      })();
    });
    const toggleHudBtn = this.root.querySelector("#toggle-hud-btn");
    toggleHudBtn?.addEventListener("click", () => {
      void (async () => {
        try {
          const [activeTab] = await chrome.tabs.query({ active: true, currentWindow: true });
          if (activeTab?.id) {
            await chrome.tabs.sendMessage(activeTab.id, { type: "TOGGLE_HUD_FROM_POPUP" });
          }
        } catch {
        }
      })();
    });
    const form = this.root.querySelector("#scan-form");
    const input = this.root.querySelector("#scan-input");
    if (input) {
      input.addEventListener("input", () => {
        this.searchQuery = input.value;
      });
    }
    if (form && input) {
      form.addEventListener("submit", (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        void this.runPlayerScan(input.value);
      });
    }
    this.root.querySelectorAll("[data-select-scan]").forEach((row) => {
      row.addEventListener("click", () => {
        const u = row.dataset.selectScan;
        const found = this.recentScans.find((s) => s.username.toLowerCase() === u?.toLowerCase());
        if (found) {
          this.activeResult = found;
          this.render();
        }
      });
    });
    const refreshBansBtn = this.root.querySelector("#refresh-bans-btn");
    refreshBansBtn?.addEventListener("click", () => {
      void (async () => {
        this.checkingBans = true;
        this.render();
        const resp = await this.send({
          type: "REFRESH_WATCHLIST_BANS"
        });
        if (resp?.ok) this.watchlist = resp.list;
        this.checkingBans = false;
        this.render();
      })();
    });
    this.root.querySelectorAll("button[data-remove-watch]").forEach((btn) => {
      btn.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
        e.stopPropagation();
        const u = btn.dataset.removeWatch;
        if (!u) return;
        void (async () => {
          const resp = await this.send({
            type: "REMOVE_WATCHLIST",
            username: u
          });
          if (resp?.ok) this.watchlist = resp.list;
          this.render();
        })();
      });
    });
    this.root.querySelectorAll("input[data-setting-check]").forEach((el) => {
      el.addEventListener("change", () => {
        const inputEl = el;
        const key = inputEl.dataset.settingCheck;
        void (async () => {
          const resp = await this.send({
            type: "UPDATE_SETTINGS",
            patch: { [key]: inputEl.checked }
          });
          if (resp?.ok) this.settings = resp.settings;
        })();
      });
    });
    this.root.querySelectorAll("select[data-setting-select]").forEach((el) => {
      el.addEventListener("change", () => {
        const selEl = el;
        const key = selEl.dataset.settingSelect;
        void (async () => {
          const patch = key === "language" ? { language: selEl.value } : { sensitivity: selEl.value };
          const resp = await this.send({
            type: "UPDATE_SETTINGS",
            patch
          });
          if (resp?.ok) this.settings = resp.settings;
          this.render();
        })();
      });
    });
  }
};
var appEl = document.getElementById("app");
if (appEl) {
  const popup = new PopupController(appEl);
  void popup.init();
}
```

`scripts/build.mjs`:

```mjs
import * as esbuild from 'esbuild';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';

const ROOT = process.cwd();
const OUT_DIR = path.join(ROOT, 'extension');

fs.rmSync(OUT_DIR, { recursive: true, force: true });
fs.mkdirSync(path.join(OUT_DIR, 'icons'), { recursive: true });

// 1. Copy static assets
fs.copyFileSync(path.join(ROOT, 'public', 'manifest.json'), path.join(OUT_DIR, 'manifest.json'));
fs.copyFileSync(path.join(ROOT, 'public', 'popup.html'), path.join(OUT_DIR, 'popup.html'));
fs.copyFileSync(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'popup', 'popup.css'), path.join(OUT_DIR, 'popup.css'));

for (const size of [16, 32, 48, 128]) {
  fs.copyFileSync(
    path.join(ROOT, 'public', 'icons', `icon${size}.png`),
    path.join(OUT_DIR, 'icons', `icon${size}.png`)
  );
}

// 2. Bundle Background Service Worker (ESM for MV3)
await esbuild.build({
  entryPoints: ['src/background/index.ts'],
  bundle: true,
  format: 'esm',
  target: 'chrome116',
  minify: false,
  outfile: path.join(OUT_DIR, 'background.js'),
});

// 3. Bundle Content Script (IIFE for MV3 content_scripts)
await esbuild.build({
  entryPoints: ['src/content/index.ts'],
  bundle: true,
  format: 'iife',
  target: 'chrome116',
  minify: false,
  outfile: path.join(OUT_DIR, 'content.js'),
});

// 4. Bundle Toolbar Popup
await esbuild.build({
  entryPoints: ['src/popup/index.ts'],
  bundle: true,
  format: 'esm',
  target: 'chrome116',
  minify: false,
  outfile: path.join(OUT_DIR, 'popup.js'),
});

// 5. Also bundle standalone browser engine+HUD for the interactive Live Demo playground
const DEMO_DIR = path.join(ROOT, 'demo');
fs.mkdirSync(DEMO_DIR, { recursive: true });
await esbuild.build({
  entryPoints: ['src/demo/entry.ts'],
  bundle: true,
  format: 'esm',
  target: 'chrome116',
  minify: false,
  outfile: path.join(DEMO_DIR, 'bundle.js'),
});

console.log('Successfully built unpacked Chrome Extension into ./extension/ and demo bundle.');

```

`scripts/dev-server.mjs`:

```mjs
import fs from 'node:fs';
import http from 'node:http';
import path from 'node:path';
import { URL } from 'node:url';

const PORT = Number(process.env.PORT || 4173);
const DEMO_DIR = path.join(process.cwd(), 'demo');

const apiCache = new Map();

async function fetchJson(url) {
  if (apiCache.has(url)) return apiCache.get(url);
  const res = await fetch(url, {
    headers: {
      Accept: 'application/json',
      'User-Agent': 'ChessSentinelDemo/1.0',
    },
  });
  if (res.status === 404) throw new Error('PLAYER_NOT_FOUND');
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP_${res.status}`);
  const data = await res.json();
  if (typeof data.code === 'number' && data.code === 0 && data.message) {
    throw new Error('CHESSCOM_ARCHIVE_ERROR');
  }
  apiCache.set(url, data);
  return data;
}

async function buildBundle(username, mode) {
  const clean = username.trim().toLowerCase();
  const profile = structuredClone(
    await fetchJson(`https://api.chess.com/pub/player/${encodeURIComponent(clean)}`)
  );
  const stats = await fetchJson(
    `https://api.chess.com/pub/player/${encodeURIComponent(clean)}/stats`
  ).catch(() => ({}));
  const archData = await fetchJson(
    `https://api.chess.com/pub/player/${encodeURIComponent(clean)}/games/archives`
  ).catch(() => ({ archives: [] }));

  const archives = (archData.archives || []).slice(-3).reverse();
  const recentGames = [];
  for (const a of archives) {
    try {
      const m = await fetchJson(a.replace(/^http:/i, 'https:'));
      if (Array.isArray(m.games)) recentGames.push(...m.games);
      if (recentGames.length >= 35) break;
    } catch {
      continue;
    }
  }

  let fakeNowMs = undefined;
  if (mode === 'preban_cheater' && clean === 'sumitgm000') {
    profile.status = 'basic';
    fakeNowMs = 1735050794000;
  }

  return {
    bundle: {
      username: clean,
      profile,
      stats,
      recentGames: recentGames.slice(-120),
      fetchedAt: Date.now(),
    },
    fakeNowMs,
  };
}

const MIME = {
  '.html': 'text/html; charset=utf-8',
  '.js': 'application/javascript; charset=utf-8',
  '.css': 'text/css; charset=utf-8',
  '.png': 'image/png',
  '.svg': 'image/svg+xml',
};

const server = http.createServer(async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const reqUrl = new URL(req.url || '/', `http://${req.headers.host || 'localhost'}`);

    if (reqUrl.pathname === '/api/player-bundle') {
      const username = reqUrl.searchParams.get('username') || 'hikaru';
      const mode = reqUrl.searchParams.get('mode') || 'normal';
      try {
        const { bundle, fakeNowMs } = await buildBundle(username, mode);
        res.writeHead(200, { 'Content-Type': 'application/json; charset=utf-8' });
        res.end(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, bundle, fakeNowMs }));
      } catch (e) {
        res.writeHead(200, { 'Content-Type': 'application/json; charset=utf-8' });
        res.end(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Error' }));
      }
      return;
    }

    const relPath = reqUrl.pathname === '/' ? 'index.html' : reqUrl.pathname.replace(/^\/+/, '');
    const safePath = path.normalize(path.join(DEMO_DIR, relPath));
    if (!safePath.startsWith(DEMO_DIR) || !fs.existsSync(safePath)) {
      res.writeHead(404);
      res.end('Not found');
      return;
    }

    const ext = path.extname(safePath);
    res.writeHead(200, { 'Content-Type': MIME[ext] || 'application/octet-stream' });
    fs.createReadStream(safePath).pipe(res);
  } catch (err) {
    res.writeHead(500);
    res.end('Internal server error');
  }
});

server.listen(PORT, '0.0.0.0', () => {
  console.log(`Chess Sentinel Live HUD Preview running on http://0.0.0.0:${PORT}`);
});

```

`scripts/generate-icons.mjs`:

```mjs
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import zlib from 'node:zlib';

const ROOT = process.cwd();
const ICONS_DIR = path.join(ROOT, 'public', 'icons');
const ASSETS_DIR = path.join(ROOT, 'assets');

fs.mkdirSync(ICONS_DIR, { recursive: true });
fs.mkdirSync(ASSETS_DIR, { recursive: true });

// Pure JS CRC32 table for valid PNG chunks
const crcTable = new Uint32Array(256);
for (let n = 0; n < 256; n++) {
  let c = n;
  for (let k = 0; k < 8; k++) {
    c = c & 1 ? 0xedb88320 ^ (c >>> 1) : c >>> 1;
  }
  crcTable[n] = c >>> 0;
}

function crc32(buf) {
  let c = 0xffffffff;
  for (let i = 0; i < buf.length; i++) {
    c = crcTable[(c ^ buf[i]) & 0xff] ^ (c >>> 8);
  }
  return (c ^ 0xffffffff) >>> 0;
}

function makeChunk(type, data) {
  const typeBuf = Buffer.from(type, 'ascii');
  const lenBuf = Buffer.alloc(4);
  lenBuf.writeUInt32BE(data.length, 0);
  const crcInput = Buffer.concat([typeBuf, data]);
  const crcBuf = Buffer.alloc(4);
  crcBuf.writeUInt32BE(crc32(crcInput), 0);
  return Buffer.concat([lenBuf, typeBuf, data, crcBuf]);
}

function encodePng(width, height, rgba) {
  const signature = Buffer.from([137, 80, 78, 71, 13, 10, 26, 10]);
  const ihdr = Buffer.alloc(13);
  ihdr.writeUInt32BE(width, 0);
  ihdr.writeUInt32BE(height, 4);
  ihdr[8] = 8; // bit depth
  ihdr[9] = 6; // RGBA
  ihdr[10] = 0;
  ihdr[11] = 0;
  ihdr[12] = 0;

  const stride = width * 4;
  const raw = Buffer.alloc((stride + 1) * height);
  for (let y = 0; y < height; y++) {
    raw[y * (stride + 1)] = 0; // Filter None
    rgba.copy(raw, y * (stride + 1) + 1, y * stride, (y + 1) * stride);
  }
  const idat = zlib.deflateSync(raw, { level: 9 });
  return Buffer.concat([
    signature,
    makeChunk('IHDR', ihdr),
    makeChunk('IDAT', idat),
    makeChunk('IEND', Buffer.alloc(0)),
  ]);
}

// Signed distance / supersampled vector rasterizer for Sentinel Shield Icon
function pointInShield(nx, ny) {
  // Normalized coords in [-1, 1]
  // Shield top at y = -0.78, shoulders at y = -0.50, bottom tip at y = 0.82
  if (ny < -0.78 || ny > 0.82) return false;
  let halfW;
  if (ny < -0.48) {
    // Sloped top crest
    const t = (ny - -0.78) / 0.3;
    halfW = 0.12 + t * 0.58;
  } else if (ny < 0.05) {
    halfW = 0.70 - (ny - -0.48) * 0.06;
  } else {
    const t = (ny - 0.05) / 0.77;
    halfW = 0.67 * (1 - t * t * 0.98);
  }
  return Math.abs(nx) <= halfW;
}

function distToSegment(px, py, ax, ay, bx, by) {
  const vx = bx - ax;
  const vy = by - ay;
  const wx = px - ax;
  const wy = py - ay;
  const c1 = vx * wx + vy * wy;
  if (c1 <= 0) return Math.hypot(px - ax, py - ay);
  const c2 = vx * vx + vy * vy;
  if (c2 <= c1) return Math.hypot(px - bx, py - by);
  const b = c1 / c2;
  return Math.hypot(px - (ax + b * vx), py - (ay + b * vy));
}

function renderShieldIcon(size) {
  const rgba = Buffer.alloc(size * size * 4);
  const samples = 4; // 4x4 MSAA supersampling

  for (let y = 0; y < size; y++) {
    for (let x = 0; x < size; x++) {
      let rSum = 0,
        gSum = 0,
        bSum = 0,
        aSum = 0;

      for (let sy = 0; sy < samples; sy++) {
        for (let sx = 0; sx < samples; sx++) {
          const ux = ((x + (sx + 0.5) / samples) / size) * 2 - 1;
          const uy = ((y + (sy + 0.5) / samples) / size) * 2 - 1;

          // Rounded squircle dark card background
          const cornerR = 0.42;
          const qx = Math.max(0, Math.abs(ux) - (0.94 - cornerR));
          const qy = Math.max(0, Math.abs(uy) - (0.94 - cornerR));
          const inCard = Math.hypot(qx, qy) <= cornerR;

          if (!inCard) continue;

          // Base dark obsidian gradient
          let r = 11 + (1 - uy) * 7;
          let g = 17 + (1 - uy) * 11;
          let b = 31 + (1 - uy) * 16;

          const inOuter = pointInShield(ux, uy);
          const inInner = pointInShield(ux * 1.15, uy * 1.15);

          if (inOuter && !inInner) {
            // Glowing emerald-to-cyan shield rim
            const mix = (uy + 0.8) / 1.6;
            r = 16 * (1 - mix) + 56 * mix;
            g = 185 * (1 - mix) + 189 * mix;
            b = 129 * (1 - mix) + 248 * mix;
          } else if (inInner) {
            // Inner tactical radar surface
            r = 13;
            g = 34;
            b = 48;
          }

          // Radar check / pulse mark inside shield
          const d1 = distToSegment(ux, uy, -0.26, 0.02, -0.05, 0.24);
          const d2 = distToSegment(ux, uy, -0.05, 0.24, 0.30, -0.18);
          const strokeD = Math.min(d1, d2);
          if (strokeD <= 0.105) {
            r = 52;
            g = 211;
            b = 153;
          } else if (strokeD <= 0.16 && inInner) {
            r += 18;
            g += 65;
            b += 55;
          }

          rSum += Math.min(255, r);
          gSum += Math.min(255, g);
          bSum += Math.min(255, b);
          aSum += 255;
        }
      }

      const totalSamples = samples * samples;
      const idx = (y * size + x) * 4;
      const alpha = Math.round(aSum / totalSamples);
      if (alpha > 0) {
        const cov = aSum / 255;
        rgba[idx] = Math.round(rSum / cov);
        rgba[idx + 1] = Math.round(gSum / cov);
        rgba[idx + 2] = Math.round(bSum / cov);
        rgba[idx + 3] = alpha;
      }
    }
  }

  return encodePng(size, size, rgba);
}

for (const size of [16, 32, 48, 128]) {
  const buf = renderShieldIcon(size);
  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(ICONS_DIR, `icon${size}.png`), buf);
}
```

```mjs
import { execFileSync } from 'node:child_process';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';

const ROOT = process.cwd();
const pkg = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(ROOT, 'package.json'), 'utf8'));
const zipName = `chess-sentinel-v${pkg.version}-chrome.zip`;
const zipPath = path.join(ROOT, zipName);

if (fs.existsSync(zipPath)) {
  fs.unlinkSync(zipPath);
}

execFileSync('zip', ['-r', '-q', zipPath, '.'], {
  cwd: path.join(ROOT, 'extension'),
});

console.log(`Packaged release archive: ${zipName}`);
```

`src/api/chesscom-client.ts`:

```ts
import {
  ChessComGame,
  ChessComProfile,
  ChessComStats,
  PlayerRawBundle,
} from '../types';

const API_BASE = 'https://api.chess.com/pub';
const CACHE_TTL_MS = 5 * 60 * 1000; // 5 minutes
const TARGET_MIN_GAMES = 30;
const MAX_ARCHIVES_TO_WALK = 3;

interface CacheItem {
  bundle: PlayerRawBundle;
  expiresAt: number;
}

/**
 * Resilient Chess.com Published-Data API Client.
 * - Enforces serial request execution to respect Chess.com's parallel rate-limiting rules.
 * - Handles Chess.com `{ code: 0 }` archive errors (e.g. oversized monthly archives) gracefully
 *   by walking backward through earlier monthly archives without failing.
 */
export class ChessComApiClient {
  private memoryCache = new Map<string, CacheItem>();
  private queue: Promise<unknown> = Promise.resolve();

  private enqueue<T>(task: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
    const run = this.queue.then(task, task);
    this.queue = run.then(
      () => undefined,
      () => undefined
    );
    return run;
  }

  private async fetchJson<T>(url: string, retries = 2): Promise<T> {
    return this.enqueue(async () => {
      let lastError: Error | null = null;

      for (let attempt = 0; attempt <= retries; attempt++) {
        const controller = new AbortController();
        const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 9000);

        try {
          const response = await fetch(url, {
            method: 'GET',
            headers: {
              Accept: 'application/json',
            },
            credentials: 'omit',
            signal: controller.signal,
          });
          clearTimeout(timer);

          if (response.status === 404) {
            throw new Error(`PLAYER_NOT_FOUND`);
          }

          if (response.status === 429) {
            // Back off gently on rate-limit
            await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 700 * (attempt + 1)));
            continue;
          }

          if (!response.ok) {
            throw new Error(`HTTP_${response.status}`);
          }

          const data = (await response.json()) as Record<string, unknown>;
          // Chess.com occasionally returns 200/500 JSON `{ code: 0, message: "An internal error..." }`
          if (typeof data.code === 'number' && data.code === 0 && data.message) {
            throw new Error(`CHESSCOM_INTERNAL_ERROR`);
          }

          return data as T;
        } catch (err) {
          clearTimeout(timer);
          lastError = err instanceof Error ? err : new Error(String(err));
          if (lastError.message === 'PLAYER_NOT_FOUND') {
            throw lastError;
          }
          if (attempt < retries) {
            await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 350 * (attempt + 1)));
          }
        }
      }

      throw lastError || new Error('API_FETCH_FAILED');
    });
  }

  async fetchProfileOnly(username: string): Promise<ChessComProfile> {
    const clean = username.trim().toLowerCase();
    return this.fetchJson<ChessComProfile>(`${API_BASE}/player/${encodeURIComponent(clean)}`);
  }

  async fetchPlayerBundle(username: string, forceRefresh = false): Promise<PlayerRawBundle> {
    const clean = username.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!clean) throw new Error('INVALID_USERNAME');

    if (!forceRefresh) {
      const cached = this.memoryCache.get(clean);
      if (cached && cached.expiresAt > Date.now()) {
        return cached.bundle;
      }
    }

    const profile = await this.fetchProfileOnly(clean);

    let stats: ChessComStats = {};
    try {
      stats = await this.fetchJson<ChessComStats>(
        `${API_BASE}/player/${encodeURIComponent(clean)}/stats`
      );
    } catch {
      // Newly created accounts may occasionally return empty stats
      stats = {};
    }

    let archiveUrls: string[] = [];
    try {
      const archResp = await this.fetchJson<{ archives?: string[] }>(
        `${API_BASE}/player/${encodeURIComponent(clean)}/games/archives`
      );
      archiveUrls = Array.isArray(archResp.archives) ? archResp.archives : [];
    } catch {
      archiveUrls = [];
    }

    const recentGames: ChessComGame[] = [];
    // Walk from newest monthly archive backwards until we have enough games or hit limit
    const candidateArchives = archiveUrls.slice(-MAX_ARCHIVES_TO_WALK).reverse();
    for (const archUrl of candidateArchives) {
      try {
        const secureUrl = archUrl.replace(/^http:\/\//i, 'https://');
        const monthData = await this.fetchJson<{ games?: ChessComGame[] }>(secureUrl, 1);
        if (Array.isArray(monthData.games)) {
          recentGames.push(...monthData.games);
        }
        if (recentGames.length >= TARGET_MIN_GAMES) {
          break;
        }
      } catch {
        // Gracefully skip broken monthly archive (e.g. Hikaru mega-month `{code:0}`) and try previous month
        continue;
      }
    }

    // Keep most recent 120 games max so memory & CPU stay ultra-lean
    recentGames.sort((a, b) => (b.end_time || 0) - (a.end_time || 0));
    const trimmedGames = recentGames.slice(0, 120);

    const bundle: PlayerRawBundle = {
      username: clean,
      profile,
      stats,
      recentGames: trimmedGames,
      fetchedAt: Date.now(),
    };

    this.memoryCache.set(clean, {
      bundle,
      expiresAt: Date.now() + CACHE_TTL_MS,
    });

    // Cap LRU memory cache at 60 players
    if (this.memoryCache.size > 60) {
      const oldestKey = this.memoryCache.keys().next().value;
      if (oldestKey) this.memoryCache.delete(oldestKey);
    }

    return bundle;
  }
}

export const chessComApi = new ChessComApiClient();

```

`src/background/index.ts`:

```ts
import { chessComApi } from '../api/chesscom-client';
import { TIER_THEMES } from '../core/calibration';
import { analyzePlayerBundle } from '../core/engine';
import {
  getRecentScans,
  getSettings,
  getWatchlist,
  recordRecentScan,
  removeWatchlistEntry,
  saveWatchlist,
  toggleWatchlistEntry,
  updateSettings,
} from '../storage/store';
import { ExtensionMessage, WatchlistEntry } from '../types';

const WATCHLIST_ALARM = 'chess_sentinel_ban_check';

function setTabBadge(tabId: number | undefined, probability: number, tierColor: string): void {
  if (!tabId || typeof chrome === 'undefined' || !chrome.action) return;
  try {
    void chrome.action.setBadgeText({ tabId, text: `${probability}%` });
    void chrome.action.setBadgeBackgroundColor({ tabId, color: tierColor });
  } catch {
    // Ignore tab closed errors
  }
}

async function refreshWatchlistBans(): Promise<WatchlistEntry[]> {
  const list = await getWatchlist();
  if (list.length === 0) return list;

  const updated: WatchlistEntry[] = [];
  for (const item of list) {
    try {
      const profile = await chessComApi.fetchProfileOnly(item.username);
      const status = profile.status || 'basic';
      const isBannedFairPlay = status.toLowerCase().includes('fair_play_violations');
      updated.push({
        ...item,
        accountStatus: status,
        isBannedFairPlay,
        avatarUrl: profile.avatar || item.avatarUrl,
        lastCheckedAt: Date.now(),
      });
    } catch {
      updated.push({ ...item, lastCheckedAt: Date.now() });
    }
  }

  await saveWatchlist(updated);
  return updated;
}

chrome.runtime.onInstalled.addListener(() => {
  if (chrome.alarms) {
    chrome.alarms.create(WATCHLIST_ALARM, { periodInMinutes: 720 }); // Check every 12h
  }
});

if (chrome.alarms) {
  chrome.alarms.onAlarm.addListener((alarm) => {
    if (alarm.name === WATCHLIST_ALARM) {
      void refreshWatchlistBans();
    }
  });
}

chrome.runtime.onMessage.addListener((message: ExtensionMessage, sender, sendResponse) => {
  void (async () => {
    try {
      switch (message.type) {
        case 'ANALYZE_PLAYER': {
          const settings = await getSettings();
          const bundle = await chessComApi.fetchPlayerBundle(
            message.username,
            Boolean(message.forceRefresh)
          );
          const result = analyzePlayerBundle(bundle, {
            preferredFormat: message.preferredFormat,
            ratedOnly: settings.ratedOnly,
            sensitivity: settings.sensitivity,
          });

          await recordRecentScan(result);
          setTabBadge(sender.tab?.id, result.probability, TIER_THEMES[result.tier].color);
          sendResponse({ ok: true, result });
          break;
        }

        case 'GET_SETTINGS': {
          const settings = await getSettings();
          sendResponse({ ok: true, settings });
          break;
        }

        case 'UPDATE_SETTINGS': {
          const settings = await updateSettings(message.patch);
          sendResponse({ ok: true, settings });
          break;
        }

        case 'GET_WATCHLIST': {
          const list = await getWatchlist();
          sendResponse({ ok: true, list });
          break;
        }

        case 'TOGGLE_WATCHLIST': {
          const { added, list } = await toggleWatchlistEntry(message.entry);
          sendResponse({ ok: true, added, list });
          break;
        }

        case 'REMOVE_WATCHLIST': {
          const list = await removeWatchlistEntry(message.username);
          sendResponse({ ok: true, list });
          break;
        }

        case 'REFRESH_WATCHLIST_BANS': {
          const list = await refreshWatchlistBans();
          sendResponse({ ok: true, list });
          break;
        }

        case 'GET_RECENT_SCANS': {
          const scans = await getRecentScans();
          sendResponse({ ok: true, scans });
          break;
        }

        default:
          sendResponse({ ok: false, error: 'UNKNOWN_MESSAGE' });
      }
    } catch (err) {
      const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'UNKNOWN_ERROR';
      sendResponse({ ok: false, error: msg });
    }
  })();

  return true; // Keep async message channel open
});
```
