`src/content/detector.ts`:

```ts
import { TimeClass } from '../types';

export interface DetectedMatchContext {
  topPlayer: string | null;
  bottomPlayer: string | null;
  opponent: string;
  opponentSide: 'top' | 'bottom';
  detectedFormat: TimeClass | undefined;
  isAbortWindowOpen: boolean;
  url: string;
}

const INVALID_NAMES = new Set([
  'opponent',
  'rakip',
  'player',
  'oyuncu',
  'anonymous',
  'anonim',
  'searching',
  'searching...',
  'aranıyor',
  'aranıyor...',
  'waiting',
  'computer',
  'chess.com',
  'guest',
  'member',
]);

const FIDE_TITLES = new Set([
  'GM',
  'WGM',
  'IM',
  'WIM',
  'FM',
  'WFM',
  'NM',
  'WNM',
  'CM',
  'WCM',
  'AGM',
  'AIM',
  'AFM',
  'ACM',
]);

// Chess.com usernames: 2-32 chars, letters, digits (start/middle/end/all-numeric), underscores, hyphens
const VALID_USERNAME_REGEX = /^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]{2,32}$/;

/**
 * Losslessly sanitizes a raw username string extracted from Chess.com DOM.
 * Preserves ALL digits inside usernames (e.g. `kaan1907`, `1905gs`, `007bond`, `ali2`, `123456`)
 * while stripping only parenthesized ratings like `(1540)` and standalone FIDE title prefixes.
 */
export function sanitizeChessUsername(raw: string | null | undefined): string | null {
  if (!raw) return null;

  const withoutParensAndInvisible = raw
    .replace(/[\u200B-\u200D\uFEFF]/g, '')
    // Strip ONLY ratings enclosed in actual parentheses e.g. `(1642)` — never bare digits!
    .replace(/\(\s*\d{2,5}\s*\)/g, ' ')
    .trim();

  if (!withoutParensAndInvisible) return null;

  const tokens = withoutParensAndInvisible.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
  if (tokens.length === 0) return null;

  // Only strip a leading FIDE title token if there is another token after it (e.g. "GM Hikaru123")
  if (tokens.length > 1 && FIDE_TITLES.has(tokens[0].toUpperCase())) {
    tokens.shift();
  }

  const candidate = tokens[0]?.trim();
  if (!candidate) return null;

  const lower = candidate.toLowerCase();
  if (INVALID_NAMES.has(lower)) return null;
  if (lower.startsWith('guest') && /^guest\d+$/i.test(lower)) return null;
  if (!VALID_USERNAME_REGEX.test(candidate)) return null;

  return candidate;
}

export function isChessComBoardUrl(url: string): boolean {
  return /chess\.com\/(game|play\/online|live|analysis\/game|events)/i.test(url);
}

const TOP_ZONE_SELECTORS = [
  '#board-layout-player-top',
  '#board-layout-top',
  '.board-layout-player-top',
  '.board-layout-top',
  '.player-component.player-top',
  '.player-top',
  '#top-player',
  '#topPlayer',
];

const BOTTOM_ZONE_SELECTORS = [
  '#board-layout-player-bottom',
  '#board-layout-bottom',
  '.board-layout-player-bottom',
  '.board-layout-bottom',
  '.player-component.player-bottom',
  '.player-bottom',
  '#bottom-player',
  '#bottomPlayer',
];

/**
 * Resiliently monitors Chess.com's 2026 SPA DOM (including Web Components & Shadow Roots)
 * to detect the opponent username, board orientation, active speed format, and abort window.
 */
export class ChessComOpponentDetector {
  private onContextChange: (ctx: DetectedMatchContext) => void;
  private onMatchLeft: () => void;
  private observer: MutationObserver | null = null;
  private pollTimer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;
  private debounceTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;
  private lastSignature = '';
  private lastUrl = '';
  private active = false;

  constructor(
    onContextChange: (ctx: DetectedMatchContext) => void,
    onMatchLeft: () => void
  ) {
    this.onContextChange = onContextChange;
    this.onMatchLeft = onMatchLeft;
  }

  start(): void {
    if (this.active) return;
    this.active = true;
    this.lastUrl = window.location.href;

    this.observer = new MutationObserver(() => {
      if (this.debounceTimer) clearTimeout(this.debounceTimer);
      this.debounceTimer = setTimeout(() => this.evaluateDom(), 220);
    });

    if (document.body) {
      this.observer.observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true });
    }

    this.pollTimer = setInterval(() => {
      if (!this.active) return;
      if (window.location.href !== this.lastUrl) {
        this.lastUrl = window.location.href;
        this.lastSignature = '';
      }
      this.evaluateDom();
    }, 600);

    setTimeout(() => this.evaluateDom(), 150);
  }

  stop(): void {
    this.active = false;
    if (this.observer) {
      this.observer.disconnect();
      this.observer = null;
    }
    if (this.pollTimer) {
      clearInterval(this.pollTimer);
      this.pollTimer = null;
    }
    if (this.debounceTimer) {
      clearTimeout(this.debounceTimer);
      this.debounceTimer = null;
    }
  }

  forceRecheck(): void {
    this.lastSignature = '';
    this.evaluateDom();
  }

  private hasBoardOnPage(): boolean {
    if (isChessComBoardUrl(window.location.href)) return true;
    return !!document.querySelector(
      'wc-chess-board, chess-board, #board-layout-main, .board-layout-main, #board-layout-player-top, .board-layout-top, .player-component.player-top'
    );
  }

  private getLoggedInUsername(): string | null {
    const notif = document.getElementById('notifications-request');
    const fromNotif = sanitizeChessUsername(notif?.getAttribute('username'));
    if (fromNotif) return fromNotif;

    const profileNav = document.querySelector(
      'a[data-nav-link="profile"][href*="/member/"], .nav-link-profile[href*="/member/"]'
    );
    if (profileNav) {
      const m = (profileNav.getAttribute('href') || '').match(/\/member\/([^/?#]+)/i);
      if (m?.[1]) {
        const s = sanitizeChessUsername(decodeURIComponent(m[1]));
        if (s) return s;
      }
    }
    return null;
  }

  private extractCleanTextWithoutDecorations(root: Element | ShadowRoot): string {
    if (root instanceof Element) {
      const clone = root.cloneNode(true) as Element;
      clone
        .querySelectorAll(
          '.chess-sentinel-inline-pill, [class*="rating"], [class*="title"], [class*="flair"], [class*="country"], [class*="clock"]'
        )
        .forEach((n) => n.remove());
      return clone.textContent || '';
    }
    return root.textContent || '';
  }

  private extractUsernameFromHref(href: string | null): string | null {
    if (!href) return null;
    const m = href.match(/\/member\/([^/?#]+)/i);
    if (!m?.[1]) return null;
    try {
      return sanitizeChessUsername(decodeURIComponent(m[1]));
    } catch {
      return sanitizeChessUsername(m[1]);
    }
  }

  private extractNameFromElement(el: Element | null): string | null {
    if (!el) return null;

    // 1. Direct data attributes (canonical & unaffected by inner badges)
    const attrName =
      el.getAttribute('username') ||
      el.getAttribute('data-username') ||
      el.getAttribute('data-player-username');
    if (attrName) {
      const s = sanitizeChessUsername(attrName);
      if (s) return s;
    }

    // 2. Direct anchor href `/member/{username}`
    if (el.tagName === 'A') {
      const s = this.extractUsernameFromHref(el.getAttribute('href'));
      if (s) return s;
    }

    const nestedMemberLink = el.querySelector('a[href*="/member/"]');
    if (nestedMemberLink) {
      const s = this.extractUsernameFromHref(nestedMemberLink.getAttribute('href'));
      if (s) return s;
    }

    // 3. ShadowRoot inspection for Chess.com Web Components (`cc-user-username-component`)
    if (el.shadowRoot) {
      const shadowLink = el.shadowRoot.querySelector('a[href*="/member/"]');
      if (shadowLink) {
        const s = this.extractUsernameFromHref(shadowLink.getAttribute('href'));
        if (s) return s;
      }
      const shadowUser = el.shadowRoot.querySelector(
        '[data-test-element="user-tagline-username"], .cc-user-username-component, [class*="username"]:not([class*="rating"]):not([class*="title"])'
      );
      if (shadowUser) {
        const s = sanitizeChessUsername(this.extractCleanTextWithoutDecorations(shadowUser));
        if (s) return s;
      }
      const sRoot = sanitizeChessUsername(this.extractCleanTextWithoutDecorations(el.shadowRoot));
      if (sRoot) return sRoot;
    }

    // 4. Specific nested username nodes
    const nestedUser = el.querySelector(
      '[data-test-element="user-tagline-username"], .cc-user-username-component, .user-tagline-username, .user-tagline-compact-username, [class*="username"]:not([class*="rating"]):not([class*="title"])'
    );
    if (nestedUser) {
      const s = sanitizeChessUsername(this.extractCleanTextWithoutDecorations(nestedUser));
      if (s) return s;
    }

    // 5. Clean text of element itself (with ratings/titles/inline-badges stripped)
    return sanitizeChessUsername(this.extractCleanTextWithoutDecorations(el));
  }

  private extractZonePlayer(zoneSelectors: string[]): string | null {
    for (const zSel of zoneSelectors) {
      const zone = document.querySelector(zSel);
      if (!zone) continue;

      const candidateSelectors = [
        'a[href*="/member/"]',
        'cc-user-username-component',
        '[data-test-element="user-tagline-username"]',
        '.cc-user-username-component',
        '.user-username-component.user-tagline-username',
        '.user-tagline-username',
        '.user-tagline-compact-username',
        '[data-username]',
        '[data-player-username]',
        '[class*="username"]:not([class*="rating"]):not([class*="title"])',
      ];

      for (const sel of candidateSelectors) {
        const els = zone.querySelectorAll(sel);
        for (const el of Array.from(els)) {
          if (el.closest('.chess-sentinel-inline-pill')) continue;
          const name = this.extractNameFromElement(el);
          if (name) return name;
        }
      }
    }
    return null;
  }

  extractPlayers(): { topPlayer: string | null; bottomPlayer: string | null } {
    let topPlayer = this.extractZonePlayer(TOP_ZONE_SELECTORS);
    let bottomPlayer = this.extractZonePlayer(BOTTOM_ZONE_SELECTORS);

    // Fallback: Geometric Y-coordinate sorting across all player tagline components on the board
    if (!topPlayer || !bottomPlayer) {
      const candidates = Array.from(
        document.querySelectorAll(
          'cc-user-username-component, [data-test-element="user-tagline-username"], .cc-user-username-component, .user-tagline-username, .user-tagline-compact-username'
        )
      )
        .filter((el) => {
          if (el.closest('.chess-sentinel-inline-pill')) return false;
          const rect = el.getBoundingClientRect();
          return rect.width > 8 && rect.height > 6 && rect.top >= 0 && rect.top < window.innerHeight;
        })
        .sort((a, b) => a.getBoundingClientRect().top - b.getBoundingClientRect().top);

      const names: string[] = [];
      for (const el of candidates) {
        const n = this.extractNameFromElement(el);
        if (n && !names.some((existing) => existing.toLowerCase() === n.toLowerCase())) {
          names.push(n);
        }
      }

      if (!topPlayer && names.length >= 1) topPlayer = names[0];
      if (!bottomPlayer && names.length >= 2) bottomPlayer = names[names.length - 1];
    }

    return { topPlayer, bottomPlayer };
  }

  private detectActiveFormat(): TimeClass | undefined {
    const url = window.location.href.toLowerCase();
    if (url.includes('/daily/')) return 'daily';

    const glyphSelectors = [
      '[data-glyph*="bullet"]',
      '.icon-font-chess.bullet',
      '[class*="time-selector"] [class*="bullet"]',
    ];
    for (const s of glyphSelectors) {
      if (document.querySelector(s)) return 'bullet';
    }
    if (
      document.querySelector(
        '[data-glyph*="blitz"], .icon-font-chess.blitz, [data-glyph*="lightning"]'
      )
    ) {
      return 'blitz';
    }
    if (document.querySelector('[data-glyph*="rapid"], .icon-font-chess.rapid')) {
      return 'rapid';
    }

    const clockEl = document.querySelector(
      '#board-layout-player-top .clock-time-monospace, .board-layout-top .clock-time-monospace, .player-top .clock-time-monospace, .clock-component'
    );
    if (clockEl) {
      const txt = (clockEl.textContent || '').trim();
      const m = txt.match(/^(\d+):(\d{2})/);
      if (m) {
        const mins = Number(m[1]);
        if (mins < 3) return 'bullet';
        if (mins < 10) return 'blitz';
        return 'rapid';
      }
    }

    return undefined;
  }

  private detectAbortWindow(): boolean {
    const plies = document.querySelectorAll(
      'wc-simple-move-list [data-ply], .move-list [data-ply], .vertical-move-list .node, .main-line-row'
    );
    if (plies.length <= 1) {
      const hasLiveClock = !!document.querySelector('.clock-component, .clock-time-monospace');
      return hasLiveClock;
    }
    return false;
  }

  private evaluateDom(): void {
    if (!this.hasBoardOnPage()) {
      if (this.lastSignature !== '') {
        this.lastSignature = '';
        this.onMatchLeft();
      }
      return;
    }

    const { topPlayer, bottomPlayer } = this.extractPlayers();
    const loggedIn = this.getLoggedInUsername()?.toLowerCase();

    // Smart orientation resolution: if the user flipped the board so their own name is on top,
    // automatically target the actual opponent on the bottom!
    let opponent = topPlayer;
    let opponentSide: 'top' | 'bottom' = 'top';

    if (
      loggedIn &&
      topPlayer &&
      bottomPlayer &&
      topPlayer.toLowerCase() === loggedIn &&
      bottomPlayer.toLowerCase() !== loggedIn
    ) {
      opponent = bottomPlayer;
      opponentSide = 'bottom';
    }

    if (!opponent) return;

    const detectedFormat = this.detectActiveFormat();
    const isAbortWindowOpen = this.detectAbortWindow();

    const sig = `${opponent.toLowerCase()}|${opponentSide}|${(bottomPlayer || '').toLowerCase()}|${isAbortWindowOpen ? '1' : '0'}`;
    if (sig === this.lastSignature) return;

    this.lastSignature = sig;
    this.onContextChange({
      topPlayer,
      bottomPlayer,
      opponent,
      opponentSide,
      detectedFormat,
      isAbortWindowOpen,
      url: window.location.href,
    });
  }
}
```

`src/content/hud-overlay.ts`:

```ts
import { LIVE_FORMATS, TIER_THEMES } from '../core/calibration';
import { formatAgeShort, formatFlag, TRANSLATIONS } from '../i18n/translations';
import {
  CheatAnalysisResult,
  Language,
  SentinelSettings,
  TimeClass,
} from '../types';
import { HUD_SHADOW_CSS } from './hud-styles';

function countryToFlagEmoji(code: string | null): string {
  if (!code || code.length !== 2) return '';
  const upper = code.toUpperCase();
  const A = 0x1f1e6;
  const first = upper.charCodeAt(0) - 65;
  const second = upper.charCodeAt(1) - 65;
  if (first < 0 || first > 25 || second < 0 || second > 25) return '';
  return String.fromCodePoint(A + first, A + second);
}

function barColor(score: number): string {
  if (score < 25) return '#10b981';
  if (score < 45) return '#38bdf8';
  if (score < 68) return '#f59e0b';
  if (score < 82) return '#f97316';
  return '#ef4444';
}

function escapeHtml(raw: string): string {
  return raw
    .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
    .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
    .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
    .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
    .replace(/'/g, '&#39;');
}

export interface HudCallbacks {
  onSwapTarget: () => void;
  onRefresh: () => void;
  onToggleLanguage: (nextLang: Language) => void;
  onToggleWatchlist: (result: CheatAnalysisResult) => void;
  onSavePosition: (pos: { right: number; top: number }) => void;
}

export class SentinelHudOverlay {
  private host: HTMLElement | null = null;
  private shadow: ShadowRoot | null = null;
  private cardEl: HTMLElement | null = null;
  private minPillEl: HTMLElement | null = null;

  private settings: SentinelSettings;
  private callbacks: HudCallbacks;
  private currentResult: CheatAnalysisResult | null = null;
  private selectedFormat: TimeClass = 'blitz';
  private isMinimized = false;
  private isHidden = true;
  private detailsExpanded = true;
  private isAbortWindowOpen = false;
  private isWatched = false;
  private loadingUsername: string | null = null;

  // Drag state
  private dragging = false;
  private dragStartX = 0;
  private dragStartY = 0;
  private startRight = 20;
  private startTop = 72;

  constructor(settings: SentinelSettings, callbacks: HudCallbacks) {
    this.settings = settings;
    this.callbacks = callbacks;
    this.detailsExpanded = !settings.compactMode;
    if (settings.hudPosition) {
      this.startRight = settings.hudPosition.right;
      this.startTop = settings.hudPosition.top;
    }
  }

  mount(): void {
    if (this.host && document.body.contains(this.host)) return;

    this.host = document.createElement('div');
    this.host.id = 'chess-sentinel-hud-root';
    this.shadow = this.host.attachShadow({ mode: 'open' });

    const styleEl = document.createElement('style');
    styleEl.textContent = HUD_SHADOW_CSS;

    this.cardEl = document.createElement('div');
    this.cardEl.className = 'sentinel-card hidden';

    this.minPillEl = document.createElement('div');
    this.minPillEl.className = 'minimized-pill hidden';
    this.minPillEl.addEventListener('click', () => {
      this.isMinimized = false;
      this.syncVisibility();
    });

    this.shadow.append(styleEl, this.cardEl, this.minPillEl);
    document.body.appendChild(this.host);

    this.applyPosition();
    this.setupGlobalDragListeners();
  }

  unmount(): void {
    if (this.host) {
      this.host.remove();
      this.host = null;
      this.shadow = null;
      this.cardEl = null;
      this.minPillEl = null;
    }
  }

  updateSettings(next: SentinelSettings): void {
    const prevLang = this.settings.language;
    this.settings = next;
    if (prevLang !== next.language) {
      this.render();
    }
  }

  setWatchedState(watched: boolean): void {
    this.isWatched = watched;
    this.render();
  }

  setAbortWindow(open: boolean): void {
    if (this.isAbortWindowOpen !== open) {
      this.isAbortWindowOpen = open;
      if (this.currentResult) this.render();
    }
  }

  showLoading(username: string): void {
    this.mount();
    this.loadingUsername = username;
    this.currentResult = null;
    this.isHidden = false;
    this.render();
    this.syncVisibility();
  }

  showResult(result: CheatAnalysisResult, watched: boolean, isAbortWindowOpen: boolean): void {
    this.mount();
    this.loadingUsername = null;
    this.currentResult = result;
    this.selectedFormat = result.primaryFormat;
    this.isWatched = watched;
    this.isAbortWindowOpen = isAbortWindowOpen;
    this.isHidden = false;
    this.render();
    this.syncVisibility();
  }

  showError(username: string, message: string): void {
    this.mount();
    this.loadingUsername = null;
    this.isHidden = false;
    if (!this.cardEl) return;

    const lang = this.settings.language;
    this.cardEl.innerHTML = `
      <div class="tier-accent-line"></div>
      ${this.renderHeaderHtml(lang)}
      <div class="loading-box">
        <div>
          <div class="loading-text">@${escapeHtml(username)}</div>
          <div class="loading-sub">${escapeHtml(message)}</div>
        </div>
      </div>
    `;
    this.bindHeaderEvents();
    this.syncVisibility();
  }

  hide(): void {
    this.isHidden = true;
    this.syncVisibility();
  }

  toggleVisibility(): void {
    this.mount();
    if (this.isHidden) {
      this.isHidden = false;
      this.isMinimized = false;
    } else if (this.isMinimized) {
      this.isMinimized = false;
    } else {
      this.isMinimized = true;
    }
    this.syncVisibility();
  }

  expandFromBadge(): void {
    this.mount();
    this.isHidden = false;
    this.isMinimized = false;
    this.syncVisibility();
  }

  private syncVisibility(): void {
    if (!this.cardEl || !this.minPillEl) return;
    if (this.isHidden) {
      this.cardEl.classList.add('hidden');
      this.minPillEl.classList.add('hidden');
      return;
    }
    if (this.isMinimized) {
      this.cardEl.classList.add('hidden');
      this.minPillEl.classList.remove('hidden');
    } else {
      this.cardEl.classList.remove('hidden');
      this.minPillEl.classList.add('hidden');
    }
  }

  private applyPosition(): void {
    const clampedRight = Math.max(8, Math.min(window.innerWidth - 180, this.startRight));
    const clampedTop = Math.max(8, Math.min(window.innerHeight - 80, this.startTop));
    if (this.cardEl) {
      this.cardEl.style.right = `${clampedRight}px`;
      this.cardEl.style.top = `${clampedTop}px`;
    }
    if (this.minPillEl) {
      this.minPillEl.style.right = `${clampedRight}px`;
      this.minPillEl.style.top = `${clampedTop}px`;
    }
  }

  private setupGlobalDragListeners(): void {
    window.addEventListener('pointermove', (e: PointerEvent) => {
      if (!this.dragging) return;
      const dx = e.clientX - this.dragStartX;
      const dy = e.clientY - this.dragStartY;
      this.startRight = Math.max(8, Math.min(window.innerWidth - 220, this.startRight - dx));
      this.startTop = Math.max(8, Math.min(window.innerHeight - 60, this.startTop + dy));
      this.dragStartX = e.clientX;
      this.dragStartY = e.clientY;
      this.applyPosition();
    });

    window.addEventListener('pointerup', () => {
      if (this.dragging) {
        this.dragging = false;
        this.callbacks.onSavePosition({ right: this.startRight, top: this.startTop });
      }
    });
  }

  private renderHeaderHtml(lang: Language): string {
    const t = TRANSLATIONS[lang];
    const nextLang = lang === 'tr' ? 'EN' : 'TR';
    return `
      <div class="hud-header" data-drag-handle="1">
        <div class="brand">
          <svg class="brand-shield" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.2">
            <path d="M12 2L4 5.5v6.2c0 5.3 3.4 9.9 8 11.3 4.6-1.4 8-6 8-11.3V5.5L12 2z"/>
            <path d="M9 12l2 2 4-4" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
          </svg>
          <span class="brand-title">Chess Sentinel</span>
        </div>
        <div class="header-actions">
          <button class="icon-btn lang-btn" data-action="lang" title="Switch Language / Dil Değiştir">${nextLang}</button>
          <button class="icon-btn" data-action="swap" title="${escapeHtml(t.actions.swapTarget)}">⇅</button>
          ${
            this.currentResult
              ? `<button class="icon-btn ${this.isWatched ? 'active' : ''}" data-action="watch" title="${escapeHtml(
                  this.isWatched ? t.actions.watchingSuspect : t.actions.watchSuspect
                )}">👁</button>`
              : ''
          }
          <button class="icon-btn" data-action="refresh" title="${escapeHtml(t.actions.refresh)}">↻</button>
          <button class="icon-btn" data-action="minimize" title="${escapeHtml(t.actions.minimize)}">—</button>
          <button class="icon-btn" data-action="close" title="Close">✕</button>
        </div>
      </div>
    `;
  }

  private bindHeaderEvents(): void {
    if (!this.cardEl) return;
    const header = this.cardEl.querySelector('[data-drag-handle="1"]') as HTMLElement | null;
    if (header) {
      header.addEventListener('pointerdown', (e: PointerEvent) => {
        const target = e.target as HTMLElement;
        if (target.closest('button')) return;
        this.dragging = true;
        this.dragStartX = e.clientX;
        this.dragStartY = e.clientY;
      });
    }

    this.cardEl.querySelectorAll('button[data-action]').forEach((btn) => {
      btn.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
        e.stopPropagation();
        const act = (btn as HTMLElement).dataset.action;
        if (act === 'lang') {
          const next: Language = this.settings.language === 'tr' ? 'en' : 'tr';
          this.settings = { ...this.settings, language: next };
          this.render();
          this.callbacks.onToggleLanguage(next);
        } else if (act === 'swap') {
          this.callbacks.onSwapTarget();
        } else if (act === 'watch' && this.currentResult) {
          this.callbacks.onToggleWatchlist(this.currentResult);
        } else if (act === 'refresh') {
          this.callbacks.onRefresh();
        } else if (act === 'minimize') {
          this.isMinimized = true;
          this.syncVisibility();
        } else if (act === 'close') {
          this.hide();
        }
      });
    });
  }

  private render(): void {
    if (!this.cardEl || !this.minPillEl) return;
    const lang = this.settings.language;
    const t = TRANSLATIONS[lang];

    if (this.loadingUsername && !this.currentResult) {
      this.cardEl.style.setProperty('--tier-color', '#38bdf8');
      this.cardEl.style.setProperty('--tier-border', 'rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.3)');
      this.cardEl.style.setProperty('--tier-glow', 'rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.25)');
      this.cardEl.innerHTML = `
        <div class="tier-accent-line"></div>
        ${this.renderHeaderHtml(lang)}
        <div class="loading-box">
          <div class="spinner"></div>
          <div>
            <div class="loading-text">@${escapeHtml(this.loadingUsername)}</div>
            <div class="loading-sub">${escapeHtml(t.scanningOpponent)}</div>
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
      this.minPillEl.innerHTML = `<span class="min-pct">…</span><span class="min-user">@${escapeHtml(
        this.loadingUsername
      )}</span>`;
      this.bindHeaderEvents();
      return;
    }

    const res = this.currentResult;
    if (!res) return;

    const theme = TIER_THEMES[res.tier];
    this.cardEl.style.setProperty('--tier-color', theme.color);
    this.cardEl.style.setProperty('--tier-bg', theme.accentBg);
    this.cardEl.style.setProperty('--tier-border', theme.border);
    this.cardEl.style.setProperty('--tier-glow', theme.glow);

    this.minPillEl.style.setProperty('--tier-color', theme.color);
    this.minPillEl.style.setProperty('--tier-border', theme.border);
    this.minPillEl.style.setProperty('--tier-glow', theme.glow);

    const fmtData = res.formats[this.selectedFormat] || res.formats[res.primaryFormat];
    const displayProb =
      res.isBannedFairPlay
        ? 100
        : this.selectedFormat === res.primaryFormat
          ? res.probability
          : fmtData.formatProbability;

    // SVG gauge geometry (r = 35 => circumference = 219.91)
    const radius = 35;
    const circ = 2 * Math.PI * radius;
    const offset = circ - (Math.min(100, Math.max(0, displayProb)) / 100) * circ;

    // Alert banner logic
    let alertHtml = '';
    if (res.isBannedFairPlay) {
      alertHtml = `<div class="alert-banner banned">🚨 ${escapeHtml(t.bannedFairPlayBanner)}</div>`;
    } else if (this.isAbortWindowOpen && displayProb >= 55) {
      alertHtml = `<div class="alert-banner abort-warn">⚠️ ${escapeHtml(t.abortWindowActive)} — ${escapeHtml(
        t.considerAborting
      )}</div>`;
    } else if (this.isAbortWindowOpen && displayProb < 30) {
      alertHtml = `<div class="alert-banner abort-safe">🛡️ ${escapeHtml(t.safeToPlay)}</div>`;
    }

    const flagEmoji = countryToFlagEmoji(res.countryCode);
    const avatarHtml = res.avatarUrl
      ? `<img class="avatar" src="${escapeHtml(res.avatarUrl)}" alt="" />`
      : `<div class="avatar">${escapeHtml(res.displayName.slice(0, 1).toUpperCase())}</div>`;

    const titleBadgeHtml = res.title
      ? `<span class="title-pill">${escapeHtml(res.title.toUpperCase())}</span>`
      : '';

    const verdictText =
      res.isBannedFairPlay
        ? t.tiers.banned.summary
        : displayProb >= 60
          ? t.considerAborting
          : displayProb >= 35
            ? t.cautionAdvised
            : t.safeToPlay;

    // Quick Stat Tiles
    const ageStr = formatAgeShort(res.accountAgeDays, lang);
    const accStr = fmtData.avgAccuracy !== null ? `${fmtData.avgAccuracy}%` : '—';
    const accClass =
      fmtData.avgAccuracy !== null && fmtData.avgAccuracy - fmtData.expectedAccuracy >= 4
        ? 'neg'
        : fmtData.avgAccuracy !== null
          ? 'pos'
          : '';

    const cvVal = res.pacing.medianCV;
    const cvStr = cvVal !== null ? String(cvVal) : '—';
    const cvClass =
      cvVal !== null && cvVal < 0.48 ? 'neg' : cvVal !== null && cvVal < 0.62 ? 'warn' : 'pos';

    const d7 = fmtData.eloDelta7d;
    const d7Str = d7 === null ? '0' : d7 > 0 ? `+${d7}` : `${d7}`;
    const d7Class = d7 !== null && d7 >= 90 ? 'warn' : d7 !== null && d7 > 0 ? 'pos' : '';

    const formDotsHtml = res.recentForm
      .map((r) => {
        const lbl =
          lang === 'tr' ? (r === 'W' ? 'G' : r === 'L' ? 'M' : 'B') : r;
        return `<span class="form-dot ${r}">${lbl}</span>`;
      })
      .join('');

    const wldLabel =
      fmtData.recentTotal > 0
        ? lang === 'tr'
          ? `Son: ${fmtData.recentWins}G ${fmtData.recentLosses}M ${fmtData.recentDraws}B (%${fmtData.recentWinPct})`
          : `Recent: ${fmtData.recentWins}W ${fmtData.recentLosses}L ${fmtData.recentDraws}D (${fmtData.recentWinPct}%)`
        : lang === 'tr'
          ? `Genel: %${fmtData.overallWinPct} (${fmtData.overallTotal})`
          : `All: ${fmtData.overallWinPct}% (${fmtData.overallTotal})`;

    const flagsHtml = res.flags
      .map(
        (f) => `
        <div class="flag-chip ${f.severity}">
          <span>${f.icon}</span>
          <span>${escapeHtml(formatFlag(lang, f.id, f.params))}</span>
        </div>
      `
      )
      .join('');

    const signalRows: Array<{ label: string; score: number }> = [
      { label: t.signals.recentWinrate, score: res.signals.recentWinrate },
      { label: t.signals.overallWinrate, score: res.signals.overallWinrate },
      { label: t.signals.accuracyAnomaly, score: res.signals.accuracyAnomaly },
      { label: t.signals.movePacing, score: res.signals.movePacing },
      { label: t.signals.eloVelocity, score: res.signals.eloVelocity },
      { label: t.signals.accountTrust, score: res.signals.accountTrust },
    ];

    const signalsHtml = signalRows
      .map(
        (s) => `
        <div class="sig-row">
          <div class="sig-head">
            <span>${escapeHtml(s.label)}</span>
            <span class="sig-score">${s.score}/100</span>
          </div>
          <div class="sig-bar-bg">
            <div class="sig-bar-fill" style="width:${s.score}%;background:${barColor(s.score)}"></div>
          </div>
        </div>
      `
      )
      .join('');

    const formatTabsHtml = LIVE_FORMATS.map((fmt) => {
      const f = res.formats[fmt];
      const active = fmt === this.selectedFormat ? 'active' : '';
      const elo = f.currentRating ? String(f.currentRating) : '—';
      return `
        <button class="fmt-tab ${active}" data-fmt="${fmt}">
          <span>${fmt}</span>
          <span class="fmt-elo">${elo}</span>
        </button>
      `;
    }).join('');

    const pctDisplay =
      lang === 'tr'
        ? `<span>%</span>${displayProb}`
        : `${displayProb}<span>%</span>`;

    this.cardEl.innerHTML = `
      <div class="tier-accent-line"></div>
      ${this.renderHeaderHtml(lang)}
      ${alertHtml}
      <div class="hero">
        <div class="gauge-wrap">
          <svg class="gauge-svg" viewBox="0 0 86 86">
            <circle class="gauge-track" cx="43" cy="43" r="${radius}"></circle>
            <circle
              class="gauge-progress"
              cx="43"
              cy="43"
              r="${radius}"
              stroke-dasharray="${circ.toFixed(2)}"
              stroke-dashoffset="${offset.toFixed(2)}"
            ></circle>
          </svg>
          <div class="gauge-center">
            <div class="gauge-pct">${pctDisplay}</div>
            <div class="gauge-caption">${escapeHtml(t.cheatProbability)}</div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="identity">
          <div class="player-row">
            ${avatarHtml}
            ${titleBadgeHtml}
            <a class="username-link" href="https://www.chess.com/member/${encodeURIComponent(
              res.displayName
            )}" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">
              ${escapeHtml(res.displayName)}
            </a>
            ${flagEmoji ? `<span>${flagEmoji}</span>` : ''}
          </div>
          <div class="tier-badge-row">
            <span class="tier-pill">${escapeHtml(t.tiers[res.tier].label)}</span>
            <span class="conf-tag">• ${escapeHtml(t.confidenceLabel[res.confidence])}</span>
          </div>
          <div class="verdict-sub">${escapeHtml(verdictText)}</div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="format-tabs">${formatTabsHtml}</div>

      <div class="stat-grid">
        <div class="stat-tile">
          <div class="stat-val">${escapeHtml(ageStr)}</div>
          <div class="stat-lbl">${escapeHtml(t.stats.accountAge)}</div>
        </div>
        <div class="stat-tile">
          <div class="stat-val ${accClass}">${escapeHtml(accStr)}</div>
          <div class="stat-lbl">${escapeHtml(t.stats.avgAccuracy)}</div>
        </div>
        <div class="stat-tile">
          <div class="stat-val ${cvClass}">${escapeHtml(cvStr)}</div>
          <div class="stat-lbl">${escapeHtml(t.stats.moveCV)}</div>
        </div>
        <div class="stat-tile">
          <div class="stat-val ${d7Class}">${escapeHtml(d7Str)}</div>
          <div class="stat-lbl">${escapeHtml(t.stats.elo7d)}</div>
        </div>
      </div>

      ${
        res.recentForm.length > 0
          ? `<div class="form-strip">
              <div class="form-dots">${formDotsHtml}</div>
              <div class="wld-summary">${escapeHtml(wldLabel)}</div>
            </div>`
          : ''
      }

      ${flagsHtml ? `<div class="flags-list">${flagsHtml}</div>` : ''}

      <button class="details-toggle" data-toggle-details="1">
        <span>${escapeHtml(this.detailsExpanded ? t.actions.hideDetails : t.actions.toggleDetails)}</span>
        <span>${this.detailsExpanded ? '▴' : '▾'}</span>
      </button>

      <div class="signals-panel ${this.detailsExpanded ? '' : 'collapsed'}">
        ${signalsHtml}
      </div>
    `;

    const minPctText = lang === 'tr' ? `%${displayProb}` : `${displayProb}%`;
    this.minPillEl.innerHTML = `
      <span>🛡️</span>
      <span class="min-pct">${minPctText}</span>
      <span class="min-user">@${escapeHtml(res.displayName)}</span>
    `;

    this.bindHeaderEvents();

    // Format tabs listener
    this.cardEl.querySelectorAll('button[data-fmt]').forEach((btn) => {
      btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        const fmt = (btn as HTMLElement).dataset.fmt as TimeClass;
        if (fmt) {
          this.selectedFormat = fmt;
          this.render();
        }
      });
    });

    // Details expand/collapse listener
    const toggleBtn = this.cardEl.querySelector('[data-toggle-details="1"]');
    if (toggleBtn) {
      toggleBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        this.detailsExpanded = !this.detailsExpanded;
        this.render();
      });
    }
  }
}

```

```ts
    color: #f8fafc;

  }

    color: #fbbf24;
    border-color: rgba(251, 191, 36, 0.45);
    background: rgba(251, 191, 36, 0.14);
  }

    letter-spacing: 0.04em;
    color: #cbd5e1;
  }

  /* Banned or Abort Alert Banner */
  .alert-banner {
    margin: 10px 13px 0;
    padding: 7px 10px;
    border-radius: 9px;
    font-size: 11px;
    font-weight: 700;
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 7px;
    letter-spacing: 0.02em;
  }

  .alert-banner.banned {
    background: rgba(244, 63, 94, 0.2);
    border: 1px solid rgba(244, 63, 94, 0.55);
    color: #fda4af;
    animation: pulseDanger 2s infinite;
  }

    background: rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.16);
    border: 1px solid rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.42);
    color: #fca5a5;

    background: rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.11);
    border: 1px solid rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.28);

  }

  /* Hero Section: Gauge + Player Identity */
  .hero {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 14px;
    padding: 13px 14px 11px;
  }

  .gauge-wrap {
    position: relative;
    width: 86px;
    height: 86px;
    flex-shrink: 0;
  }

    width: 86px;
    height: 86px;
    transform: rotate(-90deg);
  }

    fill: none;
    stroke: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.07);
    stroke-width: 7;
  }

  .gauge-progress {
    fill: none;
    stroke: var(--tier-color, #10b981);
    stroke-width: 7;
    stroke-linecap: round;
    transition: stroke-dashoffset 0.75s cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1), stroke 0.3s ease;
    filter: drop-shadow(0 0 6px var(--tier-glow, rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.5)));
  }

  .gauge-center {

    inset: 0;

    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    text-align: center;
  }

  .gauge-pct {

    font-weight: 800;

    letter-spacing: -0.03em;

    background: #1e293b;

  }

  .title-pill {

    border-radius: 4px;

    color: #fff;
    font-size: 9.5px;
    font-weight: 800;
    letter-spacing: 0.04em;
    flex-shrink: 0;
  }

  .username-link {
    font-size: 14.5px;
    font-weight: 700;
    color: #f8fafc;
    text-decoration: none;
    overflow: hidden;
    text-overflow: ellipsis;
    white-space: nowrap;
  }

  .username-link:hover {
    text-decoration: underline;
    color: #38bdf8;
  }

  .tier-badge-row {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 6px;
    margin-top: 6px;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
  }

  .tier-pill {
    display: inline-flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 5px;
    padding: 2.5px 8px;
    border-radius: 999px;
    font-size: 10px;
    font-weight: 800;
    letter-spacing: 0.06em;
```

`src/content/index.ts`:

```ts
import { DEFAULT_SETTINGS } from '../core/calibration';
import { getSettings, getWatchlist, updateSettings } from '../storage/store';
import {
  CheatAnalysisResult,
  ExtensionMessage,
  SentinelSettings,
  TimeClass,
} from '../types';
import { ChessComOpponentDetector, DetectedMatchContext } from './detector';
import { SentinelHudOverlay } from './hud-overlay';
import { removeInlineUsernameBadges, renderInlineUsernameBadge } from './inline-badge';

class SentinelContentController {
  private settings: SentinelSettings = { ...DEFAULT_SETTINGS };
  private hud: SentinelHudOverlay | null = null;
  private detector: ChessComOpponentDetector | null = null;
  private lastCtx: DetectedMatchContext | null = null;
  private targetSide: 'top' | 'bottom' = 'top';
  private activeUsername: string | null = null;
  private latestResult: CheatAnalysisResult | null = null;

  async init(): Promise<void> {
    this.settings = await getSettings();

    this.hud = new SentinelHudOverlay(this.settings, {
      onSwapTarget: () => this.swapTargetSide(),
      onRefresh: () => {
        if (this.activeUsername) {
          void this.analyzeTarget(this.activeUsername, this.lastCtx?.detectedFormat, true);
        } else {
          this.detector?.forceRecheck();
        }
      },
      onToggleLanguage: (nextLang) => {
        void this.persistSettings({ language: nextLang });
      },
      onToggleWatchlist: (result) => {
        void this.handleWatchlistToggle(result);
      },
      onSavePosition: (pos) => {
        void this.persistSettings({ hudPosition: pos });
      },
    });

    this.detector = new ChessComOpponentDetector(
      (ctx) => this.handleMatchContext(ctx),
      () => this.handleMatchLeft()
    );
    this.detector.start();

    this.setupKeyboardShortcut();
    this.setupStorageSync();
    this.setupRuntimeMessages();
  }

  private async persistSettings(patch: Partial<SentinelSettings>): Promise<void> {
    this.settings = await updateSettings(patch);
    this.hud?.updateSettings(this.settings);
    if (this.latestResult && this.settings.showInlineBadge) {
      renderInlineUsernameBadge(
        this.latestResult,
        this.targetSide,
        this.settings.language,
        () => this.hud?.expandFromBadge()
      );
    }
  }

  private swapTargetSide(): void {
    if (!this.lastCtx) return;
    const nextSide: 'top' | 'bottom' = this.targetSide === 'top' ? 'bottom' : 'top';
    const candidate = nextSide === 'top' ? this.lastCtx.topPlayer : this.lastCtx.bottomPlayer;
    if (!candidate) return;
    this.targetSide = nextSide;
    void this.analyzeTarget(candidate, this.lastCtx.detectedFormat, false);
  }

  private handleMatchContext(ctx: DetectedMatchContext): void {
    const prevOpponent = this.lastCtx?.opponent?.toLowerCase();
    this.lastCtx = ctx;

    // Reset target side to detected opponent side whenever a new opponent appears
    if (ctx.opponent.toLowerCase() !== prevOpponent) {
      this.targetSide = ctx.opponentSide;
    }

    const target =
      this.targetSide === 'bottom'
        ? ctx.bottomPlayer || ctx.opponent
        : ctx.topPlayer || ctx.opponent;

    this.hud?.setAbortWindow(ctx.isAbortWindowOpen);

    if (target.toLowerCase() !== this.activeUsername?.toLowerCase()) {
      void this.analyzeTarget(target, ctx.detectedFormat, false);
    }
  }

  private handleMatchLeft(): void {
    this.activeUsername = null;
    this.latestResult = null;
    this.lastCtx = null;
    removeInlineUsernameBadges();
    this.hud?.hide();
  }

  private async analyzeTarget(
    username: string,
    preferredFormat: TimeClass | undefined,
    forceRefresh: boolean
  ): Promise<void> {
    this.activeUsername = username;

    if (this.settings.autoShowHud || forceRefresh) {
      this.hud?.showLoading(username);
    }

    try {
      const msg: ExtensionMessage = {
        type: 'ANALYZE_PLAYER',
        username,
        preferredFormat,
        forceRefresh,
      };
      const response = (await chrome.runtime.sendMessage(msg)) as {
        ok: boolean;
        result?: CheatAnalysisResult;
        error?: string;
      };

      // Guard against race if opponent changed while request was in flight
      if (this.activeUsername?.toLowerCase() !== username.toLowerCase()) return;

      if (!response || !response.ok || !response.result) {
        this.hud?.showError(username, response?.error || 'Could not retrieve Chess.com telemetry');
        return;
      }

      const result = response.result;
      this.latestResult = result;

      const watchlist = await getWatchlist();
      const isWatched = watchlist.some(
        (w) => w.username.toLowerCase() === result.username.toLowerCase()
      );

      if (this.settings.autoShowHud || forceRefresh) {
        this.hud?.showResult(result, isWatched, this.lastCtx?.isAbortWindowOpen ?? false);
      }

      if (this.settings.showInlineBadge) {
        renderInlineUsernameBadge(result, this.targetSide, this.settings.language, () => {
          this.hud?.showResult(result, isWatched, this.lastCtx?.isAbortWindowOpen ?? false);
          this.hud?.expandFromBadge();
        });
      } else {
        removeInlineUsernameBadges();
      }
    } catch {
      // Service worker asleep or extension reloaded
    }
  }

  private async handleWatchlistToggle(result: CheatAnalysisResult): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const pf = result.formats[result.primaryFormat];
      const msg: ExtensionMessage = {
        type: 'TOGGLE_WATCHLIST',
        entry: {
          username: result.username,
          initialProbability: result.probability,
          ratingAtSave: pf.currentRating,
          format: result.primaryFormat,
          avatarUrl: result.avatarUrl,
          isBannedFairPlay: result.isBannedFairPlay,
          accountStatus: result.accountStatus,
        },
      };
      const resp = (await chrome.runtime.sendMessage(msg)) as { ok: boolean; added?: boolean };
      if (resp && resp.ok) {
        this.hud?.setWatchedState(Boolean(resp.added));
      }
    } catch {
      // Ignore
    }
  }

  private setupKeyboardShortcut(): void {
    window.addEventListener('keydown', (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.altKey && (e.key === 's' || e.key === 'S')) {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (!this.latestResult && this.lastCtx?.opponent) {
          void this.analyzeTarget(this.lastCtx.opponent, this.lastCtx.detectedFormat, true);
        } else {
          this.hud?.toggleVisibility();
        }
      }
    });
  }

  private setupStorageSync(): void {
    if (typeof chrome === 'undefined' || !chrome.storage?.onChanged) return;
    chrome.storage.onChanged.addListener(() => {
      void (async () => {
        this.settings = await getSettings();
        this.hud?.updateSettings(this.settings);
        if (!this.settings.showInlineBadge) {
          removeInlineUsernameBadges();
        } else if (this.latestResult) {
          renderInlineUsernameBadge(
            this.latestResult,
            this.targetSide,
            this.settings.language,
            () => this.hud?.expandFromBadge()
          );
        }
      })();
    });
  }

  private setupRuntimeMessages(): void {
    if (typeof chrome === 'undefined' || !chrome.runtime?.onMessage) return;
    chrome.runtime.onMessage.addListener((msg: ExtensionMessage, _sender, sendResponse) => {
      if (msg.type === 'TOGGLE_HUD_FROM_POPUP') {
        if (!this.latestResult && this.lastCtx?.opponent) {
          void this.analyzeTarget(this.lastCtx.opponent, this.lastCtx.detectedFormat, true);
        } else {
          this.hud?.toggleVisibility();
        }
        sendResponse({ ok: true });
      }
      return false;
    });
  }
}

const controller = new SentinelContentController();
if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => void controller.init());
} else {
  void controller.init();
}

```

`src/content/inline-badge.ts`:

```ts
import { TIER_THEMES } from '../core/calibration';
import { CheatAnalysisResult, Language } from '../types';

const BADGE_CLASS = 'chess-sentinel-inline-pill';
const STYLE_ID = 'chess-sentinel-inline-style';

function ensureInlineStyles(): void {
  if (document.getElementById(STYLE_ID)) return;
  const style = document.createElement('style');
  style.id = STYLE_ID;
  style.textContent = `
    .${BADGE_CLASS} {
      display: inline-flex;
      align-items: center;
      gap: 5px;
      margin-left: 7px;
      padding: 2px 8px;
      border-radius: 999px;
      font-family: 'Inter', -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, 'Segoe UI', Roboto, sans-serif;
      font-size: 11.5px;
      font-weight: 700;
      line-height: 1.35;
      letter-spacing: 0.01em;
      cursor: pointer;
      user-select: none;
      vertical-align: middle;
      transition: transform 0.16s ease, filter 0.16s ease, box-shadow 0.16s ease;
      backdrop-filter: blur(8px);
      white-space: nowrap;
      z-index: 20;
    }
    .${BADGE_CLASS}:hover {
      transform: translateY(-1px) scale(1.03);
      filter: brightness(1.12);
    }
    .${BADGE_CLASS} .cs-pill-dot {
      width: 6px;
      height: 6px;
      border-radius: 50%;
      flex-shrink: 0;
    }
    .${BADGE_CLASS} .cs-pill-wld {
      opacity: 0.85;
      font-weight: 600;
      font-size: 10.5px;
      padding-left: 4px;
      border-left: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.16);
    }
  `;
  document.head.appendChild(style);
}

function findPlayerAnchorContainer(targetSide: 'top' | 'bottom'): Element | null {
  const selectors =
    targetSide === 'top'
      ? [
          '#board-layout-player-top .user-tagline-component',
          '#board-layout-top .user-tagline-component',
          '.board-layout-top .user-tagline-component',
          '.player-component.player-top .user-tagline-component',
          '.player-top .user-tagline-component',
          '#board-layout-player-top .cc-user-block',
          '.player-top .cc-user-block',
          '#board-layout-player-top .user-tagline-compact-row',
          '.board-layout-top cc-user-username-component',
          '#board-layout-player-top cc-user-username-component',
          '.player-top cc-user-username-component',
        ]
      : [
          '#board-layout-player-bottom .user-tagline-component',
          '#board-layout-bottom .user-tagline-component',
          '.board-layout-bottom .user-tagline-component',
          '.player-component.player-bottom .user-tagline-component',
          '.player-bottom .user-tagline-component',
          '#board-layout-player-bottom .cc-user-block',
          '.player-bottom .cc-user-block',
          '#board-layout-player-bottom .user-tagline-compact-row',
          '.board-layout-bottom cc-user-username-component',
          '#board-layout-player-bottom cc-user-username-component',
          '.player-bottom cc-user-username-component',
        ];

  for (const sel of selectors) {
    const el = document.querySelector(sel);
    if (el) {
      // Never append inside the username text element itself — always attach to its tagline row!
      if (
        el.tagName.toLowerCase() === 'cc-user-username-component' ||
        el.classList.contains('cc-user-username-component') ||
        el.classList.contains('user-tagline-username')
      ) {
        return el.parentElement || el;
      }
      return el;
    }
  }
  return null;
}

export function renderInlineUsernameBadge(
  result: CheatAnalysisResult,
  targetSide: 'top' | 'bottom',
  lang: Language,
  onClick: () => void
): void {
  ensureInlineStyles();
  removeInlineUsernameBadges();

  const container = findPlayerAnchorContainer(targetSide);
  if (!container) return;

  const theme = TIER_THEMES[result.tier];
  const pf = result.formats[result.primaryFormat];

  const probText = lang === 'tr' ? `%${result.probability}` : `${result.probability}%`;
  const wldText =
    pf.recentTotal > 0
      ? lang === 'tr'
        ? `${pf.recentWins}G/${pf.recentLosses}M`
        : `${pf.recentWins}W/${pf.recentLosses}L`
      : lang === 'tr'
        ? `${pf.overallWins}G/${pf.overallLosses}M`
        : `${pf.overallWins}W/${pf.overallLosses}L`;

  const pill = document.createElement('span');
  pill.className = BADGE_CLASS;
  pill.style.background = theme.accentBg;
  pill.style.border = `1px solid ${theme.border}`;
  pill.style.color = theme.color;
  pill.style.boxShadow = `0 2px 10px ${theme.glow}`;
  pill.title =
    lang === 'tr'
      ? `Chess Sentinel — Hile Olasılığı: %${result.probability} (Detaylar için tıkla)`
      : `Chess Sentinel — Cheat Probability: ${result.probability}% (Click for HUD)`;

  const dot = document.createElement('span');
  dot.className = 'cs-pill-dot';
  dot.style.background = theme.color;
  dot.style.boxShadow = `0 0 6px ${theme.color}`;

  const probSpan = document.createElement('span');
  probSpan.textContent = probText;

  const wldSpan = document.createElement('span');
  wldSpan.className = 'cs-pill-wld';
  wldSpan.style.color = '#e2e8f0';
  wldSpan.textContent = wldText;

  pill.append(dot, probSpan, wldSpan);
  pill.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopPropagation();
    onClick();
  });

  container.appendChild(pill);
}

export function removeInlineUsernameBadges(): void {
  document.querySelectorAll(`.${BADGE_CLASS}`).forEach((el) => el.remove());
}
```

`src/core/calibration.ts`:

```ts
import { RiskTierId, SensitivityPreset, SentinelSettings, TimeClass } from '../types';

export interface AccuracyBand {
  maxRating: number;
  expected: number;
  ceiling: number;
}

/**
 * Empirically calibrated Chess.com CAPS2 accuracy benchmarks per rating band & speed.
 * `expected`: mean CAPS2 accuracy of legitimate games in that rating bucket.
 * `ceiling`: ~95th-percentile threshold above which single-game performance is a spike.
 */
export const CAPS2_ACCURACY_BANDS: Record<TimeClass, AccuracyBand[]> = {
  bullet: [
    { maxRating: 800, expected: 69, ceiling: 80 },
    { maxRating: 1100, expected: 74, ceiling: 84 },
    { maxRating: 1400, expected: 77, ceiling: 86 },
    { maxRating: 1700, expected: 80, ceiling: 88 },
    { maxRating: 2000, expected: 82.5, ceiling: 90 },
    { maxRating: 2400, expected: 85.5, ceiling: 92 },
    { maxRating: Infinity, expected: 88.5, ceiling: 94.5 },
  ],
  blitz: [
    { maxRating: 800, expected: 70, ceiling: 80 },
    { maxRating: 1100, expected: 75, ceiling: 84 },
    { maxRating: 1400, expected: 78.5, ceiling: 87 },
    { maxRating: 1700, expected: 81, ceiling: 89 },
    { maxRating: 2000, expected: 83.5, ceiling: 90.5 },
    { maxRating: 2400, expected: 86.5, ceiling: 92.5 },
    { maxRating: Infinity, expected: 89.5, ceiling: 95 },
  ],
  rapid: [
    { maxRating: 800, expected: 71, ceiling: 81 },
    { maxRating: 1100, expected: 76, ceiling: 85 },
    { maxRating: 1400, expected: 79.5, ceiling: 88 },
    { maxRating: 1700, expected: 82, ceiling: 90 },
    { maxRating: 2000, expected: 84.5, ceiling: 91.5 },
    { maxRating: 2400, expected: 88, ceiling: 93.5 },
    { maxRating: Infinity, expected: 91, ceiling: 96 },
  ],
  daily: [
    { maxRating: 1200, expected: 78, ceiling: 88 },
    { maxRating: 1800, expected: 84, ceiling: 92 },
    { maxRating: 2400, expected: 89, ceiling: 95 },
    { maxRating: Infinity, expected: 92, ceiling: 97 },
  ],
};

export function getAccuracyBand(format: TimeClass, rating: number): AccuracyBand {
  const list = CAPS2_ACCURACY_BANDS[format] || CAPS2_ACCURACY_BANDS.blitz;
  for (const band of list) {
    if (rating < band.maxRating) return band;
  }
  return list[list.length - 1];
}

export const SENSITIVITY_MULTIPLIERS: Record<SensitivityPreset, number> = {
  conservative: 0.84,
  balanced: 1.0,
  strict: 1.18,
};

export interface TierTheme {
  id: RiskTierId;
  color: string;
  accentBg: string;
  glow: string;
  border: string;
}

export const TIER_THEMES: Record<RiskTierId, TierTheme> = {
  low: {
    id: 'low',

    accentBg: 'rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.14)',
    glow: 'rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.38)',
    border: 'rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.35)',

    color: '#38bdf8',
    accentBg: 'rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.14)',
    glow: 'rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.35)',

export function probabilityToTier(probability: number, isBanned = false): RiskTierId {
  if (isBanned) return 'banned';
  if (probability < 22) return 'low';
  if (probability < 40) return 'guarded';
  if (probability < 62) return 'elevated';
  if (probability < 80) return 'high';
  return 'critical';
}

export function getTitleDampener(title?: string | null): number {
  if (!title) return 1.0;
  const normalized = title.trim().toUpperCase();
  if (normalized === 'GM' || normalized === 'WGM') return 0.42;
  if (normalized === 'IM' || normalized === 'WIM') return 0.58;
  if (normalized === 'FM' || normalized === 'WFM' || normalized === 'NM' || normalized === 'CM') {
    return 0.78;
  }
  return 1.0;
}

  language: 'tr',
  autoShowHud: true,
  compactMode: false,
  showInlineBadge: true,
  ratedOnly: true,
  sensitivity: 'balanced',
  hudPosition: null,
};
```
