`src/core/engine.ts`:

```ts
import {
  CheatAnalysisResult,
  ChessComGame,
  ChessComStats,
  ConfidenceLevel,
  FormatTelemetry,
  InsightFlag,
  PlayerRawBundle,
  SensitivityPreset,
  SignalBreakdown,
  TimeClass,
} from '../types';
import {
  getAccuracyBand,
  getTitleDampener,
  LIVE_FORMATS,
  probabilityToTier,
  SENSITIVITY_MULTIPLIERS,
} from './calibration';
import { computeAggregatePacing } from './pgn-pacing';

const ALL_FORMATS: TimeClass[] = ['rapid', 'blitz', 'bullet', 'daily'];

function sampleWeight(n: number, k = 16): number {
  if (n <= 0) return 0;
  return n / (n + k);
}

/**
 * Evaluates win-rate deviation above the 50% Glicko equilibrium,
 * smoothed by Bayesian sample weight `n / (n + k)`.
 */
export function computeWinrateSignal(wins: number, total: number, k = 16): number {
  if (total < 3) return 0;
  const wr = wins / total;
  if (wr <= 0.51) return 0;

  let raw = 0;
  if (wr <= 0.58) {
    raw = ((wr - 0.51) / 0.07) * 32;
  } else if (wr <= 0.66) {
    raw = 32 + ((wr - 0.58) / 0.08) * 38;
  } else if (wr <= 0.75) {
    raw = 70 + ((wr - 0.66) / 0.09) * 30;
  } else {
    raw = 100 + ((wr - 0.75) / 0.10) * 35;
  }

  return Math.min(100, Math.round(raw * sampleWeight(total, k)));
}

/**
 * Evaluates CAPS2 accuracy anomaly against empirically calibrated Elo-band expectations.
 */
export function computeAccuracySignal(
  avgAccuracy: number | null,
  expected: number,
  highAccRate: number,
  reviewedGames: number
): number {
  if (avgAccuracy === null || reviewedGames < 2) return 0;

  const excess = avgAccuracy - expected;
  let excessScore = 0;
  if (excess > 0.5) {
    if (excess <= 4) excessScore = ((excess - 0.5) / 3.5) * 35;
    else if (excess <= 8) excessScore = 35 + ((excess - 4) / 4) * 40;
    else excessScore = Math.min(105, 75 + ((excess - 8) / 5) * 30);
  }

  let spikeScore = 0;
  if (highAccRate > 0.12) {
    if (highAccRate <= 0.28) spikeScore = ((highAccRate - 0.12) / 0.16) * 50;
    else if (highAccRate <= 0.45) spikeScore = 50 + ((highAccRate - 0.28) / 0.17) * 40;
    else spikeScore = Math.min(105, 90 + ((highAccRate - 0.45) / 0.25) * 20);
  }

  const conf = sampleWeight(reviewedGames, 8);
  return Math.min(100, Math.round((0.6 * excessScore + 0.4 * spikeScore) * conf));
}

/**
 * Evaluates vertical Elo surge (7d and 30d rating jumps) and consecutive win streaks.
 */
export function computeEloVelocitySignal(
  eloDelta7d: number | null,
  eloDelta30d: number | null,
  winStreak: number,
  rd: number | null
): number {
  // High RD (>130) indicates provisional rating swings are somewhat expected
  const rdDamper = rd !== null && rd > 130 ? 0.65 : 1.0;

  let surge7 = 0;
  if (eloDelta7d !== null && eloDelta7d > 55) {
    surge7 = Math.min(100, ((eloDelta7d - 55) / 185) * 95) * rdDamper;
  }

  let surge30 = 0;
  if (eloDelta30d !== null && eloDelta30d > 95) {
    surge30 = Math.min(100, ((eloDelta30d - 95) / 260) * 90) * rdDamper;
  }

  let streakScore = 0;
  if (winStreak >= 6) {
    streakScore = Math.min(95, ((winStreak - 5) / 10) * 85);
  }

  return Math.min(100, Math.round(Math.max(surge7, surge30 * 0.9) * 0.72 + streakScore * 0.45));
}

/**
 * Evaluates account age, Elo-to-age ratio, and live-vs-tactics skill gap.
 */
export function computeAccountTrustSignal(
  accountAgeDays: number | null,
  bestLiveRating: number,
  lifetimeGames: number,
  stats: ChessComStats
): { trustRiskScore: number; ageMultiplier: number; tacticsGapRating: number | null } {
  let ageRisk = 0;
  let ageMultiplier = 1.0;

  if (accountAgeDays !== null) {
    if (accountAgeDays <= 7) {
      ageRisk = 92;
      ageMultiplier = 1.52;
    } else if (accountAgeDays <= 14) {
      ageRisk = 78;
      ageMultiplier = 1.40;
    } else if (accountAgeDays <= 30) {
      ageRisk = 64;
      ageMultiplier = 1.30;
    } else if (accountAgeDays <= 90) {
      ageRisk = 46;
      ageMultiplier = 1.18;
    } else if (accountAgeDays <= 180) {
      ageRisk = 28;
      ageMultiplier = 1.09;
    } else if (accountAgeDays <= 365) {
      ageRisk = 14;
      ageMultiplier = 1.03;
    } else if (accountAgeDays >= 1095 && lifetimeGames >= 800) {
      ageRisk = 0;
      ageMultiplier = 0.90; // Veteran honest baseline dampener
    }
  }

  // Fresh account already at high Elo (>= 1650 within 60 days)
  if (accountAgeDays !== null && accountAgeDays <= 75 && bestLiveRating >= 1650) {
    const ratingExcess = Math.min(25, ((bestLiveRating - 1600) / 600) * 25);
    ageRisk = Math.min(100, ageRisk + ratingExcess);
    ageMultiplier = Math.min(1.68, ageMultiplier + 0.14);
  }

  // Check Live Rating vs Tactics Rating huge discrepancy (common in burner cheat accounts)
  const tacticsHighest = stats.tactics?.highest?.rating ?? null;
  let tacticsGapRating: number | null = null;
  if (
    bestLiveRating >= 1850 &&
    tacticsHighest !== null &&
    tacticsHighest > 0 &&
    bestLiveRating - tacticsHighest >= 900
  ) {
    tacticsGapRating = tacticsHighest;
    ageRisk = Math.min(100, ageRisk + 26);
  }

  return {
    trustRiskScore: Math.round(ageRisk),
    ageMultiplier,
    tacticsGapRating,
  };
}

function extractCountryCode(countryUrl?: string): string | null {
  if (!countryUrl) return null;
  const parts = countryUrl.split('/').filter(Boolean);
  const code = parts[parts.length - 1];
  return code && code.length === 2 ? code.toUpperCase() : null;
}

function buildFormatTelemetry(
  format: TimeClass,
  stats: ChessComStats,
  games: ChessComGame[],
  usernameLower: string,
  ratedOnly: boolean,
  nowSec: number
): FormatTelemetry {
  const recObj = stats[`chess_${format}`] as ChessComStats['chess_blitz'];
  const currentRating = recObj?.last?.rating ?? null;
  const peakRating = recObj?.best?.rating ?? currentRating;
  const rd = recObj?.last?.rd ?? null;

  const overallWins = recObj?.record?.win ?? 0;
  const overallLosses = recObj?.record?.loss ?? 0;
  const overallDraws = recObj?.record?.draw ?? 0;
  const overallTotal = overallWins + overallLosses + overallDraws;
  const overallWinPct = overallTotal > 0 ? Math.round((overallWins / overallTotal) * 100) : 0;

  const effectiveRating = currentRating || peakRating || 1200;
  const band = getAccuracyBand(format, effectiveRating);

  let recentWins = 0;
  let recentLosses = 0;
  let recentDraws = 0;
  let accSum = 0;
  let reviewedGames = 0;
  let highAccCount = 0;

  const sevenDaysCutoff = nowSec - 7 * 86400;
  const thirtyDaysCutoff = nowSec - 30 * 86400;

  let oldest7Ts = Infinity;
  let newest7Ts = -Infinity;
  let oldest7Rating: number | null = null;
  let newest7Rating: number | null = null;

  let oldest30Ts = Infinity;
  let newest30Ts = -Infinity;
  let oldest30Rating: number | null = null;
  let newest30Rating: number | null = null;

  for (const g of games) {
    if (g.time_class !== format) continue;
    if (ratedOnly && g.rated === false) continue;

    const isWhite = g.white?.username?.toLowerCase() === usernameLower;
    const isBlack = g.black?.username?.toLowerCase() === usernameLower;
    if (!isWhite && !isBlack) continue;

    const player = isWhite ? g.white : g.black;
    if (!player) continue;

    if (player.result === 'win') recentWins++;
    else if (['checkmated', 'resigned', 'timeout', 'abandoned', 'lose'].includes(player.result)) {
      recentLosses++;
    } else {
      recentDraws++;
    }

    const acc = g.accuracies ? (isWhite ? g.accuracies.white : g.accuracies.black) : null;
    if (typeof acc === 'number' && !Number.isNaN(acc)) {
      reviewedGames++;
      accSum += acc;
      if (acc >= band.ceiling) highAccCount++;
    }

    const ts = g.end_time || 0;
    const r = player.rating;
    if (ts > 0 && typeof r === 'number') {
      if (ts >= sevenDaysCutoff) {
        if (ts < oldest7Ts) {
          oldest7Ts = ts;
          oldest7Rating = r;
        }
        if (ts > newest7Ts) {
          newest7Ts = ts;
          newest7Rating = r;
        }
      }
      if (ts >= thirtyDaysCutoff) {
        if (ts < oldest30Ts) {
          oldest30Ts = ts;
          oldest30Rating = r;
        }
        if (ts > newest30Ts) {
          newest30Ts = ts;
          newest30Rating = r;
        }
      }
    }
  }

  const recentTotal = recentWins + recentLosses + recentDraws;
  const recentWinPct = recentTotal > 0 ? Math.round((recentWins / recentTotal) * 100) : 0;
  const avgAccuracy = reviewedGames > 0 ? Number((accSum / reviewedGames).toFixed(1)) : null;
  const highAccRate = reviewedGames > 0 ? Number((highAccCount / reviewedGames).toFixed(3)) : 0;

  const eloDelta7d =
    oldest7Rating !== null && newest7Rating !== null && newest7Ts > oldest7Ts
      ? newest7Rating - oldest7Rating
      : null;
  const eloDelta30d =
    oldest30Rating !== null && newest30Rating !== null && newest30Ts > oldest30Ts
      ? newest30Rating - oldest30Rating
      : null;

  return {
    format,
    currentRating,
    peakRating,
    rd,
    overallWins,
    overallLosses,
    overallDraws,
    overallTotal,
    overallWinPct,
    recentWins,
    recentLosses,
    recentDraws,
    recentTotal,
    recentWinPct,
    avgAccuracy,
    expectedAccuracy: band.expected,
    highAccCeiling: band.ceiling,
    highAccRate,
    reviewedGames,
    eloDelta7d,
    eloDelta30d,
    formatProbability: 0,
  };
}

export interface AnalyzeOptions {
  preferredFormat?: TimeClass;
  ratedOnly?: boolean;
  sensitivity?: SensitivityPreset;
  nowMs?: number;
}

/**
 * Primary 6-Signal Forensic Cheat Probability Engine (`0% - 100%`).
 */
export function analyzePlayerBundle(
  bundle: PlayerRawBundle,
  options: AnalyzeOptions = {}
): CheatAnalysisResult {
  const ratedOnly = options.ratedOnly ?? true;
  const sensitivity = options.sensitivity ?? 'balanced';
  const nowMs = options.nowMs ?? Date.now();
  const nowSec = Math.floor(nowMs / 1000);

  const { profile, stats, recentGames } = bundle;
  const usernameLower = (profile.username || bundle.username).toLowerCase();
  const displayName = profile.username || bundle.username;
  const accountStatus = profile.status || 'basic';
  const isBannedFairPlay = accountStatus.toLowerCase().includes('fair_play_violations');

  const accountAgeDays = profile.joined
    ? Math.max(0, Math.round((nowSec - profile.joined) / 86400))
    : null;

  // Sort games newest first for streak & form chips
  const validGames = [...recentGames]
    .filter((g) => {
      if (ratedOnly && g.rated === false) return false;
      return (
        g.white?.username?.toLowerCase() === usernameLower ||
        g.black?.username?.toLowerCase() === usernameLower
      );
    })
    .sort((a, b) => (b.end_time || 0) - (a.end_time || 0));

  let winStreak = 0;
  for (const g of validGames) {
    const p = g.white?.username?.toLowerCase() === usernameLower ? g.white : g.black;
    if (p?.result === 'win') winStreak++;
    else break;
  }

  const recentForm: Array<'W' | 'L' | 'D'> = validGames.slice(0, 8).map((g) => {
    const p = g.white?.username?.toLowerCase() === usernameLower ? g.white : g.black;
    if (p?.result === 'win') return 'W';
    if (['checkmated', 'resigned', 'timeout', 'abandoned', 'lose'].includes(p?.result || '')) {
      return 'L';
    }
    return 'D';
  });

  // Compute PGN [%clk] Pacing Telemetry across live games
  const pacing = computeAggregatePacing(validGames, usernameLower, ratedOnly);

  // Build telemetry per format
  const formats = {} as Record<TimeClass, FormatTelemetry>;
  let bestLiveRating = 0;
  let lifetimeGames = 0;

  for (const fmt of ALL_FORMATS) {
    const ft = buildFormatTelemetry(fmt, stats, validGames, usernameLower, ratedOnly, nowSec);
    formats[fmt] = ft;
    lifetimeGames += ft.overallTotal;
    if (fmt !== 'daily' && (ft.currentRating || 0) > bestLiveRating) {
      bestLiveRating = ft.currentRating || 0;
    }
  }

  const { trustRiskScore, ageMultiplier, tacticsGapRating } = computeAccountTrustSignal(
    accountAgeDays,
    bestLiveRating,
    lifetimeGames,
    stats
  );

  const titleDampener = getTitleDampener(profile.title);
  const sensitivityMult = SENSITIVITY_MULTIPLIERS[sensitivity] ?? 1.0;

  // Score each live format individually
  const formatBreakdowns: Record<TimeClass, SignalBreakdown> = {} as Record<
    TimeClass,
    SignalBreakdown
  >;

  for (const fmt of ALL_FORMATS) {
    const ft = formats[fmt];
    const rwr = computeWinrateSignal(ft.recentWins, ft.recentTotal, 14);
    const owr = computeWinrateSignal(ft.overallWins, ft.overallTotal, 22);
    const acc = computeAccuracySignal(
      ft.avgAccuracy,
      ft.expectedAccuracy,
      ft.highAccRate,
      ft.reviewedGames
    );
    const pace = fmt === 'daily' ? 0 : pacing.pacingScore;
    const vel = computeEloVelocitySignal(ft.eloDelta7d, ft.eloDelta30d, winStreak, ft.rd);

    formatBreakdowns[fmt] = {
      recentWinrate: rwr,
      overallWinrate: owr,
      accuracyAnomaly: acc,
      movePacing: pace,
      eloVelocity: vel,
      accountTrust: trustRiskScore,
    };

    // Dynamic Bayesian weight allocation:
    // If Game Review CAPS2 data is absent (<2 reviewed games), redistribute its weight
    // proportionally to Recent Winrate, PGN Move Pacing, and Overall Winrate!
    const hasAcc = ft.reviewedGames >= 2;
    const hasPacing = fmt !== 'daily' && pacing.gamesAnalyzed >= 3;

    let wRwr = 0.25;
    let wOwr = 0.16;
    let wAcc = hasAcc ? 0.25 : 0;
    let wPace = hasPacing ? 0.18 : 0;
    let wVel = 0.08;
    let wTrust = 0.08;

    const activeWeightSum = wRwr + wOwr + wAcc + wPace + wVel + wTrust;
    wRwr /= activeWeightSum;
    wOwr /= activeWeightSum;
    wAcc /= activeWeightSum;
    wPace /= activeWeightSum;
    wVel /= activeWeightSum;
    wTrust /= activeWeightSum;

    const weightedCore =
      wRwr * rwr + wOwr * owr + wAcc * acc + wPace * pace + wVel * vel + wTrust * trustRiskScore;

    // Synergy boost when >= 2 independent strong signals align (e.g. high winrate + robotic pacing)
    const strongSignals = [rwr >= 60, owr >= 58, acc >= 60, pace >= 55, trustRiskScore >= 65].filter(
      Boolean
    ).length;
    const synergyMultiplier = strongSignals >= 3 ? 1.22 : strongSignals === 2 ? 1.10 : 1.0;

    const rawProb =
      weightedCore * ageMultiplier * synergyMultiplier * titleDampener * sensitivityMult;

    const floor = profile.title ? 1 : 3;
    ft.formatProbability =
      ft.overallTotal < 3 && ft.recentTotal < 3
        ? floor
        : Math.min(99, Math.max(floor, Math.round(rawProb)));
  }

  // Choose primary format: preferred format if active, otherwise highest-risk active live format
  let primaryFormat: TimeClass = 'blitz';
  let maxFormatProb = -1;
  let maxFormat: TimeClass = 'blitz';

  for (const fmt of LIVE_FORMATS) {
    if (formats[fmt].formatProbability > maxFormatProb) {
      maxFormatProb = formats[fmt].formatProbability;
      maxFormat = fmt;
    }
  }

  if (
    options.preferredFormat &&
    (formats[options.preferredFormat].recentTotal >= 3 ||
      formats[options.preferredFormat].overallTotal >= 5)
  ) {
    primaryFormat = options.preferredFormat;
  } else if (maxFormatProb > 0) {
    primaryFormat = maxFormat;
  } else {
    // Pick whichever format has most games
    primaryFormat =
      LIVE_FORMATS.slice().sort((a, b) => formats[b].overallTotal - formats[a].overallTotal)[0] ||
      'blitz';
  }

  let probability = formats[primaryFormat].formatProbability;
  // Cross-format bleed: if preferred format is quiet (e.g. 5 blitz games) while rapid is 90% cheater
  if (primaryFormat !== maxFormat && maxFormatProb > probability + 18) {
    probability = Math.round(probability * 0.45 + maxFormatProb * 0.55);
    primaryFormat = maxFormat;
  }

  if (isBannedFairPlay) {
    probability = 100;
  }

  const signals = formatBreakdowns[primaryFormat];
  const pf = formats[primaryFormat];

  // Determine telemetry confidence
  const totalSample = pf.recentTotal + Math.min(30, pf.overallTotal);
  let confidence: ConfidenceLevel = 'low';
  if (isBannedFairPlay || (totalSample >= 25 && (pacing.gamesAnalyzed >= 5 || pf.reviewedGames >= 4))) {
    confidence = 'high';
  } else if (totalSample >= 10) {
    confidence = 'medium';
  }

  // Build human-readable Forensic Insight Flags (sorted by importance)
  const flags: InsightFlag[] = [];

  if (isBannedFairPlay) {
    flags.push({ id: 'banned_fair_play', severity: 'danger', icon: '🚨' });
  }

  if (profile.title) {
    flags.push({
      id: 'verified_title',
      severity: 'info',
      icon: '👑',
      params: { title: profile.title.toUpperCase() },
    });
  }

  if (accountAgeDays !== null && accountAgeDays <= 45 && (pf.currentRating || 0) >= 1650) {
    flags.push({
      id: 'fresh_high_elo',
      severity: 'danger',
      icon: '🆕',
      params: { days: Math.max(1, accountAgeDays), rating: pf.currentRating || 0 },
    });
  } else if (accountAgeDays !== null && accountAgeDays <= 30) {
    flags.push({
      id: 'fresh_account',
      severity: 'warning',
      icon: '🆕',
      params: { days: Math.max(1, accountAgeDays) },
    });
  }

  if (pf.recentTotal >= 8 && pf.recentWinPct >= 68) {
    flags.push({
      id: 'extreme_recent_wr',
      severity: 'danger',
      icon: '🚩',
      params: { games: pf.recentTotal, wr: pf.recentWinPct },
    });
  } else if (pf.recentTotal >= 8 && pf.recentWinPct >= 58) {
    flags.push({
      id: 'elevated_recent_wr',
      severity: 'warning',
      icon: '📈',
      params: { games: pf.recentTotal, wr: pf.recentWinPct },
    });
  }

  if (pf.reviewedGames >= 3 && pf.avgAccuracy !== null && pf.avgAccuracy - pf.expectedAccuracy >= 3.5) {
    const excess = Number((pf.avgAccuracy - pf.expectedAccuracy).toFixed(1));
    flags.push({
      id: 'accuracy_excess',
      severity: excess >= 6.5 ? 'danger' : 'warning',
      icon: '🎯',
      params: { excess, avg: pf.avgAccuracy },
    });
  }

  if (pf.reviewedGames >= 4 && pf.highAccRate >= 0.25) {
    flags.push({
      id: 'accuracy_spikes',
      severity: pf.highAccRate >= 0.4 ? 'danger' : 'warning',
      icon: '⚡',
      params: { games: pf.reviewedGames, rate: Math.round(pf.highAccRate * 100), ceil: pf.highAccCeiling },
    });
  }

  if (pacing.gamesAnalyzed >= 4 && pacing.pacingScore >= 48 && pacing.medianCV !== null) {
    flags.push({
      id: 'robotic_pacing',
      severity: pacing.pacingScore >= 70 ? 'danger' : 'warning',
      icon: '⏱️',
      params: { cv: pacing.medianCV },
    });
  }

  if (pacing.toggleGames >= 1) {
    flags.push({
      id: 'midgame_toggle',
      severity: pacing.toggleGames >= 2 ? 'danger' : 'warning',
      icon: '🤖',
      params: { count: pacing.toggleGames },
    });
  }

  if (pf.eloDelta7d !== null && pf.eloDelta7d >= 85) {
    flags.push({
      id: 'elo_surge',
      severity: pf.eloDelta7d >= 160 ? 'danger' : 'warning',
      icon: '🚀',
      params: { delta: pf.eloDelta7d },
    });
  }

  if (winStreak >= 6) {
    flags.push({
      id: 'hot_streak',
      severity: winStreak >= 10 ? 'danger' : 'warning',
      icon: '🔥',
      params: { streak: winStreak },
    });
  }

  if (tacticsGapRating !== null && (pf.currentRating || bestLiveRating) >= 1850) {
    flags.push({
      id: 'tactics_gap',
      severity: 'warning',
      icon: '🧩',
      params: { rating: pf.currentRating || bestLiveRating, tactics: tacticsGapRating },
    });
  }

  // Positive reassurance flags when risk is low
  if (!isBannedFairPlay && probability < 28) {
    if (pacing.medianCV !== null && pacing.medianCV >= 0.72) {
      flags.push({
        id: 'natural_human',
        severity: 'positive',
        icon: '✅',
        params: { cv: pacing.medianCV },
      });
    }
    if (accountAgeDays !== null && accountAgeDays >= 730 && lifetimeGames >= 500) {
      flags.push({
        id: 'established_veteran',
        severity: 'positive',
        icon: '🛡️',
        params: { years: Math.floor(accountAgeDays / 365), total: lifetimeGames },
      });
    }
  }

  const tier = probabilityToTier(probability, isBannedFairPlay);

  return {
    username: usernameLower,
    displayName,
    avatarUrl: profile.avatar || null,
    countryCode: extractCountryCode(profile.country),
    title: profile.title || null,
    accountStatus,
    isBannedFairPlay,
    accountAgeDays,
    joinedTimestamp: profile.joined || null,
    primaryFormat,
    probability,
    tier,
    confidence,
    winStreak,
    recentForm,
    signals,
    pacing,
    formats,
    flags: flags.slice(0, 5),
    analyzedGamesCount: validGames.length,
    timestamp: nowMs,
  };
}

```

`src/core/pgn-pacing.ts`:

```ts
import { ChessComGame, PacingGameMetrics, PacingSummary } from '../types';

const CLK_REGEX = /\[%clk\s+(\d+):(\d+):(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\]/g;
const TC_HEADER_REGEX = /\[TimeControl\s+"(\d+)(?:\+(\d+))?"\]/;

/**
 * Parses `[%clk H:MM:SS.d]` annotations from a Chess.com PGN and extracts
 * move-by-move elapsed durations for the target player (`isWhite`).
 */
export function extractPlayerMoveTimes(pgn: string, isWhite: boolean): {
  incrementSec: number;
  baseSec: number;
  clocksSec: number[];
  moveDurationsSec: number[];
} | null {
  if (!pgn || !pgn.includes('%clk')) return null;

  const tcMatch = pgn.match(TC_HEADER_REGEX);
  const baseSec = tcMatch ? Number(tcMatch[1]) : 180;
  const incrementSec = tcMatch && tcMatch[2] ? Number(tcMatch[2]) : 0;

  const allClocks: number[] = [];
  let match: RegExpExecArray | null;
  CLK_REGEX.lastIndex = 0;
  while ((match = CLK_REGEX.exec(pgn)) !== null) {
    const hours = Number(match[1]);
    const mins = Number(match[2]);
    const secs = Number(match[3]);
    allClocks.push(hours * 3600 + mins * 60 + secs);
  }

  if (allClocks.length < 24) {
    // Require at least 12 plies per side for meaningful middlegame telemetry
    return null;
  }

  // Standard games start with White on ply 0, Black on ply 1
  const playerClocks = allClocks.filter((_, idx) => (isWhite ? idx % 2 === 0 : idx % 2 === 1));
  if (playerClocks.length < 12) return null;

  const moveDurationsSec: number[] = [];
  for (let i = 1; i < playerClocks.length; i++) {
    const rawElapsed = playerClocks[i - 1] - playerClocks[i] + incrementSec;
    // Clamp network jitter / premove 0.1s floor and 240s cap
    const cleanElapsed = Math.min(240, Math.max(0.1, Number(rawElapsed.toFixed(2))));
    moveDurationsSec.push(cleanElapsed);
  }

  return { incrementSec, baseSec, clocksSec: playerClocks, moveDurationsSec };
}

/**
 * Analyzes a single game's move-time vector for:
 * 1) Middlegame Metronome Flatline (abnormally low CV + near-zero instinctive recaptures)
 * 2) Mid-Game Engine Toggle Signature (human opening -> long launch pause -> robotic streak -> win)
 */
export function analyzeSingleGamePacing(
  pgn: string,
  isWhite: boolean,
  timeClass: string,
  won: boolean
): PacingGameMetrics | null {
  if (timeClass === 'daily') return null;

  const parsed = extractPlayerMoveTimes(pgn, isWhite);
  if (!parsed) return null;

  const { clocksSec, moveDurationsSec } = parsed;
  const scrambleThreshold = timeClass === 'bullet' ? 7 : 18;

  // Isolate non-scramble middlegame moves (move 9 through move 38)
  const midMoves: number[] = [];
  const startIdx = Math.min(8, Math.floor(moveDurationsSec.length * 0.25));
  const endIdx = Math.min(38, moveDurationsSec.length);

  for (let i = startIdx; i < endIdx; i++) {
    const clockRemaining = clocksSec[i + 1] ?? 0;
    if (clockRemaining < scrambleThreshold) break;
    midMoves.push(moveDurationsSec[i]);
  }

  if (midMoves.length < 8) return null;

  const mean = midMoves.reduce((sum, v) => sum + v, 0) / midMoves.length;
  const variance = midMoves.reduce((sum, v) => sum + (v - mean) ** 2, 0) / midMoves.length;
  const std = Math.sqrt(variance);
  const cv = mean > 0 ? std / mean : 1;

  // Instinctive / recapture threshold scales slightly with time control
  const fastThreshold = timeClass === 'bullet' ? 1.1 : timeClass === 'blitz' ? 1.8 : 2.3;
  const fastCount = midMoves.filter((t) => t <= fastThreshold).length;
  const fastMoveRatio = fastCount / midMoves.length;

  // Mechanical engine relay window: typically 2.3s - 8.8s (alt-tab or second-device lookup)
  const roboticLo = timeClass === 'bullet' ? 1.4 : 2.3;
  const roboticHi = timeClass === 'bullet' ? 4.5 : 8.8;
  const roboticCount = midMoves.filter((t) => t >= roboticLo && t <= roboticHi).length;
  const roboticBandRatio = roboticCount / midMoves.length;

  // Flatline game: unnatural consistency + lack of human instant recaptures + non-trivial mean delay
  const minMechanicalMean = timeClass === 'bullet' ? 1.3 : 2.5;
  const isFlatline =
    cv <= 0.44 &&
    mean >= minMechanicalMean &&
    mean <= 11.5 &&
    fastMoveRatio <= 0.09 &&
    roboticBandRatio >= 0.58;

  // Mid-game Toggle Signature:
  // A long pause (18s - 95s in Blitz/Rapid) between move 5 and 22, followed by >= 8 consecutive
  // non-scramble moves where >= 75% fall in [2.0s, 8.5s] with CV <= 0.45 and player wins!
  let hasMidgameToggle = false;
  if (won && timeClass !== 'bullet' && moveDurationsSec.length >= 16) {
    const pauseThreshold = timeClass === 'blitz' ? 18 : 26;
    const latestToggleIdx = Math.min(22, moveDurationsSec.length - 8);
    for (let i = 4; i <= latestToggleIdx; i++) {
      if (moveDurationsSec[i] >= pauseThreshold && moveDurationsSec[i] <= 110) {
        const postSlice = moveDurationsSec.slice(i + 1, Math.min(i + 13, moveDurationsSec.length));
        if (postSlice.length >= 8) {
          const pMean = postSlice.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / postSlice.length;
          const pStd = Math.sqrt(
            postSlice.reduce((a, b) => a + (b - pMean) ** 2, 0) / postSlice.length
          );
          const pCv = pMean > 0 ? pStd / pMean : 1;
          const bandHits = postSlice.filter((t) => t >= 2.0 && t <= 9.0).length / postSlice.length;
          if (pCv <= 0.44 && bandHits >= 0.75 && pMean >= 2.4) {
            hasMidgameToggle = true;
            break;
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }

  return {
    timeClass,
    movesAnalyzed: midMoves.length,
    meanMoveSec: Number(mean.toFixed(2)),
    stdMoveSec: Number(std.toFixed(2)),
    cv: Number(cv.toFixed(3)),
    fastMoveRatio: Number(fastMoveRatio.toFixed(3)),
    roboticBandRatio: Number(roboticBandRatio.toFixed(3)),
    isFlatline,
    hasMidgameToggle,
    won,
  };
}

/**
 * Aggregates PGN `[%clk]` pacing metrics across up to the player's last 35 live games.
 */
export function computeAggregatePacing(
  games: ChessComGame[],
  username: string,
  ratedOnly = true
): PacingSummary {
  const userLower = username.toLowerCase();
  const sorted = [...games]
    .filter((g) => {
      if (ratedOnly && g.rated === false) return false;
      if (!g.pgn || g.time_class === 'daily') return false;
      return (
        g.white?.username?.toLowerCase() === userLower ||
        g.black?.username?.toLowerCase() === userLower
      );
    })
    .sort((a, b) => (b.end_time || 0) - (a.end_time || 0))
    .slice(0, 35);

  const metrics: PacingGameMetrics[] = [];
  for (const g of sorted) {
    const isWhite = g.white?.username?.toLowerCase() === userLower;
    const playerObj = isWhite ? g.white : g.black;
    const won = playerObj?.result === 'win';
    const m = analyzeSingleGamePacing(g.pgn || '', isWhite, String(g.time_class || 'blitz'), won);
    if (m) metrics.push(m);
  }

  if (metrics.length < 3) {
    return {
      gamesAnalyzed: metrics.length,
      medianCV: null,
      minCV: null,
      meanFastMoveRatio: null,
      meanRoboticBandRatio: null,
      flatlineGames: 0,
      flatlineRate: 0,
      toggleGames: 0,
      pacingScore: 0,
    };
  }

  const cvs = metrics.map((m) => m.cv).sort((a, b) => a - b);
  const mid = Math.floor(cvs.length / 2);
  const medianCV =
    cvs.length % 2 === 0 ? Number(((cvs[mid - 1] + cvs[mid]) / 2).toFixed(2)) : Number(cvs[mid].toFixed(2));
  const minCV = Number(cvs[0].toFixed(2));

  const meanFastMoveRatio = Number(
    (metrics.reduce((s, m) => s + m.fastMoveRatio, 0) / metrics.length).toFixed(3)
  );
  const meanRoboticBandRatio = Number(
    (metrics.reduce((s, m) => s + m.roboticBandRatio, 0) / metrics.length).toFixed(3)
  );

  const flatlineGames = metrics.filter((m) => m.isFlatline).length;
  const flatlineRate = Number((flatlineGames / metrics.length).toFixed(3));
  const toggleGames = metrics.filter((m) => m.hasMidgameToggle).length;

  // Component 1: Low median CV score (Human median CV is typically 0.78 - 1.25; <0.58 becomes suspicious)
  let cvRisk = 0;
  if (medianCV < 0.62) {
    cvRisk = Math.min(100, ((0.62 - medianCV) / 0.30) * 85);
  }

  // Component 2: Repeated flatline games rate (1 accidental game can happen; >=20% of games is highly robotic)
  let flatlineRisk = 0;
  if (flatlineRate >= 0.12) {
    flatlineRisk = Math.min(100, ((flatlineRate - 0.10) / 0.35) * 95);
  }

  // Component 3: Absence of instinctive fast recaptures alongside high mechanical band concentration
  let instinctDeficitRisk = 0;
  if (meanFastMoveRatio < 0.08 && meanRoboticBandRatio > 0.52) {
    instinctDeficitRisk = Math.min(
      85,
      ((0.08 - meanFastMoveRatio) / 0.08) * 50 + ((meanRoboticBandRatio - 0.52) / 0.35) * 40
    );
  }

  // Component 4: Mid-game toggle signature bonus
  const toggleBonus = toggleGames >= 2 ? Math.min(35, toggleGames * 14) : toggleGames === 1 ? 10 : 0;

  // Sample confidence damper (smoothly reaches ~0.80 at 10 games, ~0.90 at 22 games)
  const sampleWeight = metrics.length / (metrics.length + 2.5);

  const rawPacing =
    Math.max(cvRisk * 0.5 + flatlineRisk * 0.5, flatlineRisk * 0.85, cvRisk * 0.7 + instinctDeficitRisk * 0.35) +
    toggleBonus;

  const pacingScore = Math.min(100, Math.max(0, Math.round(rawPacing * sampleWeight)));

  return {
    gamesAnalyzed: metrics.length,
    medianCV,
    minCV,
    meanFastMoveRatio,
    meanRoboticBandRatio,
    flatlineGames,
    flatlineRate,
    toggleGames,
    pacingScore,
  };
}
```

`src/demo/entry.ts`:

```ts
import { DEFAULT_SETTINGS } from '../core/calibration';
import { analyzePlayerBundle } from '../core/engine';
import { SentinelHudOverlay } from '../content/hud-overlay';
import { renderInlineUsernameBadge } from '../content/inline-badge';
import { CheatAnalysisResult, Language, PlayerRawBundle, SentinelSettings } from '../types';

const settings: SentinelSettings = {
  ...DEFAULT_SETTINGS,
  language: 'tr',
  autoShowHud: true,
  showInlineBadge: true,
};

const watchlist = new Set<string>();
let currentTarget: 'top' | 'bottom' = 'top';
let lastBundleTop: PlayerRawBundle | null = null;
let lastResult: CheatAnalysisResult | null = null;
let fakeNowOverride: number | undefined = undefined;

const hud = new SentinelHudOverlay(settings, {
  onSwapTarget: () => {
    currentTarget = currentTarget === 'top' ? 'bottom' : 'top';
    const targetUser =
      currentTarget === 'top'
        ? document.getElementById('top-username-txt')?.textContent?.trim() || 'sumitgm000'
        : 'chessleopard1969';
    void loadLivePlayer(targetUser, 'normal');
  },
  onRefresh: () => {
    if (lastBundleTop) {
      renderBundle(lastBundleTop, fakeNowOverride);
    }
  },
  onToggleLanguage: (nextLang: Language) => {
    settings.language = nextLang;
    hud.updateSettings(settings);
    if (lastResult) {
      renderInlineUsernameBadge(lastResult, currentTarget, settings.language, () =>
        hud.expandFromBadge()
      );
    }
  },
  onToggleWatchlist: (res) => {
    const key = res.username.toLowerCase();
    if (watchlist.has(key)) watchlist.delete(key);
    else watchlist.add(key);
    hud.setWatchedState(watchlist.has(key));
  },
  onSavePosition: () => {},
});

function renderBundle(bundle: PlayerRawBundle, nowMs?: number): void {
  const result = analyzePlayerBundle(bundle, {
    ratedOnly: settings.ratedOnly,
    sensitivity: settings.sensitivity,
    nowMs,
  });
  lastResult = result;

  // Update mock Chess.com player bar
  const topNameEl = document.getElementById('top-username-txt');
  const topEloEl = document.getElementById('top-elo-txt');
  const topTitleEl = document.getElementById('top-title-pill');
  const topAvatarEl = document.getElementById('top-avatar-box');

  if (topNameEl) topNameEl.textContent = result.displayName;
  const pf = result.formats[result.primaryFormat];
  if (topEloEl) topEloEl.textContent = `(${pf.currentRating || 1500})`;
  if (topTitleEl) {
    if (result.title) {
      topTitleEl.style.display = 'inline-block';
      topTitleEl.textContent = result.title;
    } else {
      topTitleEl.style.display = 'none';
    }
  }
  if (topAvatarEl) {
    if (result.avatarUrl) {
      topAvatarEl.innerHTML = `<img src="${result.avatarUrl}" style="width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:cover;border-radius:6px" alt=""/>`;
    } else {
      topAvatarEl.textContent = result.displayName[0]?.toUpperCase() || '?';
    }
  }

  hud.showResult(result, watchlist.has(result.username.toLowerCase()), true);
  renderInlineUsernameBadge(result, 'top', settings.language, () => hud.expandFromBadge());
}

async function loadLivePlayer(
  username: string,
  mode: 'normal' | 'preban_cheater' = 'normal'
): Promise<void> {
  const clean = username.trim();
  if (!clean) return;
  currentTarget = 'top';
  hud.showLoading(clean);

  const statusEl = document.getElementById('demo-status');
  if (statusEl) statusEl.textContent = `Canlı Chess.com API'den @${clean} çekiliyor...`;

  try {
    const resp = await fetch(`/api/player-bundle?username=${encodeURIComponent(clean)}&mode=${mode}`);
    const data = (await resp.json()) as {
      ok: boolean;
      bundle?: PlayerRawBundle;
      fakeNowMs?: number;
      error?: string;
    };

    if (!data.ok || !data.bundle) {
      hud.showError(clean, data.error || 'Oyuncu bulunamadı');
      if (statusEl) statusEl.textContent = `Hata: ${data.error || 'Oyuncu bulunamadı'}`;
      return;
    }

    lastBundleTop = data.bundle;
    fakeNowOverride = data.fakeNowMs;
    renderBundle(data.bundle, data.fakeNowMs);
    if (statusEl) {
      statusEl.textContent = `@${data.bundle.profile.username} analiz edildi (${data.bundle.recentGames.length} maç, PGN %clk & CAPS2 aktif).`;
    }
  } catch (err) {
    hud.showError(clean, err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Network error');
  }
}

window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
  // Preset scenario buttons
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-preset]').forEach((btn) => {
    btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      document.querySelectorAll('[data-preset]').forEach((b) => b.classList.remove('active'));
      btn.classList.add('active');
      const user = (btn as HTMLElement).dataset.user || 'sumitgm000';
      const mode = ((btn as HTMLElement).dataset.mode || 'normal') as 'normal' | 'preban_cheater';
      void loadLivePlayer(user, mode);
    });
  });

  const form = document.getElementById('live-lookup-form') as HTMLFormElement | null;
  const input = document.getElementById('live-lookup-input') as HTMLInputElement | null;
  form?.addEventListener('submit', (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (input?.value.trim()) {
      document.querySelectorAll('[data-preset]').forEach((b) => b.classList.remove('active'));
      void loadLivePlayer(input.value.trim(), 'normal');
    }
  });

  // Boot directly into the real pre-ban cheater benchmark so user immediately sees %89 CRITICAL HUD!
  void loadLivePlayer('sumitgm000', 'preban_cheater');
});
```

```ts
      banned_fair_play: 'Account closed by Chess.com for Fair Play (Cheating) violations!',
      verified_title: 'Verified FIDE titled player ({title}) — elite outlier calibration applied',
      fresh_high_elo: 'Brand-new account ({days} days old) already competing at {rating} Elo',
      fresh_account: 'Recently created account ({days} days old) — elevated smurf / burner risk',

      elevated_recent_wr: '{wr}% win rate across last {games} games (above Glicko equilibrium)',
      accuracy_excess: '+{excess}% average CAPS2 accuracy ({avg}%) above Elo band expectation',
      accuracy_spikes: '{rate}% of {games} reviewed games hit ceiling accuracy (≥{ceil}%)',
      robotic_pacing: 'Robotic move-time pacing detected (Median CV: {cv}, near-zero instinctive recaptures)',
      midgame_toggle: 'Mid-game pause followed by flatline mechanical moves in {count} games (toggle signature)',

      hot_streak: 'Currently on a {streak}-game consecutive rated win streak',
      tactics_gap: 'Massive gap between live rating ({rating}) and tactics rating ({tactics})',
      natural_human: 'Organic human move-time variance (CV: {cv}) and balanced win distribution',
      established_veteran: 'Established veteran account ({years}+ years old, {total} lifetime games)',
    },
  },
};

export function formatFlag(lang: Language, flagId: string, params?: Record<string, string | number>): string {
  const template = TRANSLATIONS[lang].flags[flagId] || TRANSLATIONS.en.flags[flagId] || flagId;
  if (!params) return template;
  return template.replace(/\{(\w+)\}/g, (_, key: string) =>
    params[key] !== undefined ? String(params[key]) : `{${key}}`
  );
}

export function formatAgeShort(days: number | null, lang: Language): string {
  if (days === null || days < 0) return '—';
  const s = TRANSLATIONS[lang].stats;
  if (days < 60) return `${Math.max(1, days)} ${s.daysShort}`;
  if (days < 365) return `${Math.round(days / 30)} ${s.monthsShort}`;
  const yrs = (days / 365).toFixed(days >= 1095 ? 0 : 1);
  return `${yrs} ${s.yearsShort}`;
}

```

`src/popup/index.ts`:

```ts
import { DEFAULT_SETTINGS, TIER_THEMES } from '../core/calibration';
import { formatAgeShort, formatFlag, TRANSLATIONS } from '../i18n/translations';
import {
  CheatAnalysisResult,
  ExtensionMessage,
  Language,
  SensitivityPreset,
  SentinelSettings,
  WatchlistEntry,
} from '../types';

type ActiveTab = 'scanner' | 'watchlist' | 'settings';

function escapeHtml(raw: string): string {
  return raw
    .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
    .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
    .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
    .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
    .replace(/'/g, '&#39;');
}

function barColor(score: number): string {
  if (score < 25) return '#10b981';
  if (score < 45) return '#38bdf8';
  if (score < 68) return '#f59e0b';
  if (score < 82) return '#f97316';
  return '#ef4444';
}

class PopupController {
  private root: HTMLElement;
  private tab: ActiveTab = 'scanner';
  private settings: SentinelSettings = { ...DEFAULT_SETTINGS };
  private recentScans: CheatAnalysisResult[] = [];
  private watchlist: WatchlistEntry[] = [];
  private activeResult: CheatAnalysisResult | null = null;
  private scanning = false;
  private checkingBans = false;
  private errorMsg: string | null = null;
  private searchQuery = '';

  constructor(root: HTMLElement) {
    this.root = root;
  }

  async init(): Promise<void> {
    await this.loadAll();
    if (this.recentScans.length > 0) {
      this.activeResult = this.recentScans[0];
    }
    this.render();
  }

  private async send<T>(msg: ExtensionMessage): Promise<T> {
    return (await chrome.runtime.sendMessage(msg)) as T;
  }

  private async loadAll(): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const [sResp, rResp, wResp] = await Promise.all([
        this.send<{ ok: boolean; settings: SentinelSettings }>({ type: 'GET_SETTINGS' }),
        this.send<{ ok: boolean; scans: CheatAnalysisResult[] }>({ type: 'GET_RECENT_SCANS' }),
        this.send<{ ok: boolean; list: WatchlistEntry[] }>({ type: 'GET_WATCHLIST' }),
      ]);
      if (sResp?.ok) this.settings = sResp.settings;
      if (rResp?.ok) this.recentScans = rResp.scans;
      if (wResp?.ok) this.watchlist = wResp.list;
    } catch {
      // Fallback defaults
    }
  }

  private async runPlayerScan(username: string): Promise<void> {
    const clean = username.trim();
    if (!clean) return;
    this.scanning = true;
    this.errorMsg = null;
    this.render();

    try {
      const resp = await this.send<{
        ok: boolean;
        result?: CheatAnalysisResult;
        error?: string;
      }>({
        type: 'ANALYZE_PLAYER',
        username: clean,
        forceRefresh: true,
      });

      if (!resp.ok || !resp.result) {
        this.errorMsg =
          resp.error === 'PLAYER_NOT_FOUND'
            ? this.settings.language === 'tr'
              ? 'Oyuncu bulunamadı.'
              : 'Player not found.'
            : resp.error || 'API Error';
      } else {
        this.activeResult = resp.result;
        const scansResp = await this.send<{ ok: boolean; scans: CheatAnalysisResult[] }>({
          type: 'GET_RECENT_SCANS',
        });
        if (scansResp?.ok) this.recentScans = scansResp.scans;
      }
    } catch (e) {
      this.errorMsg = e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Scan failed';
    } finally {
      this.scanning = false;
      this.render();
    }
  }

  private renderActiveResultCard(lang: Language): string {
    if (!this.activeResult) return '';
    const r = this.activeResult;
    const t = TRANSLATIONS[lang];
    const theme = TIER_THEMES[r.tier];
    const pf = r.formats[r.primaryFormat];
    const pctText = lang === 'tr' ? `%${r.probability}` : `${r.probability}%`;

    const bars = [
      { lbl: t.signals.recentWinrate, val: r.signals.recentWinrate },
      { lbl: t.signals.overallWinrate, val: r.signals.overallWinrate },
      { lbl: t.signals.accuracyAnomaly, val: r.signals.accuracyAnomaly },
      { lbl: t.signals.movePacing, val: r.signals.movePacing },
      { lbl: t.signals.eloVelocity, val: r.signals.eloVelocity },
      { lbl: t.signals.accountTrust, val: r.signals.accountTrust },
    ];

    const flagsHtml = r.flags
      .slice(0, 3)
      .map(
        (f) =>
          `<div style="font-size:10.5px;color:#cbd5e1;line-height:1.3">${f.icon} ${escapeHtml(
            formatFlag(lang, f.id, f.params)
          )}</div>`
      )
      .join('');

    return `
      <div class="scan-result-card" style="border-color:${theme.border}">
        <div class="scan-top">
          <div class="scan-user">
            ${
              r.avatarUrl
                ? `<img class="scan-avatar" src="${escapeHtml(r.avatarUrl)}" alt="" />`
                : `<div class="scan-avatar">${escapeHtml(r.displayName[0]?.toUpperCase() || '?')}</div>`
            }
            <div>
              <div>
                ${r.title ? `<span style="background:#dc2626;color:#fff;font-size:9px;font-weight:800;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:4px;margin-right:4px">${escapeHtml(r.title)}</span>` : ''}
                <a class="scan-name" href="https://www.chess.com/member/${encodeURIComponent(
                  r.displayName
                )}" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">@${escapeHtml(r.displayName)}</a>
              </div>
              <div class="scan-meta">
                ${escapeHtml(r.primaryFormat.toUpperCase())} ${pf.currentRating ?? '—'} •
                ${escapeHtml(formatAgeShort(r.accountAgeDays, lang))} •
                ${escapeHtml(t.tiers[r.tier].label)}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="prob-badge-lg" style="background:${theme.accentBg};color:${theme.color};border:1px solid ${theme.border}">
            ${pctText}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div class="mini-bars">
          ${bars
            .map(
              (b) => `
            <div class="mini-bar-item">
              <div class="mini-bar-lbl">
                <span>${escapeHtml(b.lbl)}</span>
                <strong>${b.val}</strong>
              </div>
              <div class="mini-bar-track">
                <div class="mini-bar-fill" style="width:${b.val}%;background:${barColor(b.val)}"></div>
              </div>
            </div>
          `
            )
            .join('')}
        </div>

        ${flagsHtml ? `<div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:4px;padding-top:4px;border-top:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.06)">${flagsHtml}</div>` : ''}
      </div>
    `;
  }

  private renderScannerTab(lang: Language): string {
    const t = TRANSLATIONS[lang];
    const recentHtml =
      this.recentScans.length === 0
        ? `<div class="empty-state">${escapeHtml(t.popup.emptyScans)}</div>`
        : `<div class="item-list">
            ${this.recentScans
              .slice(0, 8)
              .map((item) => {
                const th = TIER_THEMES[item.tier];
                const pStr = lang === 'tr' ? `%${item.probability}` : `${item.probability}%`;
                return `
                  <div class="list-row" data-select-scan="${escapeHtml(item.username)}">
                    <div class="row-left">
                      <div>
                        <div class="row-name">@${escapeHtml(item.displayName)}</div>
                        <div class="row-sub">${escapeHtml(item.primaryFormat.toUpperCase())} • ${escapeHtml(
                          t.tiers[item.tier].label
                        )}</div>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <span class="row-prob" style="background:${th.accentBg};color:${th.color};border:1px solid ${th.border}">${pStr}</span>
                  </div>
                `;
              })
              .join('')}
          </div>`;

    return `
      <form class="search-bar" id="scan-form">
        <input
          class="search-input"
          id="scan-input"
          type="text"
          value="${escapeHtml(this.searchQuery)}"
          placeholder="${escapeHtml(t.popup.searchPlaceholder)}"
          autocomplete="off"
        />
        <button class="primary-btn" type="submit" ${this.scanning ? 'disabled' : ''}>
          ${this.scanning ? '…' : escapeHtml(t.popup.scanBtn)}
        </button>
      </form>
      ${this.errorMsg ? `<div style="color:#f87171;font-size:11.5px;font-weight:600">${escapeHtml(this.errorMsg)}</div>` : ''}
      ${this.renderActiveResultCard(lang)}
      <div class="sec-title">${escapeHtml(t.popup.recentScansTitle)}</div>
      ${recentHtml}
    `;
  }

  private renderWatchlistTab(lang: Language): string {
    const t = TRANSLATIONS[lang];
    const bannedCount = this.watchlist.filter((w) => w.isBannedFairPlay).length;

    const listHtml =
      this.watchlist.length === 0
        ? `<div class="empty-state">${escapeHtml(t.popup.emptyWatchlist)}</div>`
        : `<div class="item-list">
            ${this.watchlist
              .map((w) => {
                const initText = t.popup.addedOn.replace('{prob}', String(w.initialProbability));
                return `
                  <div class="list-row">
                    <div class="row-left">
                      <div>
                        <a class="row-name" style="text-decoration:none" href="https://www.chess.com/member/${encodeURIComponent(
                          w.username
                        )}" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">@${escapeHtml(w.username)}</a>
                        <div class="row-sub">${escapeHtml(initText)} • ${w.ratingAtSave ?? '—'} Elo</div>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:6px">
                      ${
                        w.isBannedFairPlay
                          ? `<span class="banned-pill">🚨 ${escapeHtml(t.popup.bannedTag)}</span>`
                          : `<span class="active-pill">${escapeHtml(t.popup.activeTag)}</span>`
                      }
                      <button class="pill-btn" data-remove-watch="${escapeHtml(w.username)}" title="${escapeHtml(
                        t.popup.removeBtn
                      )}">✕</button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                `;
              })
              .join('')}
          </div>`;

    return `
      <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between">
        <div class="sec-title">${escapeHtml(t.popup.watchlistTitle)} (${bannedCount}/${this.watchlist.length})</div>
        <button class="primary-btn" id="refresh-bans-btn" style="padding:6px 10px" ${
          this.checkingBans || this.watchlist.length === 0 ? 'disabled' : ''
        }>
          ${escapeHtml(this.checkingBans ? t.popup.checkingBans : t.popup.checkBansBtn)}
        </button>
      </div>
      ${listHtml}
    `;
  }

  private renderSettingsTab(lang: Language): string {
    const t = TRANSLATIONS[lang];
    const s = this.settings;

    return `
      <div class="setting-item">
        <div>
          <div class="setting-title">${escapeHtml(t.popup.settingsLanguage)}</div>
        </div>
        <select class="select-input" data-setting-select="language">
          <option value="tr" ${s.language === 'tr' ? 'selected' : ''}>Türkçe (TR)</option>
          <option value="en" ${s.language === 'en' ? 'selected' : ''}>English (EN)</option>
        </select>
      </div>

      <div class="setting-item">
        <div>
          <div class="setting-title">${escapeHtml(t.popup.settingsSensitivity)}</div>
        </div>
        <select class="select-input" data-setting-select="sensitivity">
          <option value="conservative" ${s.sensitivity === 'conservative' ? 'selected' : ''}>${escapeHtml(
            t.popup.sensitivityConservative
          )}</option>
          <option value="balanced" ${s.sensitivity === 'balanced' ? 'selected' : ''}>${escapeHtml(
            t.popup.sensitivityBalanced
          )}</option>
          <option value="strict" ${s.sensitivity === 'strict' ? 'selected' : ''}>${escapeHtml(
            t.popup.sensitivityStrict
          )}</option>
        </select>
      </div>

      <div class="setting-item">
        <div>
          <div class="setting-title">${escapeHtml(t.popup.settingsAutoHud)}</div>
          <div class="setting-desc">${escapeHtml(t.popup.settingsAutoHudDesc)}</div>
        </div>
        <input class="toggle-input" type="checkbox" data-setting-check="autoShowHud" ${
          s.autoShowHud ? 'checked' : ''
        } />
      </div>

      <div class="setting-item">
        <div>
          <div class="setting-title">${escapeHtml(t.popup.settingsInlineBadge)}</div>
          <div class="setting-desc">${escapeHtml(t.popup.settingsInlineBadgeDesc)}</div>
        </div>
        <input class="toggle-input" type="checkbox" data-setting-check="showInlineBadge" ${
          s.showInlineBadge ? 'checked' : ''
        } />
      </div>

      <div class="setting-item">
        <div>
          <div class="setting-title">${escapeHtml(t.popup.settingsCompact)}</div>
          <div class="setting-desc">${escapeHtml(t.popup.settingsCompactDesc)}</div>
        </div>
        <input class="toggle-input" type="checkbox" data-setting-check="compactMode" ${
          s.compactMode ? 'checked' : ''
        } />
      </div>

      <div class="setting-item">
        <div>
          <div class="setting-title">${escapeHtml(t.popup.settingsRatedOnly)}</div>
          <div class="setting-desc">${escapeHtml(t.popup.settingsRatedOnlyDesc)}</div>
        </div>
        <input class="toggle-input" type="checkbox" data-setting-check="ratedOnly" ${
          s.ratedOnly ? 'checked' : ''
        } />
      </div>
    `;
  }

  private render(): void {
    const lang = this.settings.language;
    const t = TRANSLATIONS[lang];

    const bodyHtml =
      this.tab === 'scanner'
        ? this.renderScannerTab(lang)
        : this.tab === 'watchlist'
          ? this.renderWatchlistTab(lang)
          : this.renderSettingsTab(lang);

    this.root.innerHTML = `
      <div class="popup-shell">
        <div class="top-bar">
          <div class="brand-group">
            <div class="brand-icon">
              <svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.4">
                <path d="M12 2L4 5.5v6.2c0 5.3 3.4 9.9 8 11.3 4.6-1.4 8-6 8-11.3V5.5L12 2z"/>
                <path d="M9 12l2 2 4-4" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
              </svg>
            </div>
            <div>
              <div class="brand-name">${escapeHtml(t.appName)}</div>
              <div class="brand-sub">${escapeHtml(t.appSubtitle)}</div>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="top-controls">
            <button class="pill-btn" id="quick-lang-btn">${lang === 'tr' ? 'EN' : 'TR'}</button>
            <button class="pill-btn" id="toggle-hud-btn" title="${escapeHtml(t.popup.toggleInPageHud)}">HUD</button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div class="nav-tabs">
          <button class="nav-tab ${this.tab === 'scanner' ? 'active' : ''}" data-tab="scanner">
            ${escapeHtml(t.popup.tabScanner)}
          </button>
          <button class="nav-tab ${this.tab === 'watchlist' ? 'active' : ''}" data-tab="watchlist">
            ${escapeHtml(t.popup.tabWatchlist)} (${this.watchlist.length})
          </button>
          <button class="nav-tab ${this.tab === 'settings' ? 'active' : ''}" data-tab="settings">
            ${escapeHtml(t.popup.tabSettings)}
          </button>
        </div>

        <div class="tab-body">${bodyHtml}</div>

        <div class="footer-note">${escapeHtml(t.popup.privacyNote)}</div>
      </div>
    `;

    this.bindEvents();
  }

  private bindEvents(): void {
    this.root.querySelectorAll('button[data-tab]').forEach((btn) => {
      btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        this.tab = (btn as HTMLElement).dataset.tab as ActiveTab;
        this.render();
      });
    });

    const quickLang = this.root.querySelector('#quick-lang-btn');
    quickLang?.addEventListener('click', () => {
      void (async () => {
        const next: Language = this.settings.language === 'tr' ? 'en' : 'tr';
        const resp = await this.send<{ ok: boolean; settings: SentinelSettings }>({
          type: 'UPDATE_SETTINGS',
          patch: { language: next },
        });
        if (resp?.ok) this.settings = resp.settings;
        this.render();
      })();
    });

    const toggleHudBtn = this.root.querySelector('#toggle-hud-btn');
    toggleHudBtn?.addEventListener('click', () => {
      void (async () => {
        try {
          const [activeTab] = await chrome.tabs.query({ active: true, currentWindow: true });
          if (activeTab?.id) {
            await chrome.tabs.sendMessage(activeTab.id, { type: 'TOGGLE_HUD_FROM_POPUP' });
          }
        } catch {
          // Not on a Chess.com tab
        }
      })();
    });

    const form = this.root.querySelector('#scan-form') as HTMLFormElement | null;
    const input = this.root.querySelector('#scan-input') as HTMLInputElement | null;
    if (input) {
      input.addEventListener('input', () => {
        this.searchQuery = input.value;
      });
    }
    if (form && input) {
      form.addEventListener('submit', (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        void this.runPlayerScan(input.value);
      });
    }

    this.root.querySelectorAll('[data-select-scan]').forEach((row) => {
      row.addEventListener('click', () => {
        const u = (row as HTMLElement).dataset.selectScan;
        const found = this.recentScans.find((s) => s.username.toLowerCase() === u?.toLowerCase());
        if (found) {
          this.activeResult = found;
          this.render();
        }
      });
    });

    const refreshBansBtn = this.root.querySelector('#refresh-bans-btn');
    refreshBansBtn?.addEventListener('click', () => {
      void (async () => {
        this.checkingBans = true;
        this.render();
        const resp = await this.send<{ ok: boolean; list: WatchlistEntry[] }>({
          type: 'REFRESH_WATCHLIST_BANS',
        });
        if (resp?.ok) this.watchlist = resp.list;
        this.checkingBans = false;
        this.render();
      })();
    });

    this.root.querySelectorAll('button[data-remove-watch]').forEach((btn) => {
      btn.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
        e.stopPropagation();
        const u = (btn as HTMLElement).dataset.removeWatch;
        if (!u) return;
        void (async () => {
          const resp = await this.send<{ ok: boolean; list: WatchlistEntry[] }>({
            type: 'REMOVE_WATCHLIST',
            username: u,
          });
          if (resp?.ok) this.watchlist = resp.list;
          this.render();
        })();
      });
    });

    this.root.querySelectorAll('input[data-setting-check]').forEach((el) => {
      el.addEventListener('change', () => {
        const inputEl = el as HTMLInputElement;
        const key = inputEl.dataset.settingCheck as keyof SentinelSettings;
        void (async () => {
          const resp = await this.send<{ ok: boolean; settings: SentinelSettings }>({
            type: 'UPDATE_SETTINGS',
            patch: { [key]: inputEl.checked } as Partial<SentinelSettings>,
          });
          if (resp?.ok) this.settings = resp.settings;
        })();
      });
    });

    this.root.querySelectorAll('select[data-setting-select]').forEach((el) => {
      el.addEventListener('change', () => {
        const selEl = el as HTMLSelectElement;
        const key = selEl.dataset.settingSelect;
        void (async () => {
          const patch: Partial<SentinelSettings> =
            key === 'language'
              ? { language: selEl.value as Language }
              : { sensitivity: selEl.value as SensitivityPreset };
          const resp = await this.send<{ ok: boolean; settings: SentinelSettings }>({
            type: 'UPDATE_SETTINGS',
            patch,
          });
          if (resp?.ok) this.settings = resp.settings;
          this.render();
        })();
      });
    });
  }
}

const appEl = document.getElementById('app');
if (appEl) {
  const popup = new PopupController(appEl);
  void popup.init();
}

```

`src/storage/store.ts`:

```ts
import { DEFAULT_SETTINGS } from '../core/calibration';
import { CheatAnalysisResult, SentinelSettings, WatchlistEntry } from '../types';

const KEYS = {
  SETTINGS: 'sentinel_settings_v1',
  WATCHLIST: 'sentinel_watchlist_v1',
  RECENT_SCANS: 'sentinel_recent_scans_v1',
} as const;

const MAX_RECENT_SCANS = 20;

function hasChromeStorage(): boolean {
  return typeof chrome !== 'undefined' && !!chrome.storage && !!chrome.storage.local;
}

export async function getSettings(): Promise<SentinelSettings> {
  if (!hasChromeStorage()) return { ...DEFAULT_SETTINGS };
  try {
    const res = await chrome.storage.local.get(KEYS.SETTINGS);
    return { ...DEFAULT_SETTINGS, ...(res[KEYS.SETTINGS] || {}) };
  } catch {
    return { ...DEFAULT_SETTINGS };
  }
}

export async function updateSettings(patch: Partial<SentinelSettings>): Promise<SentinelSettings> {
  const current = await getSettings();
  const next: SentinelSettings = { ...current, ...patch };
  if (hasChromeStorage()) {
    try {
      await chrome.storage.local.set({ [KEYS.SETTINGS]: next });
    } catch {
      // Ignore quota / context errors
    }
  }
  return next;
}

export async function getWatchlist(): Promise<WatchlistEntry[]> {
  if (!hasChromeStorage()) return [];
  try {
    const res = await chrome.storage.local.get(KEYS.WATCHLIST);
    return Array.isArray(res[KEYS.WATCHLIST]) ? res[KEYS.WATCHLIST] : [];
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
}

export async function saveWatchlist(list: WatchlistEntry[]): Promise<void> {
  if (!hasChromeStorage()) return;
  try {
    await chrome.storage.local.set({ [KEYS.WATCHLIST]: list });
  } catch {
    // Ignore
  }
}

export async function toggleWatchlistEntry(
  entry: Omit<WatchlistEntry, 'addedAt' | 'lastCheckedAt'>
): Promise<{ added: boolean; list: WatchlistEntry[] }> {
  const list = await getWatchlist();
  const userLower = entry.username.toLowerCase();
  const existingIdx = list.findIndex((item) => item.username.toLowerCase() === userLower);

  if (existingIdx >= 0) {
    list.splice(existingIdx, 1);
    await saveWatchlist(list);
    return { added: false, list };
  }

  const now = Date.now();
  const fullEntry: WatchlistEntry = {
    ...entry,
    username: userLower,
    addedAt: now,
    lastCheckedAt: now,
  };
  const next = [fullEntry, ...list].slice(0, 150);
  await saveWatchlist(next);
  return { added: true, list: next };
}

export async function removeWatchlistEntry(username: string): Promise<WatchlistEntry[]> {
  const list = await getWatchlist();
  const userLower = username.toLowerCase();
  const next = list.filter((item) => item.username.toLowerCase() !== userLower);
  await saveWatchlist(next);
  return next;
}

export async function getRecentScans(): Promise<CheatAnalysisResult[]> {
  if (!hasChromeStorage()) return [];
  try {
    const res = await chrome.storage.local.get(KEYS.RECENT_SCANS);
    return Array.isArray(res[KEYS.RECENT_SCANS]) ? res[KEYS.RECENT_SCANS] : [];
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
}

export async function recordRecentScan(result: CheatAnalysisResult): Promise<void> {
  if (!hasChromeStorage()) return;
  try {
    const current = await getRecentScans();
    const filtered = current.filter(
      (item) => item.username.toLowerCase() !== result.username.toLowerCase()
    );
    const next = [result, ...filtered].slice(0, MAX_RECENT_SCANS);
    await chrome.storage.local.set({ [KEYS.RECENT_SCANS]: next });
  } catch {
    // Ignore
  }
}

```

`src/types/index.ts`:

```ts
export type Language = 'en' | 'tr';

export type TimeClass = 'bullet' | 'blitz' | 'rapid' | 'daily';

export type SensitivityPreset = 'conservative' | 'balanced' | 'strict';

export type RiskTierId = 'low' | 'guarded' | 'elevated' | 'high' | 'critical' | 'banned';

export type ConfidenceLevel = 'low' | 'medium' | 'high';

export interface ChessComProfile {
  player_id?: number;
  '@id'?: string;
  url?: string;
  name?: string;
  username: string;
  title?: string;
  followers?: number;
  country?: string;
  location?: string;
  last_online?: number;
  joined?: number;
  status?: string;
  is_streamer?: boolean;
  verified?: boolean;
  avatar?: string;
  league?: string;
}

export interface ChessComFormatRecord {
  last?: {
    rating: number;
    date?: number;
    rd?: number;
  };
  best?: {
    rating: number;
    date?: number;
    game?: string;
  };
  record?: {
    win: number;
    loss: number;
    draw: number;
    time_per_move?: number;
    timeout_percent?: number;
  };
}

export interface ChessComStats {
  chess_bullet?: ChessComFormatRecord;
  chess_blitz?: ChessComFormatRecord;
  chess_rapid?: ChessComFormatRecord;
  chess_daily?: ChessComFormatRecord;
  fide?: number;
  tactics?: {
    highest?: { rating: number; date?: number };
    lowest?: { rating: number; date?: number };
  };
  puzzle_rush?: {
    best?: { total_attempts: number; score: number };
  };
  [key: string]: unknown;
}

export interface ChessComGamePlayer {
  rating: number;
  result: string;
  '@id'?: string;
  username: string;
  uuid?: string;
}

export interface ChessComGame {
  url?: string;
  pgn?: string;
  time_control?: string;
  end_time?: number;
  rated?: boolean;
  accuracies?: {
    white: number;
    black: number;
  };
  tcn?: string;
  uuid?: string;
  initial_setup?: string;
  fen?: string;
  time_class?: TimeClass | string;
  rules?: string;
  white: ChessComGamePlayer;
  black: ChessComGamePlayer;
}

export interface PlayerRawBundle {
  username: string;
  profile: ChessComProfile;
  stats: ChessComStats;
  recentGames: ChessComGame[];
  fetchedAt: number;
}

export interface PacingGameMetrics {
  timeClass: string;
  movesAnalyzed: number;
  meanMoveSec: number;
  stdMoveSec: number;
  cv: number;
  fastMoveRatio: number;
  roboticBandRatio: number;
  isFlatline: boolean;
  hasMidgameToggle: boolean;
  won: boolean;
}

export interface PacingSummary {
  gamesAnalyzed: number;
  medianCV: number | null;
  minCV: number | null;
  meanFastMoveRatio: number | null;
  meanRoboticBandRatio: number | null;
  flatlineGames: number;
  flatlineRate: number;
  toggleGames: number;
  pacingScore: number; // 0-100
}

export interface SignalBreakdown {
  recentWinrate: number;    // 0-100
  overallWinrate: number;   // 0-100
  accuracyAnomaly: number;  // 0-100
  movePacing: number;       // 0-100
  eloVelocity: number;      // 0-100
  accountTrust: number;     // 0-100
}

export interface FormatTelemetry {
  format: TimeClass;
  currentRating: number | null;
  peakRating: number | null;
  rd: number | null;
  overallWins: number;
  overallLosses: number;
  overallDraws: number;
  overallTotal: number;
  overallWinPct: number;
  recentWins: number;
  recentLosses: number;
  recentDraws: number;
  recentTotal: number;
  recentWinPct: number;
  avgAccuracy: number | null;
  expectedAccuracy: number;
  highAccCeiling: number;
  highAccRate: number;
  reviewedGames: number;
  eloDelta7d: number | null;
  eloDelta30d: number | null;
  formatProbability: number;
}

export interface InsightFlag {
  id: string;
  severity: 'positive' | 'info' | 'warning' | 'danger';
  icon: string;
  params?: Record<string, string | number>;
}

export interface CheatAnalysisResult {
  username: string;
  displayName: string;
  avatarUrl: string | null;
  countryCode: string | null;
  title: string | null;
  accountStatus: string;
  isBannedFairPlay: boolean;
  accountAgeDays: number | null;
  joinedTimestamp: number | null;
  primaryFormat: TimeClass;
  probability: number; // 0 - 100 (% cheat probability)
  tier: RiskTierId;
  confidence: ConfidenceLevel;
  winStreak: number;
  recentForm: Array<'W' | 'L' | 'D'>; // Last 8 games
  signals: SignalBreakdown;
  pacing: PacingSummary;
  formats: Record<TimeClass, FormatTelemetry>;
  flags: InsightFlag[];
  analyzedGamesCount: number;
  timestamp: number;
}

export interface WatchlistEntry {
  username: string;
  addedAt: number;
  initialProbability: number;
  ratingAtSave: number | null;
  format: TimeClass;
  avatarUrl: string | null;
  lastCheckedAt: number;
  isBannedFairPlay: boolean;
  accountStatus: string;
  notes?: string;
}

export interface SentinelSettings {
  language: Language;
  autoShowHud: boolean;
  compactMode: boolean;
  showInlineBadge: boolean;
  ratedOnly: boolean;
  sensitivity: SensitivityPreset;
  hudPosition: { right: number; top: number } | null;
}

export type ExtensionMessage =
  | { type: 'ANALYZE_PLAYER'; username: string; preferredFormat?: TimeClass; forceRefresh?: boolean }
  | { type: 'GET_SETTINGS' }
  | { type: 'UPDATE_SETTINGS'; patch: Partial<SentinelSettings> }
  | { type: 'GET_WATCHLIST' }
  | { type: 'TOGGLE_WATCHLIST'; entry: Omit<WatchlistEntry, 'addedAt' | 'lastCheckedAt'> }
  | { type: 'REMOVE_WATCHLIST'; username: string }
  | { type: 'REFRESH_WATCHLIST_BANS' }
  | { type: 'GET_RECENT_SCANS' }
  | { type: 'TOGGLE_HUD_FROM_POPUP' };

```

`tests/detector.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { isChessComBoardUrl, sanitizeChessUsername } from '../src/content/detector';

describe('Chess.com 2026 DOM & URL Detector Utilities', () => {
  it('recognizes all legacy and 2026 Chess.com board routes', () => {
    expect(isChessComBoardUrl('https://www.chess.com/game/live/128552032877')).toBe(true);
    expect(isChessComBoardUrl('https://www.chess.com/game/185842167286')).toBe(true);
    expect(isChessComBoardUrl('https://www.chess.com/play/online')).toBe(true);
    expect(isChessComBoardUrl('https://www.chess.com/play/online/new')).toBe(true);
    expect(isChessComBoardUrl('https://www.chess.com/analysis/game/live/999')).toBe(true);
    expect(isChessComBoardUrl('https://www.chess.com/settings')).toBe(false);
  });

  it('sanitizes usernames while stripping FIDE titles, parenthesized ratings, and UI placeholders', () => {
    expect(sanitizeChessUsername('GM Hikaru (3245)')).toBe('Hikaru');
    expect(sanitizeChessUsername('  GothamChess  ')).toBe('GothamChess');
    expect(sanitizeChessUsername('Opponent')).toBeNull();
    expect(sanitizeChessUsername('Rakip')).toBeNull();
    expect(sanitizeChessUsername('Searching...')).toBeNull();
    expect(sanitizeChessUsername('Guest1948271')).toBeNull();
  });

  it('preserves ALL numeric usernames (1-digit, 3-digit, 4-digit, leading digits, all-digits, mixed)', () => {
    const numericCases: Array<[string, string]> = [
      ['kaan1907', 'kaan1907'],
      ['1907fb', '1907fb'],
      ['007bond', '007bond'],
      ['123chess', '123chess'],
      ['fatihmehmettezcan5', 'fatihmehmettezcan5'],
      ['chessleopard1969', 'chessleopard1969'],
      ['sumitgm000', 'sumitgm000'],
      ['al123', 'al123'],
      ['k1905', 'k1905'],
      ['99_king_99', '99_king_99'],
      ['12345678', '12345678'],
      ['a1b2c3d4', 'a1b2c3d4'],
      ['GM1907', 'GM1907'],
      ['FM kaan1907 (1842)', 'kaan1907'],
      ['1905_gs (2105)', '1905_gs'],
      ['  \u200Bplayer2026\u200B (950) ', 'player2026'],
    ];

    for (const [raw, expected] of numericCases) {
      expect(sanitizeChessUsername(raw)).toBe(expected);
    }
  });
});

```

`tests/engine.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { analyzePlayerBundle } from '../src/core/engine';
import { ChessComGame, PlayerRawBundle } from '../src/types';

function makeHumanPgn(seed = 1): string {
  const w = [
    0.8, 1.4, 6.2, 0.9, 15.4, 1.2, 0.6, 22.1, 4.0, 1.1, 18.0, 0.7, 9.5, 1.3, 25.0, 0.9, 5.2, 1.5,
    14.0, 0.8, 11.2, 1.0,
  ].map((v) => v + (seed % 2) * 0.2);
  let wc = 600;
  let bc = 600;
  const moves = w.map((d, i) => {
    if (i > 0) {
      wc -= d;
      bc -= d;
    }
    const fmt = (sec: number) =>
      `0:${String(Math.floor(sec / 60)).padStart(2, '0')}:${(sec % 60).toFixed(1).padStart(4, '0')}`;
    return `${i + 1}. e4 {[%clk ${fmt(wc)}]} ${i + 1}... e5 {[%clk ${fmt(bc)}]}`;
  });
  return `[TimeControl "600"]\n\n${moves.join(' ')}`;
}

function makeRoboticPgn(): string {
  const w = Array.from({ length: 24 }, (_, i) => 4.8 + (i % 2) * 0.3);
  let wc = 600;
  let bc = 600;
  const moves = w.map((d, i) => {
    if (i > 0) {
      wc -= d;
      bc -= d;
    }
    const fmt = (sec: number) =>
      `0:${String(Math.floor(sec / 60)).padStart(2, '0')}:${(sec % 60).toFixed(1).padStart(4, '0')}`;
    return `${i + 1}. d4 {[%clk ${fmt(wc)}]} ${i + 1}... d5 {[%clk ${fmt(bc)}]}`;
  });
  return `[TimeControl "600"]\n\n${moves.join(' ')}`;
}

describe('6-Signal Bayesian Cheat Probability Engine', () => {
  const nowMs = 1792800000 * 1000;
  const nowSec = 1792800000;

  it('scores an established honest player with ~50% WR and natural pacing in LOW tier (<20%)', () => {
    const games: ChessComGame[] = Array.from({ length: 30 }, (_, i) => ({
      time_class: 'rapid',
      rated: true,
      end_time: nowSec - i * 7200,
      pgn: makeHumanPgn(i),
      accuracies: { white: 80.5, black: 80.2 },
      white: { username: 'HonestClubPlayer', rating: 1550, result: i % 2 === 0 ? 'win' : 'resigned' },
      black: { username: 'OtherPlayer', rating: 1550, result: i % 2 === 0 ? 'resigned' : 'win' },
    }));

    const bundle: PlayerRawBundle = {
      username: 'honestclubplayer',
      profile: {
        username: 'HonestClubPlayer',
        joined: nowSec - 4 * 365 * 86400,
        status: 'premium',
      },
      stats: {
        chess_rapid: {
          last: { rating: 1550, rd: 42 },
          best: { rating: 1610 },
          record: { win: 610, loss: 595, draw: 55 },
        },
      },
      recentGames: games,
      fetchedAt: nowMs,
    };

    const res = analyzePlayerBundle(bundle, { nowMs });
    expect(res.probability).toBeLessThan(20);
    expect(res.tier).toBe('low');
  });

  it('flags a fresh burner account with 82% WR, +9% CAPS2 excess, and robotic pacing as CRITICAL (>=80%)', () => {
    const games: ChessComGame[] = Array.from({ length: 28 }, (_, i) => ({
      time_class: 'rapid',
      rated: true,
      end_time: nowSec - (28 - i) * 3600,
      pgn: makeRoboticPgn(),
      accuracies: { white: 95.4, black: 79.1 },
      white: {
        username: 'StockfishBurner99',
        rating: 1700 + i * 11,
        result: i % 6 === 0 ? 'resigned' : 'win',
      },
      black: {
        username: 'Victim',
        rating: 1850,
        result: i % 6 === 0 ? 'win' : 'checkmated',
      },
    }));

    const bundle: PlayerRawBundle = {
      username: 'stockfishburner99',
      profile: {
        username: 'StockfishBurner99',
        joined: nowSec - 9 * 86400, // 9 days old
        status: 'basic',
      },
      stats: {
        chess_rapid: {
          last: { rating: 2008, rd: 54 },
          best: { rating: 2008 },
          record: { win: 46, loss: 8, draw: 2 },
        },
        tactics: { highest: { rating: 400 } },
      },
      recentGames: games,
      fetchedAt: nowMs,
    };

    const res = analyzePlayerBundle(bundle, { nowMs });
    expect(res.probability).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(85);
    expect(res.tier).toBe('critical');
    expect(res.flags.some((f) => f.id === 'fresh_high_elo')).toBe(true);
  });

  it('immediately assigns 100% BANNED status to accounts closed for fair_play_violations', () => {
    const bundle: PlayerRawBundle = {
      username: 'bannedcheater',
      profile: {
        username: 'BannedCheater',
        joined: nowSec - 100 * 86400,
        status: 'closed:fair_play_violations',
      },
      stats: {},
      recentGames: [],
      fetchedAt: nowMs,
    };

    const res = analyzePlayerBundle(bundle, { nowMs });
    expect(res.probability).toBe(100);
    expect(res.isBannedFairPlay).toBe(true);
    expect(res.tier).toBe('banned');
  });

  it('applies FIDE Grandmaster dampener so verified GMs with high winrates do not trigger false alarms', () => {
    const games: ChessComGame[] = Array.from({ length: 24 }, (_, i) => ({
      time_class: 'blitz',
      rated: true,
      end_time: nowSec - i * 1800,
      pgn: makeHumanPgn(i),
      accuracies: { white: 90.2, black: 86.0 },
      white: {
        username: 'SuperGM_Test',
        rating: 3120,
        result: i % 4 === 0 ? 'timeout' : 'win',
      },
      black: {
        username: 'OpponentGM',
        rating: 2980,
        result: i % 4 === 0 ? 'win' : 'resigned',
      },
    }));

    const bundle: PlayerRawBundle = {
      username: 'supergm_test',
      profile: {
        username: 'SuperGM_Test',
        title: 'GM',
        joined: nowSec - 8 * 365 * 86400,
        status: 'premium',
      },
      stats: {
        chess_blitz: {
          last: { rating: 3120, rd: 38 },
          best: { rating: 3250 },
          record: { win: 14200, loss: 4800, draw: 1900 },
        },
      },
      recentGames: games,
      fetchedAt: nowMs,
    };

    const res = analyzePlayerBundle(bundle, { nowMs });
    expect(res.probability).toBeLessThan(35);
    expect(res.flags.some((f) => f.id === 'verified_title')).toBe(true);
  });
});

```

`tests/pgn-pacing.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import {
  analyzeSingleGamePacing,
  computeAggregatePacing,
  extractPlayerMoveTimes,
} from '../src/core/pgn-pacing';
import { ChessComGame } from '../src/types';

function buildMockPgn(whiteDeltas: number[], blackDeltas: number[], baseSec = 600, inc = 0): string {
  let wClock = baseSec;
  let bClock = baseSec;
  const moves: string[] = [];
  const count = Math.min(whiteDeltas.length, blackDeltas.length);

  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    if (i > 0) wClock = Math.max(25, wClock - whiteDeltas[i] + inc);
    if (i > 0) bClock = Math.max(25, bClock - blackDeltas[i] + inc);
    const fmt = (sec: number) => {
      const m = Math.floor(sec / 60);
      const s = (sec % 60).toFixed(1).padStart(4, '0');
      return `0:${String(m).padStart(2, '0')}:${s}`;
    };
    moves.push(`${i + 1}. Nf3 {[%clk ${fmt(wClock)}]} ${i + 1}... Nf6 {[%clk ${fmt(bClock)}]}`);
  }

  return `[Event "Live Chess"]\n[TimeControl "${baseSec}${inc ? `+${inc}` : ''}"]\n\n${moves.join(' ')}`;
}

describe('PGN [%clk] Forensic Move-Pacing Analyzer', () => {
  it('extracts accurate per-move durations with increment handling', () => {
    const deltas = Array.from({ length: 18 }, (_, i) => (i % 2 === 0 ? 3.5 : 8.0));
    const pgn = buildMockPgn(deltas, deltas, 180, 2);
    const res = extractPlayerMoveTimes(pgn, true);
    expect(res).not.toBeNull();
    expect(res!.incrementSec).toBe(2);
    expect(res!.moveDurationsSec.length).toBe(17);
    expect(res!.moveDurationsSec[1]).toBeCloseTo(3.5, 1);
    expect(res!.moveDurationsSec[2]).toBeCloseTo(8.0, 1);
  });

  it('flags metronomic flatline engine pacing (low CV, zero fast recaptures)', () => {
    // Classic 4.5s - 5.5s Stockfish relay flatline across 28 moves
    const roboticDeltas = [
      2.1, 3.0, 4.1, 4.8, 5.1, 4.9, 5.2, 4.7, 5.0, 5.3, 4.8, 4.6, 5.1, 5.0, 4.9, 5.4, 4.7, 5.0,
      4.8, 5.1, 4.9, 5.2, 4.6, 5.0, 4.8, 5.1, 4.9, 5.0,
    ];
    const humanDeltas = [
      0.8, 1.2, 4.5, 0.5, 14.2, 1.1, 0.6, 22.4, 3.1, 0.9, 18.5, 1.4, 0.7, 9.2, 28.0, 1.0, 0.4,
      11.5, 2.0, 0.9, 16.2, 1.3, 0.8, 7.4, 1.1, 19.0, 0.5, 6.2,
    ];
    const pgn = buildMockPgn(roboticDeltas, humanDeltas, 600, 0);

    const cheaterMetrics = analyzeSingleGamePacing(pgn, true, 'rapid', true);
    const humanMetrics = analyzeSingleGamePacing(pgn, false, 'rapid', false);

    expect(cheaterMetrics).not.toBeNull();
    expect(cheaterMetrics!.isFlatline).toBe(true);
    expect(cheaterMetrics!.cv).toBeLessThan(0.2);

    expect(humanMetrics).not.toBeNull();
    expect(humanMetrics!.isFlatline).toBe(false);
    expect(humanMetrics!.cv).toBeGreaterThan(0.8);
  });

  it('detects mid-game engine launch toggle signature', () => {
    // Normal opening -> 42s pause at move 10 to open engine -> 12 robotic 4.5s moves -> win
    const toggleDeltas = [
      1.2, 2.5, 0.9, 6.4, 1.8, 11.2, 2.0, 4.5, 1.5, 42.0, 4.4, 4.8, 4.6, 5.0, 4.3, 4.9, 4.7, 4.5,
      4.8, 4.6, 5.1, 4.4, 4.7,
    ];
    const oppDeltas = Array.from({ length: toggleDeltas.length }, (_, i) =>
      i % 3 === 0 ? 1.4 : 9.5
    );
    const pgn = buildMockPgn(toggleDeltas, oppDeltas, 600, 0);

    const m = analyzeSingleGamePacing(pgn, true, 'rapid', true);
    expect(m).not.toBeNull();
    expect(m!.hasMidgameToggle).toBe(true);
  });

  it('produces high aggregate pacingScore for repeated flatline games vs near-zero for organic play', () => {
    const roboticDeltas = Array.from({ length: 26 }, (_, i) => 4.6 + (i % 3) * 0.4);
    const humanDeltas = [
      0.6, 1.1, 8.2, 0.9, 21.0, 1.4, 0.5, 14.3, 3.2, 0.8, 29.5, 1.2, 0.7, 6.8, 17.4, 1.0, 0.9,
      12.1, 1.5, 24.0, 0.8, 5.5, 1.3, 15.2, 0.7, 9.0,
    ];
    const pgn = buildMockPgn(roboticDeltas, humanDeltas, 600, 0);

    const games: ChessComGame[] = Array.from({ length: 10 }, (_, idx) => ({
      pgn,
      time_class: 'rapid',
      rated: true,
      end_time: 1760000000 + idx * 900,
      white: { username: 'BotSuspect', rating: 2050, result: 'win' },
      black: { username: 'HonestJoe', rating: 2040, result: 'checkmated' },
    }));

    const suspectSummary = computeAggregatePacing(games, 'BotSuspect');
    const honestSummary = computeAggregatePacing(games, 'HonestJoe');

    expect(suspectSummary.pacingScore).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(75);
    expect(honestSummary.pacingScore).toBeLessThanOrEqual(10);
  });
});
```
